# Renown Organisations A1 — Core (model, store, membership, OIDC) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Ship the foundation of Renown organisations in renown-package: the `powerhouse/renown-organization` document model, the `renown-orgs` subgraph with its relational store, org CRUD and handles in the shared user/org namespace, the full membership lifecycle (invites, invite links, leave/remove, roles, app scopes, hidden, limits), teams, the directory, `memberOf` on `renownUser`, the public `orgMembership` query Vetra calls, the OIDC `orgs` scope with org-restricted clients, the audit log and rate limits.

**Architecture:** A new subgraph `subgraphs/renown-orgs` (same shape as renown-stats: `BaseSubgraph`, own Kysely namespace `renown-orgs`, resolvers that authorise their callers themselves) writes every state change twice under one keyed lock per org: first to the org document through the in-process reactor client (the signed record), then to the store (the source of truth for every read). Callers prove an address with the host bearer or a `personal_sign` over `orgMessage(...)` from a new framework-free `sdk/orgs.ts`. Other subgraphs (renown-auth, renown-read-model, renown-oidc) read the `renown-orgs` namespace through small reader functions in `subgraphs/renown-orgs/store/lookups.ts`, never through the subgraph object.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript (nodenext, strict), Powerhouse 6.2.3 (`ph-cli generate`, `BaseSubgraph`, reactor client), Kysely 0.28 on Postgres (PGlite in tests), graphql-tag SDL, viem, vitest 4.1.1, oxlint (type-aware).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-renown-organisations-design.md` — sections 3 (concepts), 4.1–4.3 (A backend: model, subgraph, changes to existing subgraphs), 7 (think-beyond items), 9 (testing and rollout). This plan is A1 of three; A2 (`2026-10-12-orgs-a2-security-apps.md`) and A3 (`2026-10-12-orgs-a3-integrations.md`) build on it. Sub-projects B (renown.id) and C (Vetra) consume the API contract in the appendix at the end of this file.

## Rulings (binding for A1, A2 and A3)

1. **Model identity.** `id: "powerhouse/renown-organization"`, `name: "RenownOrganization"` (the codegen identifier; every model in this repo uses the PascalCase form; "Renown Organization" is the human label), `extension: "rorg"`. Created from `specs/renown-organization.json` with `pnpm generate document-model --document ./specs/renown-organization.json`, exactly like `renown/oidc-client`. Model enums are prefixed `RenownOrg*` so they never collide with the subgraph's own `OrgRole` enum in the supergraph.
2. **One extra operation, `INIT_ORGANIZATION {handle, name, createdBy}`** in the `profile` module. The spec lists `createdBy` in the state but no operation sets it, and the first write needs handle and name atomically. `AlreadyInitializedError` once `createdBy` is set.
3. **Reducer invariants** (pure, no authorisation): at most one open (INVITED or ACTIVE) record per address; transitions INVITED→ACTIVE|DECLINED|REVOKED and ACTIVE→LEFT|REMOVED only; ≤ 500 ACTIVE members; ≤ 50 teams; last-owner (a status or role change may not take a non-archived org from 1 active owner to 0; an org with 0 owners — mid-creation — is not blocked). `EXPIRED` is never stored: an INVITED record older than 14 days reads `EXPIRED`; re-inviting the same address first REVOKEs the expired record.
4. **Store.** Kysely only. `store/memory.ts` opens the same `KyselyOrgStore` on an in-process PGlite (one implementation, nothing to drift). The contract suite runs it twice: on that memory store and on a `withSchema("renown-orgs")` PGlite like the host's namespace, which catches raw SQL that would land in `public` (the dd9a802 lesson). JSON columns are `jsonb`, written with `JSON.stringify` and read through `jsonValue` (the renown-user `links` convention). `migrate()` grows per phase: A1 creates `organizations, org_members, org_teams, org_team_members, org_invite_links, org_apps, org_domains, org_audit, reserved_handles`; `org_apps` and `org_domains` are written from A2 on but read by A1 (capabilities, verified domains).
5. **Signed messages.** `orgMessage(field, args.orgId ?? "-", args, timestamp)` = `Renown organization <field> <orgId or -> <sha256 hex of canonicalJson(args without auth and orgId)> at <timestamp>`. `field` is the GraphQL field name, for queries too. Canonical JSON: keys sorted recursively (UTF-16 order, `Array.prototype.sort`), no whitespace, `undefined` dropped, `null` kept. **No renown-orgs argument has a GraphQL default value** (a default would be hashed by the server but not by a client that omitted it). A present `auth` is always verified and never falls back to the bearer; without `auth` the bearer (`ctx.user.address`) is the caller. Smart wallets are checked on chain 1. `canonicalJson`/`orgMessage` live in `sdk/orgs.ts` and the subgraph imports them, so client and server share one implementation.
6. **Reuse in place, do not move.** `subgraphs/renown-auth/core/{signed-message,smart-wallet,rate-limit,handle}.ts`, `subgraphs/renown-stats/core/keyed-lock.ts`, `subgraphs/renown-stats/lookups.ts` (`contributorProfiles`) and `subgraphs/renown-read-model/search.ts` (`likePrefix`) are imported from where they are; moving them would break every existing `vi.mock("../core/smart-wallet.js")`.
7. **Roles are uppercase everywhere** (GraphQL, OIDC `orgs` claim, webhook payloads, SDK constants). The SDK's `roleAtLeast` accepts either case.
8. **One handle namespace.** An org handle must pass renown-auth's `handleProblem`, must not be an org-only reserved word (`org, orgs, new, invite, invites, notifications, me, verify, people, search`), must not be in `reserved_handles` (until its `until`), and must not be held by another org or any user. `renown_upsertProfile` and `renownHandleAvailability` additionally refuse org handles and reserved handles. A missing `renown-orgs` namespace (Postgres 42P01/3F000) reads as "no org handles"; any other read error is `SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE` on writes. A user and an org claiming the same handle in the same millisecond is an accepted race; each namespace keeps its own unique index.
9. **Activity.** Every successful mutation calls `rt.record(activity)` exactly once with the audit details, the webhook event type and the notification intents. A1 writes only the `org_audit` row; A3 registers the notification and webhook sinks. A failing audit or sink write is logged and never fails the mutation (the change is already applied). Details never contain tokens, secrets or signatures.
10. **Visibility.** `organization(...)` returns a taken-down org with id, did, handle, name, `takenDown`, `archived` only (bio, website, avatar, links, apps, teams empty). `organizationSearch` lists only `listed` orgs that are neither archived nor taken down; an absent or blank `query` lists all of them (the `/orgs` directory). `organizationsByMember(address)` shows other callers ACTIVE memberships with `!hidden` in `membersPublic` orgs; the address itself (bearer or `auth`) sees all its ACTIVE memberships, hidden included. `orgMembership(orgId, address)` answers any ACTIVE membership (hidden or not, public or not — Vetra needs it) with role, status, appScope, effectiveScope and team slugs, never `hidden`; null otherwise and for archived or taken-down orgs.
11. **Who changes whom.** Admins invite, re-role and remove up to PUBLISHER; granting, changing or removing ADMIN needs an OWNER; OWNER is never granted or removed by these mutations (A2 proposals do it). Admins revoke invites with role ≤ PUBLISHER, owners any. Owners and admins always have scope ALL; `setOrgMemberAppScope` on them is `BAD_USER_INPUT`. Demoting keeps the stored scope.
12. **Limits.** 20 orgs per creator (lifetime, archived included); 50 open memberships per wallet (ACTIVE or unexpired INVITED); 200 unexpired pending invites per org; 500 ACTIVE members; 50 teams; 200 app DIDs per scope or team; 60 writes per address per minute across all renown-orgs mutations; 50 invites per org per hour (direct invites and invite-link creations). Limits answer `LIMIT_REACHED`, rate limits `RATE_LIMITED`.
13. **Org avatar** is uploaded through the existing `POST <package>/media/uploads` (purpose `avatar`) and checked with `avatarProblem` like profile avatars; `/media/<orgId>/avatar` serves it because the avatar lookup falls back to `organizations.avatar_ref`.
14. **OIDC.** The `orgs` claim lists ACTIVE memberships (hidden included) of orgs that are neither archived nor taken down: `{id, did, handle, name, role, teams, verifiedDomains}`. `allowedOrgs` is a `text` JSON column `allowed_orgs` (the renown-oidc convention) added with the probe-then-alter pattern; it is omitted from `OidcClient` objects when empty so every existing fixture stays equal; it is not mirrored to the `renown/oidc-client` document (org-owned clients are audited in `org_audit`, A3).
15. **SDL grows per phase**, assembled in `subgraphs/renown-orgs/schema.ts` from `schema-core.ts` (A1), `schema-security.ts` (A2), `schema-integrations.ts` (A3) using `extend type Query/Mutation`. The appendix "API contract" fixes every type and field name of all three phases now; `tests/api-contract.test.ts` pins the shipped phases' signatures, and A2/A3 only flip their phase on.
16. **Mutation return types.** Writes return the changed object (`Organization`, `OrgMember`, `OrgInvite`, `OrgTeam`, …) so a UI never needs a second round trip; deletions and leave return `Boolean!`.

## Global Constraints

- Conventional commits (`feat(orgs): …`, `fix(…)`, `test(…)`, `docs: …`); **no `Co-Authored-By` or "Generated with" lines** in commits or PR descriptions.
- Never edit `gen/` folders or `subgraphs/index.ts`/`processors/switchboard.ts` by hand; model changes go through the JSON + `pnpm generate document-model --document <json>`; restore generator side effects outside `gen/`, `schema.graphql` and the model JSON with `git checkout --`.
- Reducers are pure and synchronous; ids and timestamps come from the action input; reducer coverage ≥ 95 % on lines, branches, functions and statements (`pnpm vitest run --coverage`); reducer errors are tested through `operations.global[i].error`, never `.toThrow()`.
- After every task: `pnpm tsc`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test` are green. Keep command output short: `2>&1 | tail -20`, or `grep -E "Test Files|Tests|FAIL"`.
- Relative imports carry `.js`; no `@/` alias; use the tsconfig path aliases (`document-models/<name>`).
- Error codes are exactly: `BAD_USER_INPUT` (with `extensions.field`), `FORBIDDEN`, `NOT_FOUND`, `HANDLE_TAKEN`, `HANDLE_RESERVED`, `LAST_OWNER`, `LIMIT_REACHED`, `DOMAIN_TAKEN`, `DOMAIN_PROOF_MISSING`, `ORG_HAS_APPS`, `APP_FROZEN`, `PROPOSAL_NOT_EXECUTABLE`, `RATE_LIMITED`, `SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE`.
- Handle regex `^[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{1,28}[a-z0-9])$`; org DID `did:web:<RENOWN_ORG_DID_HOST>:org:<orgId>` with host default `www.renown.id`.
- New env vars default to safe values: `RENOWN_ORG_DID_HOST` (default `www.renown.id`), `RENOWN_PLATFORM_ADMINS` (default `ADMINS`), `RENOWN_ORG_TIMELOCK_SECONDS` (172800), `RENOWN_ORG_RECOVERY_SECONDS` (604800).
- Staging first, then main/prod (`staging` branch → renown-package staging channel → smoke → main).

## Review Focus

1. **A signed write whose client omitted an optional argument the server then sees as `null`** (or the reverse). Expected: both sides hash the same object, because GraphQL leaves omitted arguments absent and the client signs the exact variables it sends; a mismatch is `FORBIDDEN`, never a write. Pinned in Task 3 ("drops undefined but keeps null") and Task 5 ("accepts a signed createOrganization and rejects one signed over other arguments").
2. **Two owners demoting or leaving at once in a two-owner org.** Expected: exactly one succeeds, the other gets `LAST_OWNER`; the org never has 0 owners. Pinned in Task 7 ("serialises concurrent leaves under the org lock").
3. **Re-inviting an address whose invite expired 15 days ago.** Expected: the old record reads EXPIRED, the new invite succeeds (the old one is REVOKED in document and store), and expired invites do not count toward the 200 pending limit. Pinned in Task 7 ("re-invites over an expired invite").
4. **An invite link raced by two acceptors when one use is left.** Expected: exactly one membership, the other caller gets `BAD_USER_INPUT` (field `token`). Pinned in Task 4 (store contract "uses an invite link atomically") and Task 7.
5. **A user claiming a handle an org already holds (and vice versa), including case variants (`Acme` vs `acme`).** Expected: `HANDLE_TAKEN` both ways, and `renownHandleAvailability`/`orgHandleAvailability` say TAKEN. Pinned in Task 6.

| `subgraphs/renown-orgs/{schema-core,schema}.ts` | SDL |
| `subgraphs/renown-orgs/runtime.ts` | per-subgraph runtime: store access, lock, limits, dispatch, activity |
| `subgraphs/renown-orgs/output.ts` | store records → GraphQL objects |
| `subgraphs/renown-orgs/areas/{organizations,members,teams}.ts` | resolvers per area |
| `subgraphs/renown-orgs/{resolvers,index}.ts` | resolver assembly, the subgraph class |
| `subgraphs/renown-orgs/tests/**` | harness, contract, resolver and API-contract tests |

| `subgraphs/renown-oidc/**` | `orgs` scope and claim, `allowedOrgs` |
| `powerhouse.manifest.json`, `subgraphs/index.ts` (generated) | registration |

### Task 1: `powerhouse/renown-organization` model — spec, codegen, profile/apps/domains/security reducers

**Files:**
- Create: `specs/renown-organization.json` (by the script in Step 1)
- Generated: `document-models/renown-organization/**`, `document-models/{document-models,index,upgrade-manifests}.ts`, `powerhouse.manifest.json` (new entry only)
- Replace: `document-models/renown-organization/v1/src/utils.ts`, `v1/src/reducers/{profile,apps,domains,security}.ts`
- Replace (generated mock tests): `document-models/renown-organization/v1/tests/{profile,apps,domains,security}.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces (all from `document-models/renown-organization/v1`, re-exported by `document-models/renown-organization`):
  - `renownOrganizationDocumentType = "powerhouse/renown-organization"`, `reducer`, `utils`, `actions`, types `RenownOrganizationState`, `RenownOrganizationDocument`, `RenownOrgMember`, `RenownOrgTeam`, `RenownOrgApp`, `RenownOrgDomain`, `RenownOrgLink`, `RenownOrgRole = "OWNER" | "ADMIN" | "PUBLISHER" | "MEMBER"`, `RenownOrgMemberStatus = "INVITED" | "ACTIVE" | "DECLINED" | "LEFT" | "REMOVED" | "REVOKED"`, `RenownOrgDomainStatus = "PENDING" | "VERIFIED" | "LAPSED"`.
  - Action creators (named exports and `actions.*`): `initOrganization({handle, name, createdBy})`, `setOrgProfile({name?, bio?, website?, avatar?, links?})`, `setOrgHandle({handle})`, `setOrgListed({listed})`, `setMembersPublic({membersPublic})`, `archiveOrg({archivedAt})`, `setTakenDown({takenDown})`, `inviteMember({id, address, role, invitedAt})`, `setMemberStatus({id, status, at})`, `setMemberRole({id, role, at})`, `setMemberAppScope({id, all, appDids, at})`, `setMemberHidden({id, hidden, at})`, `addTeam({id, slug, name, appDids})`, `updateTeam({id, slug?, name?, appDids?})`, `removeTeam({id})`, `setTeamMembers({id, memberIds})`, `addOrgApp({appDid, addedAt})`, `removeOrgApp({appDid})`, `setAppFrozen({appDid, frozen})`, `addDomain({id, domain})`, `setDomainStatus({id, status, verifiedAt?})`, `removeDomain({id})`, `suspendCredentials({credentialIds})`, `unsuspendCredential({credentialId})`.
  - Utils (`v1/src/utils.ts`, also exported from the barrel): `ORG_HANDLE_RE`, `MAX_ORG_NAME_LENGTH = 64`, `MAX_ORG_BIO_LENGTH = 280`, `MAX_ORG_LINKS = 8`, `MAX_ACTIVE_MEMBERS = 500`, `MAX_TEAMS = 50`, `MAX_SCOPE_APPS = 200`, `isOrgHandle`, `isOrgName`, `isOrgWebsite`, `isOrgAvatarRef`, `isOrgLinkLabel`, `isOrgLinkUrl`, `isOrgAppDid`, `isOrgDomain`, `isTeamSlug`, `isTeamName`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the model spec**

From the repo root:

```bash
python3 - <<'PY'
import json, re

def kebab(name):
    return re.sub(r"(?<!^)(?=[A-Z])", "-", name).lower()

def err(name, description):
    code = re.sub(r"(?<!^)(?=[A-Z])", "_", name[: -len("Error")]).upper()
    return {"id": kebab(name), "name": name, "code": code, "description": description, "template": ""}

def op(name, description, schema, errors=()):
    return {"id": name.lower().replace("_", "-"), "name": name, "description": description, "schema": schema,
            "template": "", "reducer": "", "errors": list(errors), "examples": [], "scope": "global"}

def module(name, operations):
    return {"id": f"renown-org-{name}", "name": name, "description": "", "operations": operations}

state = """type RenownOrganizationState {
  "Lowercase; unique across users and organizations (enforced by the write path)"
  handle: String
  "1-64 characters"
  name: String
  "At most 280 characters"
  bio: String
  "An http(s) URL"
  website: String
  "attachment://v1:<sha256>"
  avatar: String
  "At most 8, in display order"
  links: [RenownOrgLink!]!
  "Opted in to the organization directory"
  listed: Boolean!
  "Active, non-hidden members are shown publicly"
  membersPublic: Boolean!
  archived: Boolean!
  takenDown: Boolean!
  "Lowercase address of the creator; set once by INIT_ORGANIZATION"
  createdBy: EthereumAddress
  "Every membership record ever created (rows are never removed)"
  members: [RenownOrgMember!]!
  "At most 50 flat teams"
  teams: [RenownOrgTeam!]!
  apps: [RenownOrgApp!]!
  domains: [RenownOrgDomain!]!
  "Delegation credential ids suspended by the organization"
  suspendedCredentialIds: [String!]!
}

type RenownOrgLink {
  id: OID!
  label: String!
  url: String!
}

enum RenownOrgRole {
  OWNER
  ADMIN
  PUBLISHER
  MEMBER
}

enum RenownOrgMemberStatus {
  INVITED
  ACTIVE
  DECLINED
  LEFT
  REMOVED
  REVOKED
}

type RenownOrgAppScope {
  all: Boolean!
  appDids: [String!]!
}

type RenownOrgMember {
  id: OID!
  "Lowercase"
  address: EthereumAddress!
  role: RenownOrgRole!
  status: RenownOrgMemberStatus!
  appScope: RenownOrgAppScope!
  hidden: Boolean!
  joinedAt: DateTime
  updatedAt: DateTime!
}

type RenownOrgTeam {
  id: OID!
  slug: String!
  name: String!
  appDids: [String!]!
  memberIds: [OID!]!
}

type RenownOrgApp {
  appDid: String!
  addedAt: DateTime!
  frozen: Boolean!
}

enum RenownOrgDomainStatus {
  PENDING
  VERIFIED
  LAPSED
}

type RenownOrgDomain {
  id: OID!
  domain: String!
  status: RenownOrgDomainStatus!
  verifiedAt: DateTime
}"""

initial = {"handle": None, "name": None, "bio": None, "website": None, "avatar": None, "links": [],
           "listed": False, "membersPublic": False, "archived": False, "takenDown": False, "createdBy": None,
           "members": [], "teams": [], "apps": [], "domains": [], "suspendedCredentialIds": []}

profile = module("profile", [
    op("INIT_ORGANIZATION", "Sets handle, name and creator once, when the organization is created",
       "input InitOrganizationInput {\n  handle: String!\n  name: String!\n  createdBy: EthereumAddress!\n}",
       [err("AlreadyInitializedError", "INIT_ORGANIZATION already ran"),
        err("InvalidHandleError", "The handle does not match ^[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{1,28}[a-z0-9])$"),
        err("NameLengthError", "The name is not 1-64 characters without surrounding whitespace")]),
    op("SET_ORG_PROFILE", "Patches the profile: null or absent keeps a field, an empty string clears bio/website/avatar, links replaces the whole list",
       "input SetOrgProfileInput {\n  name: String\n  bio: String\n  website: String\n  avatar: String\n  links: [SetOrgProfileLinkInput!]\n}\n\ninput SetOrgProfileLinkInput {\n  id: OID!\n  label: String!\n  url: String!\n}",
       [err("BioTooLongError", "The bio is longer than 280 characters"),
        err("InvalidWebsiteError", "The website is not an http(s) URL of at most 2048 characters"),
        err("InvalidAvatarError", "The avatar is not an attachment://v1:<sha256> reference"),
        err("TooManyLinksError", "More than 8 links"),
        err("InvalidLinkError", "A link has a duplicate id, a label outside 1-40 characters or a non-http(s) URL")]),
    op("SET_ORG_HANDLE", "Changes the handle (uniqueness is checked by the write path)", "input SetOrgHandleInput {\n  handle: String!\n}"),
    op("SET_ORG_LISTED", "Opts in to or out of the organization directory", "input SetOrgListedInput {\n  listed: Boolean!\n}"),
    op("SET_MEMBERS_PUBLIC", "Shows or hides the member list publicly", "input SetMembersPublicInput {\n  membersPublic: Boolean!\n}"),
    op("ARCHIVE_ORG", "Archives the organization: unlisted, open invites revoked, active members removed", "input ArchiveOrgInput {\n  archivedAt: DateTime!\n}"),
    op("SET_TAKEN_DOWN", "Platform moderation: takes the organization down or restores it", "input SetTakenDownInput {\n  takenDown: Boolean!\n}"),
])

members = module("members", [
    op("INVITE_MEMBER", "Creates an INVITED membership record",
       "input InviteMemberInput {\n  id: OID!\n  address: EthereumAddress!\n  role: RenownOrgRole!\n  invitedAt: DateTime!\n}",
       [err("DuplicateMemberError", "The id is used, or the address already has an INVITED or ACTIVE record")]),
    op("SET_MEMBER_STATUS", "Moves a membership record through its lifecycle",
       "input SetMemberStatusInput {\n  id: OID!\n  status: RenownOrgMemberStatus!\n  at: DateTime!\n}",
       [err("MemberNotFoundError", "No membership record with this id"),
        err("InvalidStatusTransitionError", "Only INVITED to ACTIVE/DECLINED/REVOKED and ACTIVE to LEFT/REMOVED are allowed"),
        err("LastOwnerError", "The change would leave the organization without an active owner"),
        err("TooManyMembersError", "The organization already has 500 active members")]),
    op("SET_MEMBER_ROLE", "Changes the role of an INVITED or ACTIVE member",
       "input SetMemberRoleInput {\n  id: OID!\n  role: RenownOrgRole!\n  at: DateTime!\n}",
       [err("MemberNotActiveError", "The membership record is not INVITED or ACTIVE (not ACTIVE for team membership)")]),
    op("SET_MEMBER_APP_SCOPE", "Sets the apps a publisher or member acts for",
       "input SetMemberAppScopeInput {\n  id: OID!\n  all: Boolean!\n  appDids: [String!]!\n  at: DateTime!\n}",
       [err("InvalidAppDidError", "An app DID is not a did:key, or there are more than 200")]),
    op("SET_MEMBER_HIDDEN", "The member hides or shows their membership publicly",
       "input SetMemberHiddenInput {\n  id: OID!\n  hidden: Boolean!\n  at: DateTime!\n}"),
    op("ADD_TEAM", "Adds a flat team",
       "input AddTeamInput {\n  id: OID!\n  slug: String!\n  name: String!\n  appDids: [String!]!\n}",
       [err("DuplicateTeamIdError", "The team id is used"),
        err("TooManyTeamsError", "The organization already has 50 teams"),
        err("InvalidTeamError", "The slug or name is malformed"),
        err("DuplicateTeamSlugError", "Another team has this slug")]),
    op("UPDATE_TEAM", "Patches a team: null or absent keeps a field",
       "input UpdateTeamInput {\n  id: OID!\n  slug: String\n  name: String\n  appDids: [String!]\n}",
       [err("TeamNotFoundError", "No team with this id")]),
    op("REMOVE_TEAM", "Removes a team", "input RemoveTeamInput {\n  id: OID!\n}"),
    op("SET_TEAM_MEMBERS", "Replaces a team's members (ACTIVE membership ids)",
       "input SetTeamMembersInput {\n  id: OID!\n  memberIds: [OID!]!\n}"),
])

apps = module("apps", [
    op("ADD_ORG_APP", "Records an app the organization owns",
       "input AddOrgAppInput {\n  appDid: String!\n  addedAt: DateTime!\n}",
       [err("InvalidAppDidError", "The app DID is not a did:key"),
        err("DuplicateAppError", "The app already belongs to the organization")]),
    op("REMOVE_ORG_APP", "Removes an app (transferred out)", "input RemoveOrgAppInput {\n  appDid: String!\n}",
       [err("AppNotFoundError", "The app does not belong to the organization")]),
    op("SET_APP_FROZEN", "Freezes or unfreezes an org app", "input SetAppFrozenInput {\n  appDid: String!\n  frozen: Boolean!\n}"),
])

domains = module("domains", [
    op("ADD_DOMAIN", "Adds a PENDING domain", "input AddDomainInput {\n  id: OID!\n  domain: String!\n}",
       [err("InvalidDomainError", "Not a lowercase DNS name with at least two labels"),
        err("DuplicateDomainError", "The id or the domain is already present")]),
    op("SET_DOMAIN_STATUS", "Records a verification result",
       "input SetDomainStatusInput {\n  id: OID!\n  status: RenownOrgDomainStatus!\n  verifiedAt: DateTime\n}",
       [err("DomainNotFoundError", "No domain with this id")]),
    op("REMOVE_DOMAIN", "Removes a domain", "input RemoveDomainInput {\n  id: OID!\n}"),
])

security = module("security", [
    op("SUSPEND_CREDENTIALS", "Suspends delegation credentials for org apps", "input SuspendCredentialsInput {\n  credentialIds: [String!]!\n}"),
    op("UNSUSPEND_CREDENTIAL", "Lifts one suspension", "input UnsuspendCredentialInput {\n  credentialId: String!\n}",
       [err("CredentialNotSuspendedError", "The credential is not suspended")]),
])

    "specifications": [{
        "state": {"global": {"schema": state, "initialValue": json.dumps(initial, indent=2), "examples": []},
                  "local": {"schema": "", "initialValue": "", "examples": []}},
        "modules": [profile, members, apps, domains, security],
        "version": 1,
        "changeLog": [],
    }],
}
open("specs/renown-organization.json", "w").write(json.dumps(spec, indent=2) + "\n")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Replace the utils**

Write `document-models/renown-organization/v1/src/utils.ts`:

```ts
/** Organisation validators and limits, shared by the reducers and the renown-orgs subgraph. */

/** 3-30 characters, lowercase letters, digits and inner hyphens: the same rule as user handles. */
export const ORG_HANDLE_RE = /^[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{1,28}[a-z0-9])$/;
export const MAX_ORG_NAME_LENGTH = 64;
export const MAX_ORG_BIO_LENGTH = 280;
export const MAX_ORG_LINKS = 8;
export const MAX_ORG_LINK_LABEL_LENGTH = 40;
export const MAX_ORG_URL_LENGTH = 2048;
export const MAX_ACTIVE_MEMBERS = 500;
export const MAX_TEAMS = 50;
export const MAX_SCOPE_APPS = 200;
export const MAX_TEAM_NAME_LENGTH = 64;

const TEAM_SLUG_RE = /^[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,38}[a-z0-9])?$/;
const ORG_AVATAR_RE = /^attachment:\/\/v1:[0-9a-f]{64}$/;
const ORG_APP_DID_RE = /^did:key:z[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{32,128}$/;
// Lowercase labels of 1-63 characters, at least two labels, a top-level label
// starting with a letter (so an IP address never passes), 4-253 characters.
const ORG_DOMAIN_RE =
  /^(?=.{4,253}$)(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])$/;

function trimmedWithin(value: string, max: number): boolean {
  return value.length > 0 && value.length <= max && value === value.trim();
}

function isHttpUrl(value: string): boolean {
  if (value.length > MAX_ORG_URL_LENGTH) return false;
  try {
    const { protocol } = new URL(value);
    return protocol === "https:" || protocol === "http:";
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function isOrgHandle(value: string): boolean {
  return ORG_HANDLE_RE.test(value);
}

export function isOrgName(value: string): boolean {
  return trimmedWithin(value, MAX_ORG_NAME_LENGTH);
}

export function isOrgWebsite(value: string): boolean {
  return isHttpUrl(value);
}

export function isOrgAvatarRef(value: string): boolean {
  return ORG_AVATAR_RE.test(value);
}

export function isOrgLinkLabel(value: string): boolean {
  return trimmedWithin(value, MAX_ORG_LINK_LABEL_LENGTH);
}

export function isOrgLinkUrl(value: string): boolean {
  return isHttpUrl(value);
}

export function isOrgAppDid(value: string): boolean {
  return ORG_APP_DID_RE.test(value);
}

export function isOrgDomain(value: string): boolean {
  return ORG_DOMAIN_RE.test(value);
}

export function isTeamSlug(value: string): boolean {
  return TEAM_SLUG_RE.test(value);
}

export function isTeamName(value: string): boolean {
  return trimmedWithin(value, MAX_TEAM_NAME_LENGTH);
}
```

```ts
import { addOrgApp, reducer, removeOrgApp, setAppFrozen, utils } from "document-models/renown-organization/v1";

const APP = "did:key:z6MkhaXgBZDvotDkL5257faiztiGiC2QtKLGpbnnEGta2doK";

describe("RenownOrganization apps module", () => {

      `${OTHER} is not an app of the organization`,
      `${OTHER} is not an app of the organization`,
    ]);
    expect(d.state.global.apps).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});
```

```ts
});
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the four reducers**

Write `document-models/renown-organization/v1/src/reducers/profile.ts`:

```ts
import type { RenownOrganizationProfileOperations } from "document-models/renown-organization/v1";
import {
  AlreadyInitializedError,
  BioTooLongError,
  InvalidAvatarError,
  InvalidHandleError,
  InvalidLinkError,
  InvalidWebsiteError,
  NameLengthError,
  TooManyLinksError,
} from "../../gen/profile/error.js";
import {
  isOrgAvatarRef,
  isOrgHandle,
  isOrgLinkLabel,
  isOrgLinkUrl,
  isOrgName,
  isOrgWebsite,
  MAX_ORG_BIO_LENGTH,
  MAX_ORG_LINKS,
} from "../utils.js";

const NAME_RULE = "The name must be 1-64 characters without surrounding whitespace";

/** undefined (absent) and null keep a field; "" (after trimming) clears it; otherwise the trimmed value. */
function patched(value: string | null | undefined): string | null | undefined {
  if (value === undefined || value === null) return undefined;
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  return trimmed === "" ? null : trimmed;
}

export const renownOrganizationProfileOperations: RenownOrganizationProfileOperations = {
  initOrganizationOperation(state, action) {
    const { handle, name, createdBy } = action.input;
    if (state.createdBy) throw new AlreadyInitializedError("The organization is already initialized");
    if (!isOrgHandle(handle)) throw new InvalidHandleError(`Invalid handle: ${handle}`);
    if (!isOrgName(name)) throw new NameLengthError(NAME_RULE);
    state.handle = handle;
    state.name = name;
    state.createdBy = createdBy.toLowerCase();
  },
  setOrgProfileOperation(state, action) {
    const { name, links } = action.input;
    const bio = patched(action.input.bio);
    const website = patched(action.input.website);
    const avatar = patched(action.input.avatar);
    if (name != null && !isOrgName(name)) throw new NameLengthError(NAME_RULE);
    if (bio && bio.length > MAX_ORG_BIO_LENGTH) {
      throw new BioTooLongError(`The bio exceeds ${MAX_ORG_BIO_LENGTH} characters`);
    }
    if (website && !isOrgWebsite(website)) throw new InvalidWebsiteError(`Invalid website: ${website}`);
    if (avatar && !isOrgAvatarRef(avatar)) throw new InvalidAvatarError(`Invalid avatar reference: ${avatar}`);
    if (links) {
      if (links.length > MAX_ORG_LINKS) {
        throw new TooManyLinksError(`An organization holds at most ${MAX_ORG_LINKS} links`);
      }
      const ids = new Set<string>();
      for (const link of links) {
        if (ids.has(link.id)) throw new InvalidLinkError(`Duplicate link id ${link.id}`);
        ids.add(link.id);
        if (!isOrgLinkLabel(link.label) || !isOrgLinkUrl(link.url)) {
          throw new InvalidLinkError(`Link ${link.id} needs a 1-40 character label and an http(s) URL`);
        }
      }
    }
    if (name != null) state.name = name;
    if (bio !== undefined) state.bio = bio;
    if (website !== undefined) state.website = website;
    if (avatar !== undefined) state.avatar = avatar;
    if (links) state.links = links.map(({ id, label, url }) => ({ id, label, url }));
  },
  setOrgHandleOperation(state, action) {
    const { handle } = action.input;
    if (!isOrgHandle(handle)) throw new InvalidHandleError(`Invalid handle: ${handle}`);
    state.handle = handle;
  },
  setOrgListedOperation(state, action) {
    state.listed = action.input.listed;
  },
  setMembersPublicOperation(state, action) {
    state.membersPublic = action.input.membersPublic;
  },
  archiveOrgOperation(state, action) {
    const at = action.input.archivedAt;
    state.archived = true;
    state.listed = false;
    for (const member of state.members) {
      if (member.status === "INVITED") {
        member.status = "REVOKED";
        member.updatedAt = at;
      } else if (member.status === "ACTIVE") {
        member.status = "REMOVED";
        member.updatedAt = at;
      }
    }
    for (const team of state.teams) team.memberIds = [];
  },
  setTakenDownOperation(state, action) {
    state.takenDown = action.input.takenDown;
  },
};
```

Write `document-models/renown-organization/v1/src/reducers/apps.ts`:

```ts
import type { RenownOrganizationAppsOperations } from "document-models/renown-organization/v1";
import { AppNotFoundError, DuplicateAppError, InvalidAppDidError } from "../../gen/apps/error.js";
import { isOrgAppDid } from "../utils.js";

export const renownOrganizationAppsOperations: RenownOrganizationAppsOperations = {
  addOrgAppOperation(state, action) {
    const { appDid, addedAt } = action.input;
    if (!isOrgAppDid(appDid)) throw new InvalidAppDidError(`Invalid app DID: ${appDid}`);
    if (state.apps.some((app) => app.appDid === appDid)) {
      throw new DuplicateAppError(`${appDid} already belongs to the organization`);
    }
    state.apps.push({ appDid, addedAt, frozen: false });
  },
  removeOrgAppOperation(state, action) {
    const { appDid } = action.input;
    const index = state.apps.findIndex((app) => app.appDid === appDid);
    if (index === -1) throw new AppNotFoundError(`${appDid} is not an app of the organization`);
    state.apps.splice(index, 1);
  },
  setAppFrozenOperation(state, action) {
    const { appDid, frozen } = action.input;
    const app = state.apps.find((a) => a.appDid === appDid);
    if (!app) throw new AppNotFoundError(`${appDid} is not an app of the organization`);
    app.frozen = frozen;
  },
};
```

Write `document-models/renown-organization/v1/src/reducers/domains.ts`:

```ts
import type { RenownOrganizationDomainsOperations } from "document-models/renown-organization/v1";
import { DomainNotFoundError, DuplicateDomainError, InvalidDomainError } from "../../gen/domains/error.js";
import { isOrgDomain } from "../utils.js";

export const renownOrganizationDomainsOperations: RenownOrganizationDomainsOperations = {
  addDomainOperation(state, action) {
    const { id, domain } = action.input;
    if (!isOrgDomain(domain)) throw new InvalidDomainError(`Invalid domain: ${domain}`);
    if (state.domains.some((d) => d.id === id || d.domain === domain)) {
      throw new DuplicateDomainError(`Domain id ${id} or ${domain} is already present`);
    }
    state.domains.push({ id, domain, status: "PENDING", verifiedAt: null });
  },
  setDomainStatusOperation(state, action) {
    const { id, status, verifiedAt } = action.input;
    const domain = state.domains.find((d) => d.id === id);
    if (!domain) throw new DomainNotFoundError(`No domain with id ${id}`);
    domain.status = status;
    if (verifiedAt) domain.verifiedAt = verifiedAt;
  },
  removeDomainOperation(state, action) {
    const index = state.domains.findIndex((d) => d.id === action.input.id);
    if (index === -1) throw new DomainNotFoundError(`No domain with id ${action.input.id}`);
    state.domains.splice(index, 1);
  },
};
```

Write `document-models/renown-organization/v1/src/reducers/security.ts`:

```ts
import type { RenownOrganizationSecurityOperations } from "document-models/renown-organization/v1";
import { CredentialNotSuspendedError } from "../../gen/security/error.js";

export const renownOrganizationSecurityOperations: RenownOrganizationSecurityOperations = {
  suspendCredentialsOperation(state, action) {
    for (const id of action.input.credentialIds) {
      if (!state.suspendedCredentialIds.includes(id)) state.suspendedCredentialIds.push(id);
    }
  },
  unsuspendCredentialOperation(state, action) {
    const { credentialId } = action.input;
    const index = state.suspendedCredentialIds.indexOf(credentialId);
    if (index === -1) throw new CredentialNotSuspendedError(`Credential ${credentialId} is not suspended`);
    state.suspendedCredentialIds.splice(index, 1);
  },
};
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  addTeam,
  inviteMember,
  reducer,
  removeTeam,
  setMemberAppScope,
  setMemberHidden,
  setMemberRole,
  setMemberStatus,
  setTeamMembers,
  updateTeam,
  utils,
  type RenownOrgMember,
} from "document-models/renown-organization/v1";

type Doc = ReturnType<typeof utils.createDocument>;
const AT = "2026-10-12T12:00:00.000Z";
const LATER = "2026-10-13T12:00:00.000Z";
const B58 = "123456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz";
const app = (n: number) => `did:key:z6Mk${"x".repeat(40)}${B58[n % 58]}${B58[Math.floor(n / 58) % 58]}`;
const address = (n: number) => `0x${n.toString(16).padStart(40, "0")}`;

function errors(d: Doc): (string | undefined)[] {
  return d.operations.global.map((op) => op.error);
}

/** n invites accepted: m1 OWNER, then MEMBERs. */
function withActive(n: number): Doc {
  let d = utils.createDocument();
  for (let i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
    d = reducer(d, inviteMember({ id: `m${i}`, address: address(i), role: i === 1 ? "OWNER" : "MEMBER", invitedAt: AT }));
    d = reducer(d, setMemberStatus({ id: `m${i}`, status: "ACTIVE", at: AT }));
  }
  return d;
}

function member(d: Doc, id: string): RenownOrgMember {
  return d.state.global.members.find((m) => m.id === id)!;
}

        id: "m1",

        address: address(2),
        role: "PUBLISHER",
        status: "INVITED",

    ]);

  it("refuses a used id or a second open record for an address, allows re-inviting after leaving", () => {

    d = reducer(d, inviteMember({ id: "m9", address: address(2), role: "MEMBER", invitedAt: AT }));
    d = reducer(d, setMemberStatus({ id: "m2", status: "LEFT", at: LATER }));

    expect(member(d, "m2").appScope).toEqual({ all: true, appDids: [app(1)] });
    d = reducer(d, setMemberStatus({ id: "m2", status: "LEFT", at: LATER }));
    d = reducer(d, setMemberRole({ id: "m2", role: "PUBLISHER", at: LATER }));
    d = reducer(d, setMemberAppScope({ id: "m2", all: true, appDids: [], at: LATER }));
    expect(errors(d).slice(-2)).toEqual(["Member m2 is LEFT", "Member m2 is LEFT"]);
  });

  it("sets app scopes: deduplicated, validated, always ALL for owners", () => {
    let d = withActive(2);
    d = reducer(d, setMemberAppScope({ id: "m2", all: false, appDids: [app(1), app(2), app(1)], at: AT }));
    expect(member(d, "m2").appScope).toEqual({ all: false, appDids: [app(1), app(2)] });
    d = reducer(d, setMemberAppScope({ id: "m1", all: false, appDids: [], at: AT }));

    expect(errors(d).slice(-2)).toEqual(["Invalid app DID: did:web:x", "At most 200 app DIDs"]);
    expect(member(d, "m2").appScope.appDids).toEqual([app(1), app(2)]);
    d = reducer(d, setMemberAppScope({ id: "m2", all: true, appDids: [], at: LATER }));

  it("lets a member hide their membership", () => {
    let d = withActive(1);
    d = reducer(d, setMemberHidden({ id: "m1", hidden: true, at: LATER }));
    expect(member(d, "m1")).toMatchObject({ hidden: true, updatedAt: LATER });
    d = reducer(d, setMemberHidden({ id: "m9", hidden: true, at: LATER }));
    expect(errors(d).at(-1)).toBe("No member with id m9");
  });
});

describe("RenownOrganization teams", () => {
  it("adds, updates and removes teams", () => {

    expect(d.state.global.teams).toEqual([
      { id: "t1", slug: "kernel", name: "Core team", appDids: [app(2)], memberIds: [] },
      { id: "t2", slug: "web", name: "Web", appDids: [], memberIds: [] },
    ]);
    d = reducer(d, removeTeam({ id: "t2" }));
    expect(d.state.global.teams.map((t) => t.id)).toEqual(["t1"]);
    expect(errors(d).every((e) => e === undefined)).toBe(true);
  });

    let d = reducer(withActive(1), addTeam({ id: "t1", slug: "core", name: "Core", appDids: [] }));

    d = reducer(d, addTeam({ id: "t1", slug: "other", name: "Other", appDids: [] }));
    d = reducer(d, addTeam({ id: "t3", slug: "Bad Slug", name: "Bad", appDids: [] }));
    d = reducer(d, addTeam({ id: "t3", slug: "bad", name: " ", appDids: [] }));
    d = reducer(d, addTeam({ id: "t3", slug: "core", name: "Again", appDids: [] }));
    d = reducer(d, addTeam({ id: "t3", slug: "apps", name: "Apps", appDids: ["nope"] }));
    d = reducer(d, updateTeam({ id: "t1", slug: "web" }));
    d = reducer(d, updateTeam({ id: "t1", name: "" }));
    d = reducer(d, updateTeam({ id: "t1", appDids: ["nope"] }));
    d = reducer(d, updateTeam({ id: "t9", name: "X" }));
    d = reducer(d, removeTeam({ id: "t9" }));
    expect(errors(d).slice(3)).toEqual([
      undefined,
      "Team id t1 is used",
      "Team slug or name is malformed",
      "Team slug or name is malformed",
      "Another team has the slug core",
      "Invalid app DID: nope",
      "Another team has the slug web",
      "Team slug or name is malformed",
      "Invalid app DID: nope",
      "No team with id t9",
      "No team with id t9",
    ]);
    expect(d.state.global.teams.map((t) => t.slug)).toEqual(["core", "web"]);
  });

  it("caps teams at 50", () => {
    const d = utils.createDocument();
    d.state.global.teams = Array.from({ length: 50 }, (_, i) => ({ id: `t${i}`, slug: `team-${i}`, name: `Team ${i}`, appDids: [], memberIds: [] }));
    const next = reducer(d, addTeam({ id: "t50", slug: "late", name: "Late", appDids: [] }));
    expect(errors(next)).toEqual(["An organization holds at most 50 teams"]);
  });

  it("sets team members (active only, deduplicated) and drops members who stop being active", () => {
    let d = withActive(3);
    d = reducer(d, inviteMember({ id: "m4", address: address(4), role: "MEMBER", invitedAt: AT }));
    d = reducer(d, addTeam({ id: "t1", slug: "core", name: "Core", appDids: [] }));
    d = reducer(d, setTeamMembers({ id: "t1", memberIds: ["m2", "m3", "m2"] }));
    expect(d.state.global.teams[0].memberIds).toEqual(["m2", "m3"]);
    d = reducer(d, setTeamMembers({ id: "t1", memberIds: ["m4"] }));
    d = reducer(d, setTeamMembers({ id: "t1", memberIds: ["m9"] }));
    d = reducer(d, setTeamMembers({ id: "t9", memberIds: [] }));
    expect(errors(d).slice(-3)).toEqual(["Member m4 is not active", "No member with id m9", "No team with id t9"]);
    d = reducer(d, setMemberStatus({ id: "m2", status: "REMOVED", at: LATER }));
    expect(d.state.global.teams[0].memberIds).toEqual(["m3"]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the reducer**

Write `document-models/renown-organization/v1/src/reducers/members.ts`:

```ts
import type {
  RenownOrganizationMembersOperations,
  RenownOrganizationState,
  RenownOrgMember,
  RenownOrgTeam,
} from "document-models/renown-organization/v1";
import {
  DuplicateMemberError,
  DuplicateTeamIdError,
  DuplicateTeamSlugError,
  InvalidAppDidError,
  InvalidStatusTransitionError,
  InvalidTeamError,
  LastOwnerError,
  MemberNotActiveError,
  MemberNotFoundError,
  TeamNotFoundError,
  TooManyMembersError,
  TooManyTeamsError,
} from "../../gen/members/error.js";
import {
  isOrgAppDid,
  isTeamName,
  isTeamSlug,
  MAX_ACTIVE_MEMBERS,
  MAX_SCOPE_APPS,
  MAX_TEAMS,
} from "../utils.js";

type Role = RenownOrgMember["role"];
type Status = RenownOrgMember["status"];

/** The only status changes a membership record may make. */
const NEXT_STATUS: Partial<Record<Status, readonly Status[]>> = {
  INVITED: ["ACTIVE", "DECLINED", "REVOKED"],
  ACTIVE: ["LEFT", "REMOVED"],
};

function isOpen(status: Status): boolean {
  return status === "INVITED" || status === "ACTIVE";
}

/** Owners and admins act for every app of the organization. */
function hasAllScope(role: Role): boolean {
  return role === "OWNER" || role === "ADMIN";
}

function memberById(state: RenownOrganizationState, id: string): RenownOrgMember {
  const member = state.members.find((m) => m.id === id);
  if (!member) throw new MemberNotFoundError(`No member with id ${id}`);
  return member;
}

function teamById(state: RenownOrganizationState, id: string): RenownOrgTeam {
  const team = state.teams.find((t) => t.id === id);
  if (!team) throw new TeamNotFoundError(`No team with id ${id}`);
  return team;
}

/** LastOwnerError when `next` would take the organization from one active owner to none. */
function assertKeepsAnOwner(
  state: RenownOrganizationState,
  member: RenownOrgMember,
  next: { role: Role; status: Status },
): void {
  const ownsNow = member.status === "ACTIVE" && member.role === "OWNER";
  const ownsAfter = next.status === "ACTIVE" && next.role === "OWNER";
  if (!ownsNow || ownsAfter) return;
  const owners = state.members.filter((m) => m.status === "ACTIVE" && m.role === "OWNER").length;
  if (owners <= 1) throw new LastOwnerError("The organization must keep at least one owner");
}

/** Deduplicated, validated app DIDs (at most MAX_SCOPE_APPS). */
function appDidList(appDids: readonly string[]): string[] {
  const unique = [...new Set(appDids)];
  for (const appDid of unique) {
    if (!isOrgAppDid(appDid)) throw new InvalidAppDidError(`Invalid app DID: ${appDid}`);
  }
  if (unique.length > MAX_SCOPE_APPS) throw new InvalidAppDidError(`At most ${MAX_SCOPE_APPS} app DIDs`);
  return unique;
}

export const renownOrganizationMembersOperations: RenownOrganizationMembersOperations = {
  inviteMemberOperation(state, action) {
    const { id, address, role, invitedAt } = action.input;
    const lower = address.toLowerCase();
    if (state.members.some((m) => m.id === id || (m.address === lower && isOpen(m.status)))) {
      throw new DuplicateMemberError(`${lower} already has an open membership, or the id ${id} is used`);
    }
    state.members.push({
      id,
      address: lower,
      role,
      status: "INVITED",
      appScope: { all: hasAllScope(role), appDids: [] },
      hidden: false,
      joinedAt: null,
      updatedAt: invitedAt,
    });
  },
  setMemberStatusOperation(state, action) {
    const { id, status, at } = action.input;
    const member = memberById(state, id);
    if (!(NEXT_STATUS[member.status] ?? []).includes(status)) {
      throw new InvalidStatusTransitionError(`${member.status} cannot become ${status}`);
    }
    if (status === "ACTIVE" && state.members.filter((m) => m.status === "ACTIVE").length >= MAX_ACTIVE_MEMBERS) {
      throw new TooManyMembersError(`The organization already has ${MAX_ACTIVE_MEMBERS} active members`);
    }
    assertKeepsAnOwner(state, member, { role: member.role, status });
    member.status = status;
    member.updatedAt = at;
    if (status === "ACTIVE") {
      member.joinedAt = at;
      return;
    }
    for (const team of state.teams) team.memberIds = team.memberIds.filter((memberId) => memberId !== id);
  },
  setMemberRoleOperation(state, action) {
    const { id, role, at } = action.input;
    const member = memberById(state, id);
    if (!isOpen(member.status)) throw new MemberNotActiveError(`Member ${id} is ${member.status}`);
    assertKeepsAnOwner(state, member, { role, status: member.status });
    member.role = role;
    if (hasAllScope(role)) member.appScope = { all: true, appDids: member.appScope.appDids };
    member.updatedAt = at;
  },
  setMemberAppScopeOperation(state, action) {
    const { id, all, appDids, at } = action.input;
    const member = memberById(state, id);
    if (!isOpen(member.status)) throw new MemberNotActiveError(`Member ${id} is ${member.status}`);
    member.appScope = { all: all || hasAllScope(member.role), appDids: appDidList(appDids) };
    member.updatedAt = at;
  },
  setMemberHiddenOperation(state, action) {
    const member = memberById(state, action.input.id);
    member.hidden = action.input.hidden;
    member.updatedAt = action.input.at;
  },
  addTeamOperation(state, action) {
    const { id, slug, name, appDids } = action.input;
    if (state.teams.some((t) => t.id === id)) throw new DuplicateTeamIdError(`Team id ${id} is used`);
    if (state.teams.length >= MAX_TEAMS) throw new TooManyTeamsError(`An organization holds at most ${MAX_TEAMS} teams`);
    if (!isTeamSlug(slug) || !isTeamName(name)) throw new InvalidTeamError("Team slug or name is malformed");
    if (state.teams.some((t) => t.slug === slug)) throw new DuplicateTeamSlugError(`Another team has the slug ${slug}`);
    state.teams.push({ id, slug, name, appDids: appDidList(appDids), memberIds: [] });
  },
  updateTeamOperation(state, action) {
    const { id } = action.input;
    const team = teamById(state, id);
    const slug = action.input.slug ?? team.slug;
    const name = action.input.name ?? team.name;
    if (!isTeamSlug(slug) || !isTeamName(name)) throw new InvalidTeamError("Team slug or name is malformed");
    if (state.teams.some((t) => t.id !== id && t.slug === slug)) {
      throw new DuplicateTeamSlugError(`Another team has the slug ${slug}`);
    }
    const appDids = action.input.appDids ? appDidList(action.input.appDids) : team.appDids;
    team.slug = slug;
    team.name = name;
    team.appDids = appDids;
  },
  removeTeamOperation(state, action) {
    const index = state.teams.findIndex((t) => t.id === action.input.id);
    if (index === -1) throw new TeamNotFoundError(`No team with id ${action.input.id}`);
    state.teams.splice(index, 1);
  },
  setTeamMembersOperation(state, action) {
    const team = teamById(state, action.input.id);
    const memberIds = [...new Set(action.input.memberIds)];
    for (const memberId of memberIds) {
      if (memberById(state, memberId).status !== "ACTIVE") {
        throw new MemberNotActiveError(`Member ${memberId} is not active`);
      }
    }
    team.memberIds = memberIds;
  },
};
```

```ts
import { privateKeyToAccount } from "viem/accounts";
import { GraphQLError } from "graphql";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { orgMessage } from "../../../sdk/orgs.js";
import { provenAddress, requireAddress } from "../core/auth.js";
import { loadOrgsConfig, orgDid } from "../core/config.js";
import { badInput, orgError } from "../core/errors.js";

const chain = vi.hoisted(() => ({ verifies: false }));
vi.mock("../../renown-auth/core/smart-wallet.js", () => ({
  verifyMessageOnChain: () => Promise.resolve(chain.verifies),
  verifyTypedDataOnChain: () => Promise.resolve(false),
}));

const ALICE = privateKeyToAccount("0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80");
const BOB = "0xb0b0000000000000000000000000000000000002";
const NOW = new Date("2026-10-12T12:00:00.000Z");

async function signed(field: string, args: Record<string, unknown>, timestamp = NOW.toISOString()) {
  const message = await orgMessage(field, typeof args.orgId === "string" ? args.orgId : null, args, timestamp);
  return { address: ALICE.address, signature: await ALICE.signMessage({ message }), timestamp };
}

async function code(promise: Promise<unknown>): Promise<unknown> {
  const error = await promise.then(() => undefined, (e: unknown) => e);
  expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(GraphQLError);
  return (error as GraphQLError).extensions.code;
}

describe("caller resolution", () => {
  it("uses the bearer when no auth is sent, lowercased", async () => {
    expect(await provenAddress({ user: { address: "0xB0B0000000000000000000000000000000000002" } }, "leaveOrganization", { orgId: "o" }, NOW)).toBe(BOB);
    expect(await provenAddress({}, "leaveOrganization", { orgId: "o" }, NOW)).toBeNull();
    expect(await provenAddress({ user: { address: "not-an-address" } }, "x", {}, NOW)).toBeNull();
    expect(await code(requireAddress({}, "leaveOrganization", { orgId: "o" }, NOW))).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
  });

  it("accepts a signed message over exactly the sent arguments, even when a bearer is present", async () => {
    const args = { orgId: "org-1", invitee: "bob", role: "MEMBER" };
    const auth = await signed("inviteOrgMember", args);
    expect(await requireAddress({ user: { address: BOB } }, "inviteOrgMember", { ...args, auth }, NOW)).toBe(
      ALICE.address.toLowerCase(),
    );
  });

  it("never falls back to the bearer when auth is present but wrong", async () => {
    const auth = await signed("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: "org-1", invitee: "bob", role: "MEMBER" });
    const ctx = { user: { address: BOB } };
    expect(await code(provenAddress(ctx, "inviteOrgMember", { orgId: "org-1", invitee: "bob", role: "ADMIN", auth }, NOW))).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect(await code(provenAddress(ctx, "inviteOrgMember", { orgId: "org-2", invitee: "bob", role: "MEMBER", auth }, NOW))).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect(await code(provenAddress(ctx, "revokeOrgInvite", { orgId: "org-1", invitee: "bob", role: "MEMBER", auth }, NOW))).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
  });

  it("refuses stale timestamps and malformed addresses", async () => {
    const stale = await signed("leaveOrganization", { orgId: "o" }, "2026-10-12T11:49:00.000Z");
    expect(await code(provenAddress({}, "leaveOrganization", { orgId: "o", auth: stale }, NOW))).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    const fresh = await signed("leaveOrganization", { orgId: "o" });
    expect(await code(provenAddress({}, "leaveOrganization", { orgId: "o", auth: { ...fresh, address: "alice" } }, NOW))).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
  });

  it("accepts a smart-wallet signature the chain verifies", async () => {
    chain.verifies = true;
    try {
      const auth = { address: BOB, signature: "0x1234", timestamp: NOW.toISOString() };
      expect(await requireAddress({}, "leaveOrganization", { orgId: "o", auth }, NOW)).toBe(BOB);
    } finally {
      chain.verifies = false;
    }
  });
});

});
```

Create `sdk/orgs.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Renown organisations: framework-free helpers shared by renown-package (the
 * renown-orgs subgraph), renown.id and Vetra. No imports; WebCrypto only, so
 * it runs in browsers and Node 20+.
 */

/** Organisation roles, highest first. */
export const ORG_ROLES = ["OWNER", "ADMIN", "PUBLISHER", "MEMBER"] as const;
export type OrgRole = (typeof ORG_ROLES)[number];

const RANK: Record<OrgRole, number> = { MEMBER: 0, PUBLISHER: 1, ADMIN: 2, OWNER: 3 };

/** The role named by `value` (any case, trimmed), or null. */
export function parseOrgRole(value: string): OrgRole | null {
  const upper = value.trim().toUpperCase();
  return Object.hasOwn(RANK, upper) ? (upper as OrgRole) : null;
}

/** True when `role` is `min` or higher; unknown roles never qualify. */
export function roleAtLeast(role: string, min: string): boolean {
  const have = parseOrgRole(role);
  const need = parseOrgRole(min);
  return have !== null && need !== null && RANK[have] >= RANK[need];
}

function sortKeys(value: unknown): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((item) => (item === undefined ? null : sortKeys(item)));
  if (value !== null && typeof value === "object") {
    const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const key of Object.keys(record).sort()) {
      if (record[key] !== undefined) out[key] = sortKeys(record[key]);
    }
    return out;
  }
  return value;
}

/** JSON with object keys sorted recursively, no whitespace, undefined dropped and null kept. */
export function canonicalJson(value: unknown): string {
  return JSON.stringify(sortKeys(value)) ?? "null";
}

export async function sha256Hex(input: string): Promise<string> {
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(input));
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest), (byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

/**
 * The message a wallet personal_signs to authorise a renown-orgs field
 * (mutation, or a query that needs a caller) without a bearer:
 *   Renown organization <field> <orgId or -> <sha256 hex of canonicalJson(args)> at <timestamp>
 * `args` are the field's arguments exactly as sent; `auth` and `orgId` are
 * dropped from them before hashing. `timestamp`: ISO-8601 with a zone,
 * within 10 minutes of the server's clock.
 */
export async function orgMessage(
  mutation: string,
  orgId: string | null | undefined,
  args: Record<string, unknown>,
  timestamp: string,
): Promise<string> {
  const { auth: _auth, orgId: _orgId, ...rest } = args;
  return `Renown organization ${mutation} ${orgId || "-"} ${await sha256Hex(canonicalJson(rest))} at ${timestamp}`;
}
```

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/core/config.ts`:

```ts
export const DEFAULT_ORG_DID_HOST = "www.renown.id";
const DEFAULT_TIMELOCK_SECONDS = 172_800;
const DEFAULT_RECOVERY_SECONDS = 604_800;
const HOST_RE = /^[a-z0-9.-]+(?::[0-9]{1,5})?$/;
const ADDRESS_RE = /^0x[0-9a-f]{40}$/;

export interface OrgsConfig {
  /** did:web host, ":" percent-encoded (did:web rules). */
  didHost: string;
  /** Delay of ADD_OWNER, REMOVE_OWNER, TRANSFER_APP_OUT, CHANGE_HANDLE and DELETE_ORG proposals. */
  timelockSeconds: number;
  /** Delay of RECOVER proposals. */
  recoverySeconds: number;
  /** Lowercase addresses that resolve organization reports. */
  platformAdmins: ReadonlySet<string>;
}

function positiveSeconds(raw: string | undefined, fallback: number): number {
  const value = Number(raw?.trim());
  return Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value > 0 ? value : fallback;
}

function addresses(raw: string): Set<string> {
  return new Set(
    raw
      .split(",")
      .map((entry) => entry.trim().toLowerCase())
      .filter((entry) => ADDRESS_RE.test(entry)),
  );
}

/** Reads the renown-orgs settings; every malformed value falls back to its safe default. */
export function loadOrgsConfig(env: Record<string, string | undefined>): OrgsConfig {
  const host = env.RENOWN_ORG_DID_HOST?.trim().toLowerCase();
  const admins = env.RENOWN_PLATFORM_ADMINS?.trim() ? env.RENOWN_PLATFORM_ADMINS : (env.ADMINS ?? "");
  return {
    didHost: host && HOST_RE.test(host) ? host.replace(":", "%3A") : DEFAULT_ORG_DID_HOST,
    timelockSeconds: positiveSeconds(env.RENOWN_ORG_TIMELOCK_SECONDS, DEFAULT_TIMELOCK_SECONDS),
    recoverySeconds: positiveSeconds(env.RENOWN_ORG_RECOVERY_SECONDS, DEFAULT_RECOVERY_SECONDS),
    platformAdmins: addresses(admins),
  };
}

/** did:web:<host>:org:<orgId>, served by renown.id at https://<host>/org/<orgId>/did.json. */
export function orgDid(didHost: string, orgId: string): string {
  return `did:web:${didHost}:org:${orgId}`;
}
```

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/core/errors.ts`:

```ts
import { GraphQLError } from "graphql";

/** The only extensions.code values renown-orgs answers with. */
export type OrgErrorCode =
  | "BAD_USER_INPUT"
  | "FORBIDDEN"
  | "NOT_FOUND"
  | "HANDLE_TAKEN"
  | "HANDLE_RESERVED"
  | "LAST_OWNER"
  | "LIMIT_REACHED"
  | "DOMAIN_TAKEN"
  | "DOMAIN_PROOF_MISSING"
  | "ORG_HAS_APPS"
  | "APP_FROZEN"
  | "PROPOSAL_NOT_EXECUTABLE"
  | "RATE_LIMITED"
  | "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE";

export function orgError(code: OrgErrorCode, message: string, field?: string): GraphQLError {
  return new GraphQLError(message, { extensions: field ? { code, field } : { code } });
}

export function forbidden(message = "Forbidden"): GraphQLError {
  return orgError("FORBIDDEN", message);
}

export function notFound(what: string): GraphQLError {
  return orgError("NOT_FOUND", `${what} not found`);
}

/** An argument the caller must fix; `field` names it so a form can show the error inline. */
export function badInput(field: string, message: string): GraphQLError {
  return orgError("BAD_USER_INPUT", message, field);
}

export function limitReached(message: string): GraphQLError {
  return orgError("LIMIT_REACHED", message);
}

export function rateLimited(): GraphQLError {
  return orgError("RATE_LIMITED", "Rate limited");
}

export function unavailable(message = "Organizations are temporarily unavailable"): GraphQLError {
  return orgError("SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE", message);
}
```

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/core/auth.ts`:

```ts
import { orgMessage } from "../../../sdk/orgs.js";
import { isFreshTimestamp, verifySignedMessage } from "../../renown-auth/core/signed-message.js";
import { verifyMessageOnChain } from "../../renown-auth/core/smart-wallet.js";
import { forbidden } from "./errors.js";

const ADDRESS_RE = /^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}$/;
// Org messages carry no chain; smart-wallet signatures are checked on chain 1.
const MESSAGE_CHAIN_ID = 1;

/** A personal_sign over orgMessage(field, orgId, args, timestamp). */
export interface OrgAuthInput {
  address: string;
  signature: string;
  timestamp: string;
}

/** The host's resolver context: `user` is set only when the host resolved a bearer. */
export interface OrgContext {
  user?: { address?: string; appKey?: string };
  headers?: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;
}

export type OrgArgs = Record<string, unknown> & { auth?: OrgAuthInput | null };

/**
 * The lowercase address the caller proves, or null when they prove none.
 * A present `auth` is always verified (EOA offline, then ERC-1271/6492 on
 * chain 1) and never falls back to the bearer: a wrong one is FORBIDDEN.
 * Without `auth`, the host-resolved bearer's address.
 */
export async function provenAddress(
  ctx: OrgContext,
  field: string,
  args: OrgArgs,
  now: Date,
): Promise<string | null> {
  const auth = args.auth;
  if (auth) {
    if (!ADDRESS_RE.test(auth.address) || !isFreshTimestamp(auth.timestamp, now)) throw forbidden();
    const orgId = typeof args.orgId === "string" ? args.orgId : null;
    const message = await orgMessage(field, orgId, args, auth.timestamp);
    const address = auth.address.toLowerCase();
    const signed =
      (await verifySignedMessage({ address, message, signature: auth.signature })) ||
      (await verifyMessageOnChain({ chainId: MESSAGE_CHAIN_ID, address, message, signature: auth.signature }));
    if (!signed) throw forbidden();
    return address;
  }
  const bearer = ctx.user?.address;
  return bearer && ADDRESS_RE.test(bearer) ? bearer.toLowerCase() : null;
}

/** provenAddress, but FORBIDDEN when the caller proves nothing. */
export async function requireAddress(ctx: OrgContext, field: string, args: OrgArgs, now: Date): Promise<string> {
  const address = await provenAddress(ctx, field, args, now);
  if (address === null) throw forbidden();
  return address;
}
```

- Produces (`store/types.ts`): `ORGS_NAMESPACE = "renown-orgs"`, `type OrgRole`, `type StoredMemberStatus`, `type DomainStatus`, `interface OrgLink`, `interface OrgRecord`, `type OrgPatch`, `interface MemberRecord`, `type MemberPatch`, `interface TeamRecord`, `type TeamPatch`, `interface InviteLinkRecord`, `interface OrgAppRecord`, `interface DomainRecord`, `interface AuditRecord`, `interface AuditCursor`, `interface ReservedHandleRecord`, `interface OrgSearch`, `interface OrgsDB`, `type OrgsKysely`, `interface OrgStore` (methods below); `store/kysely.ts`: `class KyselyOrgStore implements OrgStore`, `jsonValue<T>(value: unknown, fallback: T): T`, `toDate(value: Date | string): Date`; `store/migrations.ts`: `migrate(db: Kysely<any>): Promise<void>`; `store/memory.ts`: `openMemoryOrgStore(): Promise<{ store: KyselyOrgStore; db: OrgsKysely; close(): Promise<void> }>`.

`OrgStore` (A1 part; A2/A3 add methods to the same interface):

```ts
insertOrganization(org: OrgRecord): Promise<boolean>;           // false when the id or LOWER(handle) is taken
organization(id: string): Promise<OrgRecord | undefined>;
organizationByHandle(handle: string): Promise<OrgRecord | undefined>; // case-insensitive
organizationsByIds(ids: readonly string[]): Promise<OrgRecord[]>;
updateOrganization(id: string, patch: OrgPatch, now: Date): Promise<OrgRecord | undefined>;
countOrganizationsCreatedBy(address: string): Promise<number>;
searchOrganizations(search: OrgSearch): Promise<OrgRecord[]>;     // listed, not archived, not taken down; at most limit + 1 rows
insertMember(member: MemberRecord): Promise<void>;
member(id: string): Promise<MemberRecord | undefined>;
openMember(orgId: string, address: string): Promise<MemberRecord | undefined>; // INVITED or ACTIVE
updateMember(id: string, patch: MemberPatch): Promise<MemberRecord | undefined>;
members(orgId: string, statuses?: readonly StoredMemberStatus[]): Promise<MemberRecord[]>; // invitedAt, id order
membershipsOf(address: string, statuses: readonly StoredMemberStatus[]): Promise<MemberRecord[]>;
insertTeam(team: TeamRecord): Promise<void>;
team(id: string): Promise<TeamRecord | undefined>;
teams(orgId: string): Promise<TeamRecord[]>;                       // createdAt, id order, memberIds included
updateTeam(id: string, patch: TeamPatch): Promise<TeamRecord | undefined>;
deleteTeam(id: string): Promise<boolean>;
setTeamMembers(teamId: string, memberIds: readonly string[]): Promise<void>;
removeMemberFromTeams(memberId: string): Promise<void>;
insertInviteLink(link: InviteLinkRecord): Promise<void>;
inviteLink(id: string): Promise<InviteLinkRecord | undefined>;
inviteLinkByHash(tokenHash: string): Promise<InviteLinkRecord | undefined>;
inviteLinks(orgId: string): Promise<InviteLinkRecord[]>;           // newest first
useInviteLink(id: string, now: Date): Promise<boolean>;            // atomic: not revoked, not expired, uses < maxUses
revokeInviteLink(id: string): Promise<void>;
orgApps(orgId: string): Promise<OrgAppRecord[]>;
orgOfApp(appDid: string): Promise<OrgAppRecord | undefined>;
verifiedDomains(orgIds: readonly string[]): Promise<Map<string, string[]>>;
verifiedOrgNamed(name: string, exceptOrgId: string): Promise<OrgRecord | undefined>;
appendAudit(entry: AuditRecord): Promise<void>;
auditPage(orgId: string, limit: number, after?: AuditCursor): Promise<AuditRecord[]>; // newest first
reserveHandle(record: ReservedHandleRecord): Promise<void>;        // upsert
reservedHandle(handle: string, now: Date): Promise<ReservedHandleRecord | undefined>; // only while until > now
```

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/tests/store-contract.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { MemberRecord, OrgRecord, OrgStore, OrgsKysely, TeamRecord } from "../store/types.js";

export interface OpenedStore {
  store: OrgStore;
  /** The same database, for rows A1 has no writer for yet (org_apps, org_domains). */
  db: OrgsKysely;
}

const T0 = new Date("2026-10-12T12:00:00.000Z");
const at = (minutes: number) => new Date(T0.getTime() + minutes * 60_000);
const ALICE = "0xabc0000000000000000000000000000000000001";
const BOB = "0xb0b0000000000000000000000000000000000002";

export function org(id: string, handle: string, over: Partial<OrgRecord> = {}): OrgRecord {
  return {
    id,
    handle,
    name: handle.toUpperCase(),
    bio: null,
    website: null,
    avatarRef: null,
    links: [],
    listed: true,
    membersPublic: false,
    archived: false,
    takenDown: false,
    createdBy: ALICE,
    createdAt: T0,
    updatedAt: T0,
    ...over,
  };
}

export function memberRecord(id: string, orgId: string, address: string, over: Partial<MemberRecord> = {}): MemberRecord {
  return {
    id,
    orgId,
    address,
    role: "MEMBER",
    status: "ACTIVE",
    appScopeAll: false,
    appDids: [],
    hidden: false,
    invitedBy: ALICE,
    invitedAt: T0,
    joinedAt: T0,
    endedAt: null,
    updatedAt: T0,
    ...over,
  };
}

function team(id: string, orgId: string, slug: string, createdAt = T0): TeamRecord {
  return { id, orgId, slug, name: slug, appDids: [], memberIds: [], createdAt };
}

export function describeOrgStoreContract(name: string, open: () => Promise<OpenedStore>): void {
  describe(name, () => {
    it("stores one organization per id and per handle, case-insensitively", async () => {
      const { store } = await open();
      expect(await store.insertOrganization(org("o1", "acme", { links: [{ id: "l1", label: "Docs", url: "https://d.example" }] }))).toBe(true);
      expect(await store.insertOrganization(org("o2", "ACME"))).toBe(false);
      expect(await store.insertOrganization(org("o1", "other"))).toBe(false);
      expect(await store.organization("o1")).toEqual(org("o1", "acme", { links: [{ id: "l1", label: "Docs", url: "https://d.example" }] }));
      expect((await store.organizationByHandle("Acme"))?.id).toBe("o1");
      expect(await store.organization("o2")).toBeUndefined();
      expect((await store.organizationsByIds(["o1", "nope"])).map((o) => o.id)).toEqual(["o1"]);
      expect(await store.organizationsByIds([])).toEqual([]);
    });

    it("patches an organization and counts what an address created", async () => {
      const { store } = await open();
      await store.insertOrganization(org("o1", "acme"));
      await store.insertOrganization(org("o2", "beta", { createdBy: BOB }));
      const updated = await store.updateOrganization("o1", { name: "Acme Inc.", bio: "Hi", listed: false, archived: true }, at(5));
      expect(updated).toMatchObject({ name: "Acme Inc.", bio: "Hi", listed: false, archived: true, handle: "acme", updatedAt: at(5) });
      expect(await store.updateOrganization("nope", { name: "x" }, at(5))).toBeUndefined();
      expect(await store.countOrganizationsCreatedBy(ALICE)).toBe(1);
      expect(await store.countOrganizationsCreatedBy(BOB)).toBe(1);
    });

    it("searches listed, live organizations by handle or name prefix with a keyset cursor", async () => {
      const { store } = await open();
      await store.insertOrganization(org("o1", "acme", { name: "Acme" }));
      await store.insertOrganization(org("o2", "acorn", { name: "Oak Labs" }));
      await store.insertOrganization(org("o3", "zeta", { name: "Ace Ventures" }));
      await store.insertOrganization(org("o4", "acid", { listed: false }));
      await store.insertOrganization(org("o5", "aced", { archived: true }));
      await store.insertOrganization(org("o6", "ace", { takenDown: true }));
      await store.insertOrganization(org("o7", "a_b", { name: "Under" }));
      const handles = (rows: OrgRecord[]) => rows.map((o) => o.handle);
      expect(handles(await store.searchOrganizations({ pattern: "ac%", limit: 10 }))).toEqual(["acme", "acorn", "zeta"]);
      const first = await store.searchOrganizations({ pattern: "ac%", limit: 1 });
      expect(handles(first)).toEqual(["acme", "acorn"]);
      expect(handles(await store.searchOrganizations({ pattern: "ac%", limit: 1, after: { handle: "acme", id: "o1" } }))).toEqual(["acorn", "zeta"]);
      expect(handles(await store.searchOrganizations({ pattern: "a\\_%", limit: 10 }))).toEqual(["a_b"]);
      expect(handles(await store.searchOrganizations({ pattern: null, limit: 10 }))).toEqual(["a_b", "acme", "acorn", "zeta"]);
    });

    it("keeps membership records, one open record per address and org", async () => {
      const { store } = await open();
      await store.insertMember(memberRecord("m1", "o1", ALICE, { role: "OWNER", appScopeAll: true }));
      await store.insertMember(memberRecord("m2", "o1", BOB, { status: "INVITED", joinedAt: null, invitedAt: at(1), appDids: ["did:key:z1"] }));
      await expect(store.insertMember(memberRecord("m3", "o1", BOB))).rejects.toThrow();
      expect(await store.member("m2")).toEqual(memberRecord("m2", "o1", BOB, { status: "INVITED", joinedAt: null, invitedAt: at(1), appDids: ["did:key:z1"] }));
      expect((await store.openMember("o1", BOB))?.id).toBe("m2");
      const declined = await store.updateMember("m2", { status: "DECLINED", endedAt: at(2), updatedAt: at(2) });
      expect(declined).toMatchObject({ status: "DECLINED", endedAt: at(2), appDids: ["did:key:z1"] });
      expect(await store.openMember("o1", BOB)).toBeUndefined();
      await store.insertMember(memberRecord("m3", "o1", BOB, { invitedAt: at(3) }));
      expect((await store.members("o1")).map((m) => m.id)).toEqual(["m1", "m2", "m3"]);
      expect((await store.members("o1", ["ACTIVE"])).map((m) => m.id)).toEqual(["m1", "m3"]);
      await store.insertMember(memberRecord("m4", "o2", BOB, { status: "INVITED" }));
      expect((await store.membershipsOf(BOB, ["ACTIVE", "INVITED"])).map((m) => m.id).sort()).toEqual(["m3", "m4"]);
      expect(await store.updateMember("nope", { hidden: true, updatedAt: at(4) })).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it("keeps teams with their members", async () => {
      const { store } = await open();
      await store.insertMember(memberRecord("m1", "o1", ALICE));
      await store.insertMember(memberRecord("m2", "o1", BOB));
      await store.insertTeam({ ...team("t1", "o1", "core"), appDids: ["did:key:za"] });
      await store.insertTeam(team("t2", "o1", "web", at(1)));
      await expect(store.insertTeam(team("t3", "o1", "core"))).rejects.toThrow();
      await store.setTeamMembers("t1", ["m1", "m2"]);
      await store.setTeamMembers("t2", ["m2"]);
      expect(await store.team("t1")).toEqual({ ...team("t1", "o1", "core"), appDids: ["did:key:za"], memberIds: ["m1", "m2"] });
      await store.setTeamMembers("t1", ["m1"]);
      await store.removeMemberFromTeams("m2");
      expect((await store.teams("o1")).map((t) => [t.slug, t.memberIds])).toEqual([["core", ["m1"]], ["web", []]]);
      expect(await store.updateTeam("t2", { name: "Web team", appDids: ["did:key:zb"] })).toMatchObject({ slug: "web", name: "Web team", appDids: ["did:key:zb"] });
      expect(await store.updateTeam("nope", { name: "x" })).toBeUndefined();
      expect(await store.deleteTeam("t1")).toBe(true);
      expect(await store.deleteTeam("t1")).toBe(false);
      expect((await store.teams("o1")).map((t) => t.id)).toEqual(["t2"]);
    });

    it("uses an invite link atomically, never after revocation or expiry", async () => {
      const { store } = await open();
      const link = { id: "k1", orgId: "o1", tokenHash: "h1", role: "MEMBER" as const, maxUses: 2, uses: 0, expiresAt: at(60), revoked: false, createdBy: ALICE, createdAt: T0 };
      await store.insertInviteLink(link);
      await store.insertInviteLink({ ...link, id: "k2", tokenHash: "h2", createdAt: at(1) });
      expect((await store.inviteLinkByHash("h1"))?.id).toBe("k1");
      const uses = await Promise.all([store.useInviteLink("k1", at(1)), store.useInviteLink("k1", at(1)), store.useInviteLink("k1", at(1))]);
      expect(uses.filter(Boolean)).toHaveLength(2);
      expect((await store.inviteLink("k1"))?.uses).toBe(2);
      expect(await store.useInviteLink("k2", at(61))).toBe(false);
      await store.revokeInviteLink("k2");
      expect(await store.useInviteLink("k2", at(2))).toBe(false);
      expect((await store.inviteLinks("o1")).map((l) => [l.id, l.revoked])).toEqual([["k2", true], ["k1", false]]);
    });

    it("reads org apps, verified domains and verified organizations by name", async () => {
      const { store, db } = await open();
      await store.insertOrganization(org("o1", "acme", { name: "Acme" }));
      await store.insertOrganization(org("o2", "acme-real", { name: "ACME" }));
      await db.insertInto("org_apps").values({ app_did: "did:key:za", org_id: "o1", frozen: true, added_at: T0 }).execute();
      await db
        .insertInto("org_domains")
        .values([
          { id: "d1", org_id: "o2", domain: "acme.example", token: "t", status: "VERIFIED", verified_at: T0, last_checked_at: T0, failures: 0, created_at: T0 },
          { id: "d2", org_id: "o2", domain: "pending.example", token: "t", status: "PENDING", verified_at: null, last_checked_at: null, failures: 0, created_at: T0 },
        ])
        .execute();
      expect(await store.orgApps("o1")).toEqual([{ appDid: "did:key:za", orgId: "o1", frozen: true, addedAt: T0 }]);
      expect((await store.orgOfApp("did:key:za"))?.orgId).toBe("o1");
      expect(await store.orgOfApp("did:key:zz")).toBeUndefined();
      expect(await store.verifiedDomains(["o1", "o2"])).toEqual(new Map([["o2", ["acme.example"]]]));
      expect(await store.verifiedDomains([])).toEqual(new Map());
      expect((await store.verifiedOrgNamed("acme", "o1"))?.id).toBe("o2");
      expect(await store.verifiedOrgNamed("acme", "o2")).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it("pages the audit log newest first", async () => {
      const { store } = await open();
      for (const [id, minutes] of [["a1", 1], ["a2", 2], ["a3", 2], ["a4", 3]] as const) {
        await store.appendAudit({ id, orgId: "o1", at: at(minutes), actor: ALICE, action: "x", details: { n: id } });
      }
      await store.appendAudit({ id: "b1", orgId: "o2", at: at(9), actor: "system", action: "y", details: {} });
      const first = await store.auditPage("o1", 2);
      expect(first.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["a4", "a3"]);
      expect(first[0]).toEqual({ id: "a4", orgId: "o1", at: at(3), actor: ALICE, action: "x", details: { n: "a4" } });
      expect((await store.auditPage("o1", 5, { at: at(2), id: "a3" })).map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["a2", "a1"]);
    });

    it("reserves handles until a time, and re-reserving replaces the window", async () => {
      const { store } = await open();
      await store.reserveHandle({ handle: "acme", until: at(10), reason: "ARCHIVED", orgId: "o1" });
      expect(await store.reservedHandle("acme", at(5))).toEqual({ handle: "acme", until: at(10), reason: "ARCHIVED", orgId: "o1" });
      expect(await store.reservedHandle("acme", at(10))).toBeUndefined();
      await store.reserveHandle({ handle: "acme", until: at(20), reason: "RELEASED", orgId: null });
      expect((await store.reservedHandle("acme", at(15)))?.reason).toBe("RELEASED");
    });
  });
}
```

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/tests/store.test.ts`:

```ts
import { PGlite } from "@electric-sql/pglite";
import { Kysely, sql } from "kysely";
import { PGliteDialect } from "kysely-pglite-dialect";
import { afterAll } from "vitest";
import { KyselyOrgStore } from "../store/kysely.js";
import { openMemoryOrgStore } from "../store/memory.js";
import { migrate } from "../store/migrations.js";
import { ORGS_NAMESPACE, type OrgsDB } from "../store/types.js";
import { describeOrgStoreContract } from "./store-contract.js";

const closers: (() => Promise<void>)[] = [];
afterAll(async () => {
  await Promise.all(closers.map((close) => close()));
});

describeOrgStoreContract("memory org store", async () => {
  const opened = await openMemoryOrgStore();
  closers.push(opened.close);
  return opened;
});

describeOrgStoreContract("Kysely org store in the renown-orgs namespace", async () => {
  const root = new Kysely<OrgsDB>({ dialect: new PGliteDialect(new PGlite()) });
  closers.push(() => root.destroy());
  await sql`create schema ${sql.id(ORGS_NAMESPACE)}`.execute(root);
  const db = root.withSchema(ORGS_NAMESPACE);
  // Twice: migrate() runs on every boot.
  await migrate(db);
  await migrate(db);
  return { store: new KyselyOrgStore(db), db };
});
```
