- [ ] **Step 3: Write the types**

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/store/types.ts`:

```ts
import type { ColumnType, Kysely } from "kysely";

/** The relational namespace of the renown-orgs store. Other subgraphs read it through store/lookups.ts. */
export const ORGS_NAMESPACE = "renown-orgs";

/** A `timestamptz` column: the driver returns a `Date`, but accepts either on write. */
export type Timestamp = Date | string;
/** A `jsonb` column: written as a JSON string, read back parsed (see jsonValue). */
export type JsonColumn = ColumnType<unknown, string, string>;
type Defaulted<T> = ColumnType<T, T | undefined, T>;

export type OrgRole = "OWNER" | "ADMIN" | "PUBLISHER" | "MEMBER";
export type StoredMemberStatus = "INVITED" | "ACTIVE" | "DECLINED" | "LEFT" | "REMOVED" | "REVOKED";
export type DomainStatus = "PENDING" | "VERIFIED" | "LAPSED";

export interface OrgLink {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  url: string;
}

export interface OrganizationsTable {
  id: string;
  handle: string;
  name: string;
  bio: string | null;
  website: string | null;
  avatar_ref: string | null;
  links: JsonColumn;
  listed: boolean;
  members_public: boolean;
  archived: boolean;
  taken_down: boolean;
  created_by: string;
  created_at: Timestamp;
  updated_at: Timestamp;
}

export interface OrgMembersTable {
  id: string;
  org_id: string;
  address: string;
  role: OrgRole;
  status: StoredMemberStatus;
  app_scope_all: boolean;
  app_dids: JsonColumn;
  hidden: boolean;
  invited_by: string | null;
  invited_at: Timestamp;
  joined_at: Timestamp | null;
  ended_at: Timestamp | null;
  updated_at: Timestamp;
}

export interface OrgTeamsTable {
  id: string;
  org_id: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  app_dids: JsonColumn;
  created_at: Timestamp;
}

export interface OrgTeamMembersTable {
  team_id: string;
  member_id: string;
}

export interface OrgInviteLinksTable {
  id: string;
  org_id: string;
  token_hash: string;
  role: OrgRole;
  max_uses: number;
  uses: Defaulted<number>;
  expires_at: Timestamp;
  revoked: Defaulted<boolean>;
  created_by: string;
  created_at: Timestamp;
}

export interface OrgAppsTable {
  app_did: string;
  org_id: string;
  frozen: Defaulted<boolean>;
  added_at: Timestamp;
}

export interface OrgDomainsTable {
  id: string;
  org_id: string;
  domain: string;
  token: string;
  status: DomainStatus;
  verified_at: Timestamp | null;
  last_checked_at: Timestamp | null;
  failures: Defaulted<number>;
  created_at: Timestamp;
}

export interface OrgAuditTable {
  id: string;
  org_id: string;
  at: Timestamp;
  /** Lowercase address, or "system". */
  actor: string;
  action: string;
  details: JsonColumn;
}

export interface ReservedHandlesTable {
  handle: string;
  until: Timestamp;
  reason: string;
  org_id: string | null;
}

export interface OrgsDB {
  organizations: OrganizationsTable;
  org_members: OrgMembersTable;
  org_teams: OrgTeamsTable;
  org_team_members: OrgTeamMembersTable;
  org_invite_links: OrgInviteLinksTable;
  org_apps: OrgAppsTable;
  org_domains: OrgDomainsTable;
  org_audit: OrgAuditTable;
  reserved_handles: ReservedHandlesTable;
}

export type OrgsKysely = Kysely<OrgsDB>;

export interface OrgRecord {
  id: string;
  handle: string;
  name: string;
  bio: string | null;
  website: string | null;
  avatarRef: string | null;
  links: OrgLink[];
  listed: boolean;
  membersPublic: boolean;
  archived: boolean;
  takenDown: boolean;
  createdBy: string;
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
}

export type OrgPatch = Partial<
  Pick<OrgRecord, "handle" | "name" | "bio" | "website" | "avatarRef" | "links" | "listed" | "membersPublic" | "archived" | "takenDown">
>;

export interface MemberRecord {
  id: string;
  orgId: string;
  /** Lowercase. */
  address: string;
  role: OrgRole;
  status: StoredMemberStatus;
  appScopeAll: boolean;
  appDids: string[];
  hidden: boolean;
  invitedBy: string | null;
  invitedAt: Date;
  joinedAt: Date | null;
  endedAt: Date | null;
  updatedAt: Date;
}

export type MemberPatch = Partial<
  Pick<MemberRecord, "role" | "status" | "appScopeAll" | "appDids" | "hidden" | "joinedAt" | "endedAt">
> & { updatedAt: Date };

export interface TeamRecord {
  id: string;
  orgId: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  appDids: string[];
  /** Member record ids. */
  memberIds: string[];
  createdAt: Date;
}

export type TeamPatch = Partial<Pick<TeamRecord, "slug" | "name" | "appDids">>;

export interface InviteLinkRecord {
  id: string;
  orgId: string;
  /** sha256 hex of the token; the token itself is never stored. */
  tokenHash: string;
  role: OrgRole;
  maxUses: number;
  uses: number;
  expiresAt: Date;
  revoked: boolean;
  createdBy: string;
  createdAt: Date;
}

export interface OrgAppRecord {
  appDid: string;
  orgId: string;
  frozen: boolean;
  addedAt: Date;
}

export interface DomainRecord {
  id: string;
  orgId: string;
  domain: string;
  /** The random part of the proof token. */
  token: string;
  status: DomainStatus;
  verifiedAt: Date | null;
  lastCheckedAt: Date | null;
  failures: number;
  createdAt: Date;
}

export interface AuditRecord {
  id: string;
  orgId: string;
  at: Date;
  actor: string;
  action: string;
  details: Record<string, unknown>;
}

export interface AuditCursor {
  at: Date;
  id: string;
}

export interface ReservedHandleRecord {
  handle: string;
  until: Date;
  /** ARCHIVED, CHANGED or RELEASED. */
  reason: string;
  orgId: string | null;
}

export interface OrgSearch {
  /** A LIKE prefix pattern (escape character \), or null for every listed organization. */
  pattern: string | null;
  limit: number;
  after?: { handle: string; id: string };
}

export interface OrgStore {
  insertOrganization(org: OrgRecord): Promise<boolean>;
  organization(id: string): Promise<OrgRecord | undefined>;
  organizationByHandle(handle: string): Promise<OrgRecord | undefined>;
  organizationsByIds(ids: readonly string[]): Promise<OrgRecord[]>;
  updateOrganization(id: string, patch: OrgPatch, now: Date): Promise<OrgRecord | undefined>;
  countOrganizationsCreatedBy(address: string): Promise<number>;
  searchOrganizations(search: OrgSearch): Promise<OrgRecord[]>;
  insertMember(member: MemberRecord): Promise<void>;
  member(id: string): Promise<MemberRecord | undefined>;
  openMember(orgId: string, address: string): Promise<MemberRecord | undefined>;
  updateMember(id: string, patch: MemberPatch): Promise<MemberRecord | undefined>;
  members(orgId: string, statuses?: readonly StoredMemberStatus[]): Promise<MemberRecord[]>;
  membershipsOf(address: string, statuses: readonly StoredMemberStatus[]): Promise<MemberRecord[]>;
  insertTeam(team: TeamRecord): Promise<void>;
  team(id: string): Promise<TeamRecord | undefined>;
  teams(orgId: string): Promise<TeamRecord[]>;
  updateTeam(id: string, patch: TeamPatch): Promise<TeamRecord | undefined>;
  deleteTeam(id: string): Promise<boolean>;
  setTeamMembers(teamId: string, memberIds: readonly string[]): Promise<void>;
  removeMemberFromTeams(memberId: string): Promise<void>;
  insertInviteLink(link: InviteLinkRecord): Promise<void>;
  inviteLink(id: string): Promise<InviteLinkRecord | undefined>;
  inviteLinkByHash(tokenHash: string): Promise<InviteLinkRecord | undefined>;
  inviteLinks(orgId: string): Promise<InviteLinkRecord[]>;
  useInviteLink(id: string, now: Date): Promise<boolean>;
  revokeInviteLink(id: string): Promise<void>;
  orgApps(orgId: string): Promise<OrgAppRecord[]>;
  orgOfApp(appDid: string): Promise<OrgAppRecord | undefined>;
  verifiedDomains(orgIds: readonly string[]): Promise<Map<string, string[]>>;
  verifiedOrgNamed(name: string, exceptOrgId: string): Promise<OrgRecord | undefined>;
  appendAudit(entry: AuditRecord): Promise<void>;
  auditPage(orgId: string, limit: number, after?: AuditCursor): Promise<AuditRecord[]>;
  reserveHandle(record: ReservedHandleRecord): Promise<void>;
  reservedHandle(handle: string, now: Date): Promise<ReservedHandleRecord | undefined>;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the migration**

```ts
import { sql, type Kysely } from "kysely";

/**
 * Creates the renown-orgs tables. Idempotent: it runs on every boot. Built
 * with the schema builder only, so `withSchema("renown-orgs")` applies to
 * every table and index (raw `sql` DDL would land in `public`).
 */
export async function migrate(db: Kysely<any>): Promise<void> {
  await db.schema
    .createTable("organizations")
    .ifNotExists()
    .addColumn("id", "text", (col) => col.primaryKey())
    .addColumn("handle", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("name", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("bio", "text")
    .addColumn("website", "text")
    .addColumn("avatar_ref", "text")
    .addColumn("links", "jsonb", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(sql`'[]'::jsonb`))
    .addColumn("listed", "boolean", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(false))
    .addColumn("members_public", "boolean", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(false))
    .addColumn("archived", "boolean", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(false))
    .addColumn("taken_down", "boolean", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(false))
    .addColumn("created_by", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("created_at", "timestamptz", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("updated_at", "timestamptz", (col) => col.notNull())
    .execute();
  await db.schema
    .createIndex("organizations_handle_lower")
    .ifNotExists()
    .unique()
    .on("organizations")
    .expression(sql`lower(handle)`)
    .execute();
  await db.schema.createIndex("organizations_created_by").ifNotExists().on("organizations").column("created_by").execute();
  await db.schema
    .createIndex("organizations_listed_handle")
    .ifNotExists()
    .on("organizations")
    .expression(sql`lower(handle) text_pattern_ops`)
    .where(sql.ref("listed"), "=", true)
    .execute();
  await db.schema
    .createIndex("organizations_listed_name")
    .ifNotExists()
    .on("organizations")
    .expression(sql`lower(name) text_pattern_ops`)
    .where(sql.ref("listed"), "=", true)
    .execute();

  await db.schema
    .createTable("org_members")
    .ifNotExists()
    .addColumn("id", "text", (col) => col.primaryKey())
    .addColumn("org_id", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("address", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("role", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("status", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("app_scope_all", "boolean", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(false))
    .addColumn("app_dids", "jsonb", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(sql`'[]'::jsonb`))
    .addColumn("hidden", "boolean", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(false))
    .addColumn("invited_by", "text")
    .addColumn("invited_at", "timestamptz", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("joined_at", "timestamptz")
    .addColumn("ended_at", "timestamptz")
    .addColumn("updated_at", "timestamptz", (col) => col.notNull())
    .execute();
  await db.schema.createIndex("org_members_org_status").ifNotExists().on("org_members").columns(["org_id", "status"]).execute();
  await db.schema.createIndex("org_members_address_status").ifNotExists().on("org_members").columns(["address", "status"]).execute();
  // One open (INVITED or ACTIVE) record per address and org; closed records are kept as history.
  await db.schema
    .createIndex("org_members_one_open")
    .ifNotExists()
    .unique()
    .on("org_members")
    .columns(["org_id", "address"])
    .where(sql<boolean>`status IN ('INVITED', 'ACTIVE')`)
    .execute();

  await db.schema
    .createTable("org_teams")
    .ifNotExists()
    .addColumn("id", "text", (col) => col.primaryKey())
    .addColumn("org_id", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("slug", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("name", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("app_dids", "jsonb", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(sql`'[]'::jsonb`))
    .addColumn("created_at", "timestamptz", (col) => col.notNull())
    .execute();
  await db.schema.createIndex("org_teams_slug").ifNotExists().unique().on("org_teams").columns(["org_id", "slug"]).execute();
  await db.schema
    .createTable("org_team_members")
    .ifNotExists()
    .addColumn("team_id", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("member_id", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addPrimaryKeyConstraint("org_team_members_pk", ["team_id", "member_id"])
    .execute();
  await db.schema.createIndex("org_team_members_member").ifNotExists().on("org_team_members").column("member_id").execute();

  await db.schema
    .createTable("org_invite_links")
    .ifNotExists()
    .addColumn("id", "text", (col) => col.primaryKey())
    .addColumn("org_id", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("token_hash", "text", (col) => col.notNull().unique())
    .addColumn("role", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("max_uses", "integer", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("uses", "integer", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(0))
    .addColumn("expires_at", "timestamptz", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("revoked", "boolean", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(false))
    .addColumn("created_by", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("created_at", "timestamptz", (col) => col.notNull())
    .execute();
  await db.schema.createIndex("org_invite_links_org").ifNotExists().on("org_invite_links").column("org_id").execute();

  await db.schema
    .createTable("org_apps")
    .ifNotExists()
    .addColumn("app_did", "text", (col) => col.primaryKey())
    .addColumn("org_id", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("frozen", "boolean", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(false))
    .addColumn("added_at", "timestamptz", (col) => col.notNull())
    .execute();
  await db.schema.createIndex("org_apps_org").ifNotExists().on("org_apps").column("org_id").execute();

  await db.schema
    .createTable("org_domains")
    .ifNotExists()
    .addColumn("id", "text", (col) => col.primaryKey())
    .addColumn("org_id", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("domain", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("token", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("status", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("verified_at", "timestamptz")
    .addColumn("last_checked_at", "timestamptz")
    .addColumn("failures", "integer", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(0))
    .addColumn("created_at", "timestamptz", (col) => col.notNull())
    .execute();
  await db.schema.createIndex("org_domains_org").ifNotExists().on("org_domains").column("org_id").execute();
  // A domain is verified for at most one organization: the first verification wins.
  await db.schema
    .createIndex("org_domains_verified_once")
    .ifNotExists()
    .unique()
    .on("org_domains")
    .expression(sql`lower(domain)`)
    .where(sql.ref("status"), "=", "VERIFIED")
    .execute();

  await db.schema
    .createTable("org_audit")
    .ifNotExists()
    .addColumn("id", "text", (col) => col.primaryKey())
    .addColumn("org_id", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("at", "timestamptz", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("actor", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("action", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("details", "jsonb", (col) => col.notNull().defaultTo(sql`'{}'::jsonb`))
    .execute();
  await db.schema.createIndex("org_audit_org_at").ifNotExists().on("org_audit").columns(["org_id", "at", "id"]).execute();

  await db.schema
    .createTable("reserved_handles")
    .ifNotExists()
    .addColumn("handle", "text", (col) => col.primaryKey())
    .addColumn("until", "timestamptz", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("reason", "text", (col) => col.notNull())
    .addColumn("org_id", "text")
    .execute();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the Kysely store and the memory store**

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/store/kysely.ts`:

```ts
import { sql, type Selectable } from "kysely";
import type {
  AuditCursor,
  AuditRecord,
  InviteLinkRecord,
  MemberPatch,
  MemberRecord,
  OrgAppRecord,
  OrganizationsTable,
  OrgInviteLinksTable,
  OrgLink,
  OrgMembersTable,
  OrgPatch,
  OrgRecord,
  OrgSearch,
  OrgsKysely,
  OrgStore,
  OrgTeamsTable,
  ReservedHandleRecord,
  StoredMemberStatus,
  TeamPatch,
  TeamRecord,
} from "./types.js";

export function toDate(value: Date | string): Date {
  return value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
}

function toDateOrNull(value: Date | string | null): Date | null {
  return value === null ? null : toDate(value);
}

/** A jsonb value as the driver returned it (parsed, or a JSON string), or `fallback`. */
export function jsonValue<T>(value: unknown, fallback: T): T {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return fallback;
  if (typeof value === "string") {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(value) as T;
    } catch {
      return fallback;
    }
  }
  return value as T;
}

function stringList(value: unknown): string[] {
  const list = jsonValue<unknown>(value, []);
  return Array.isArray(list) ? list.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string") : [];
}

function linkList(value: unknown): OrgLink[] {
  const list = jsonValue<unknown>(value, []);
  if (!Array.isArray(list)) return [];
  return list.flatMap((item: unknown) => {
    const link = item as Partial<OrgLink> | null;
    return link && typeof link.id === "string" && typeof link.label === "string" && typeof link.url === "string"
      ? [{ id: link.id, label: link.label, url: link.url }]
      : [];
  });
}

function toOrg(row: Selectable<OrganizationsTable>): OrgRecord {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    handle: row.handle,
    name: row.name,
    bio: row.bio,
    website: row.website,
    avatarRef: row.avatar_ref,
    links: linkList(row.links),
    listed: row.listed,
    membersPublic: row.members_public,
    archived: row.archived,
    takenDown: row.taken_down,
    createdBy: row.created_by,
    createdAt: toDate(row.created_at),
    updatedAt: toDate(row.updated_at),
  };
}

function toMember(row: Selectable<OrgMembersTable>): MemberRecord {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    orgId: row.org_id,
    address: row.address,
    role: row.role,
    status: row.status,
    appScopeAll: row.app_scope_all,
    appDids: stringList(row.app_dids),
    hidden: row.hidden,
    invitedBy: row.invited_by,
    invitedAt: toDate(row.invited_at),
    joinedAt: toDateOrNull(row.joined_at),
    endedAt: toDateOrNull(row.ended_at),
    updatedAt: toDate(row.updated_at),
  };
}

function toLink(row: Selectable<OrgInviteLinksTable>): InviteLinkRecord {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    orgId: row.org_id,
    tokenHash: row.token_hash,
    role: row.role,
    maxUses: row.max_uses,
    uses: row.uses,
    expiresAt: toDate(row.expires_at),
    revoked: row.revoked,
    createdBy: row.created_by,
    createdAt: toDate(row.created_at),
  };
}

/** Kysely-backed `OrgStore`. The `db` passed in is already namespace-scoped. */
export class KyselyOrgStore implements OrgStore {
  constructor(readonly db: OrgsKysely) {}

  async insertOrganization(org: OrgRecord): Promise<boolean> {
    const inserted = await this.db
      .insertInto("organizations")
      .values({
        id: org.id,
        handle: org.handle,
        name: org.name,
        bio: org.bio,
        website: org.website,
        avatar_ref: org.avatarRef,
        links: JSON.stringify(org.links),
        listed: org.listed,
        members_public: org.membersPublic,
        archived: org.archived,
        taken_down: org.takenDown,
        created_by: org.createdBy,
        created_at: org.createdAt,
        updated_at: org.updatedAt,
      })
      .onConflict((oc) => oc.doNothing())
      .returning("id")
      .executeTakeFirst();
    return inserted !== undefined;
  }

  async organization(id: string): Promise<OrgRecord | undefined> {
    const row = await this.db.selectFrom("organizations").selectAll().where("id", "=", id).executeTakeFirst();
    return row && toOrg(row);
  }

  async organizationByHandle(handle: string): Promise<OrgRecord | undefined> {
    const row = await this.db
      .selectFrom("organizations")
      .selectAll()
      .where(sql<string>`lower(handle)`, "=", handle.toLowerCase())
      .executeTakeFirst();
    return row && toOrg(row);
  }

  async organizationsByIds(ids: readonly string[]): Promise<OrgRecord[]> {
    if (ids.length === 0) return [];
    const rows = await this.db.selectFrom("organizations").selectAll().where("id", "in", [...ids]).orderBy("id").execute();
    return rows.map(toOrg);
  }

  async updateOrganization(id: string, patch: OrgPatch, now: Date): Promise<OrgRecord | undefined> {
    const row = await this.db
      .updateTable("organizations")
      .set({
        ...(patch.handle !== undefined && { handle: patch.handle }),
        ...(patch.name !== undefined && { name: patch.name }),
        ...(patch.bio !== undefined && { bio: patch.bio }),
        ...(patch.website !== undefined && { website: patch.website }),
        ...(patch.avatarRef !== undefined && { avatar_ref: patch.avatarRef }),
        ...(patch.links !== undefined && { links: JSON.stringify(patch.links) }),
        ...(patch.listed !== undefined && { listed: patch.listed }),
        ...(patch.membersPublic !== undefined && { members_public: patch.membersPublic }),
        ...(patch.archived !== undefined && { archived: patch.archived }),
        ...(patch.takenDown !== undefined && { taken_down: patch.takenDown }),
        updated_at: now,
      })
      .where("id", "=", id)
      .returningAll()
      .executeTakeFirst();
    return row && toOrg(row);
  }

  async countOrganizationsCreatedBy(address: string): Promise<number> {
    const row = await this.db
      .selectFrom("organizations")
      .select(sql<number>`count(*)::int`.as("n"))
      .where("created_by", "=", address.toLowerCase())
      .executeTakeFirstOrThrow();
    return Number(row.n);
  }

  async searchOrganizations(search: OrgSearch): Promise<OrgRecord[]> {
    const handleKey = sql<string>`lower(handle) COLLATE "C"`;
    const idKey = sql<string>`id COLLATE "C"`;
    let query = this.db
      .selectFrom("organizations")
      .selectAll()
      .where("listed", "=", true)
      .where("archived", "=", false)
      .where("taken_down", "=", false);
    if (search.pattern !== null) {
      query = query.where(
        sql<boolean>`(lower(handle) LIKE lower(${search.pattern}) ESCAPE '\\' OR lower(name) LIKE lower(${search.pattern}) ESCAPE '\\')`,
      );
    }
    const after = search.after;
    if (after) {
      query = query.where(
        sql<boolean>`(${handleKey} > ${after.handle} OR (${handleKey} = ${after.handle} AND ${idKey} > ${after.id}))`,
      );
    }
    const rows = await query
      .orderBy(sql`${handleKey} ASC`)
      .orderBy(sql`${idKey} ASC`)
      .limit(search.limit + 1)
      .execute();
    return rows.map(toOrg);
  }

  async insertMember(member: MemberRecord): Promise<void> {
    await this.db
      .insertInto("org_members")
      .values({
        id: member.id,
        org_id: member.orgId,
        address: member.address.toLowerCase(),
        role: member.role,
        status: member.status,
        app_scope_all: member.appScopeAll,
        app_dids: JSON.stringify(member.appDids),
        hidden: member.hidden,
        invited_by: member.invitedBy,
        invited_at: member.invitedAt,
        joined_at: member.joinedAt,
        ended_at: member.endedAt,
        updated_at: member.updatedAt,
      })
      .execute();
  }

  async member(id: string): Promise<MemberRecord | undefined> {
    const row = await this.db.selectFrom("org_members").selectAll().where("id", "=", id).executeTakeFirst();
    return row && toMember(row);
  }

  async openMember(orgId: string, address: string): Promise<MemberRecord | undefined> {
    const row = await this.db
      .selectFrom("org_members")
      .selectAll()
      .where("org_id", "=", orgId)
      .where("address", "=", address.toLowerCase())
      .where("status", "in", ["INVITED", "ACTIVE"])
      .executeTakeFirst();
    return row && toMember(row);
  }

  async updateMember(id: string, patch: MemberPatch): Promise<MemberRecord | undefined> {
    const row = await this.db
      .updateTable("org_members")
      .set({
        ...(patch.role !== undefined && { role: patch.role }),
        ...(patch.status !== undefined && { status: patch.status }),
        ...(patch.appScopeAll !== undefined && { app_scope_all: patch.appScopeAll }),
        ...(patch.appDids !== undefined && { app_dids: JSON.stringify(patch.appDids) }),
        ...(patch.hidden !== undefined && { hidden: patch.hidden }),
        ...(patch.joinedAt !== undefined && { joined_at: patch.joinedAt }),
        ...(patch.endedAt !== undefined && { ended_at: patch.endedAt }),
        updated_at: patch.updatedAt,
      })
      .where("id", "=", id)
      .returningAll()
      .executeTakeFirst();
    return row && toMember(row);
  }

  async members(orgId: string, statuses?: readonly StoredMemberStatus[]): Promise<MemberRecord[]> {
    let query = this.db.selectFrom("org_members").selectAll().where("org_id", "=", orgId);
    if (statuses) query = query.where("status", "in", [...statuses]);
    const rows = await query.orderBy("invited_at").orderBy("id").execute();
    return rows.map(toMember);
  }

  async membershipsOf(address: string, statuses: readonly StoredMemberStatus[]): Promise<MemberRecord[]> {
    const rows = await this.db
      .selectFrom("org_members")
      .selectAll()
      .where("address", "=", address.toLowerCase())
      .where("status", "in", [...statuses])
      .orderBy("invited_at")
      .orderBy("id")
      .execute();
    return rows.map(toMember);
  }

  private async withMembers(rows: Selectable<OrgTeamsTable>[]): Promise<TeamRecord[]> {
    if (rows.length === 0) return [];
    const links = await this.db
      .selectFrom("org_team_members")
      .select(["team_id", "member_id"])
      .where(
        "team_id",
        "in",
        rows.map((row) => row.id),
      )
      .orderBy("member_id")
      .execute();
    return rows.map((row) => ({
      id: row.id,
      orgId: row.org_id,
      slug: row.slug,
      name: row.name,
      appDids: stringList(row.app_dids),
      memberIds: links.filter((link) => link.team_id === row.id).map((link) => link.member_id),
      createdAt: toDate(row.created_at),
    }));
  }

  async insertTeam(team: TeamRecord): Promise<void> {
    await this.db
      .insertInto("org_teams")
      .values({
        id: team.id,
        org_id: team.orgId,
        slug: team.slug,
        name: team.name,
        app_dids: JSON.stringify(team.appDids),
        created_at: team.createdAt,
      })
      .execute();
    await this.setTeamMembers(team.id, team.memberIds);
  }

  async team(id: string): Promise<TeamRecord | undefined> {
    const rows = await this.db.selectFrom("org_teams").selectAll().where("id", "=", id).execute();
    return (await this.withMembers(rows))[0];
  }

  async teams(orgId: string): Promise<TeamRecord[]> {
    const rows = await this.db
      .selectFrom("org_teams")
      .selectAll()
      .where("org_id", "=", orgId)
      .orderBy("created_at")
      .orderBy("id")
      .execute();
    return this.withMembers(rows);
  }

  async updateTeam(id: string, patch: TeamPatch): Promise<TeamRecord | undefined> {
    const updated = await this.db
      .updateTable("org_teams")
      .set({
        ...(patch.slug !== undefined && { slug: patch.slug }),
        ...(patch.name !== undefined && { name: patch.name }),
        ...(patch.appDids !== undefined && { app_dids: JSON.stringify(patch.appDids) }),
      })
      .where("id", "=", id)
      .returningAll()
      .execute();
    return (await this.withMembers(updated))[0];
  }

  async deleteTeam(id: string): Promise<boolean> {
    await this.db.deleteFrom("org_team_members").where("team_id", "=", id).execute();
    const result = await this.db.deleteFrom("org_teams").where("id", "=", id).executeTakeFirst();
    return Number(result.numDeletedRows) > 0;
  }

  async setTeamMembers(teamId: string, memberIds: readonly string[]): Promise<void> {
    await this.db.deleteFrom("org_team_members").where("team_id", "=", teamId).execute();
    const unique = [...new Set(memberIds)];
    if (unique.length === 0) return;
    await this.db
      .insertInto("org_team_members")
      .values(unique.map((memberId) => ({ team_id: teamId, member_id: memberId })))
      .execute();
  }

  async removeMemberFromTeams(memberId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.db.deleteFrom("org_team_members").where("member_id", "=", memberId).execute();
  }

  async insertInviteLink(link: InviteLinkRecord): Promise<void> {
    await this.db
      .insertInto("org_invite_links")
      .values({
        id: link.id,
        org_id: link.orgId,
        token_hash: link.tokenHash,
        role: link.role,
        max_uses: link.maxUses,
        uses: link.uses,
        expires_at: link.expiresAt,
        revoked: link.revoked,
        created_by: link.createdBy,
        created_at: link.createdAt,
      })
      .execute();
  }

  async inviteLink(id: string): Promise<InviteLinkRecord | undefined> {
    const row = await this.db.selectFrom("org_invite_links").selectAll().where("id", "=", id).executeTakeFirst();
    return row && toLink(row);
  }

  async inviteLinkByHash(tokenHash: string): Promise<InviteLinkRecord | undefined> {
    const row = await this.db
      .selectFrom("org_invite_links")
      .selectAll()
      .where("token_hash", "=", tokenHash)
      .executeTakeFirst();
    return row && toLink(row);
  }

  async inviteLinks(orgId: string): Promise<InviteLinkRecord[]> {
    const rows = await this.db
      .selectFrom("org_invite_links")
      .selectAll()
      .where("org_id", "=", orgId)
      .orderBy("created_at", "desc")
      .orderBy("id", "desc")
      .execute();
    return rows.map(toLink);
  }

  async useInviteLink(id: string, now: Date): Promise<boolean> {
    const row = await this.db
      .updateTable("org_invite_links")
      .set((eb) => ({ uses: eb("uses", "+", 1) }))
      .where("id", "=", id)
      .where("revoked", "=", false)
      .where("expires_at", ">", now)
      .where((eb) => eb("uses", "<", eb.ref("max_uses")))
      .returning("id")
      .executeTakeFirst();
    return row !== undefined;
  }

  async revokeInviteLink(id: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.db.updateTable("org_invite_links").set({ revoked: true }).where("id", "=", id).execute();
  }

  async orgApps(orgId: string): Promise<OrgAppRecord[]> {
    const rows = await this.db
      .selectFrom("org_apps")
      .selectAll()
      .where("org_id", "=", orgId)
      .orderBy("added_at")
      .orderBy("app_did")
      .execute();
    return rows.map((row) => ({ appDid: row.app_did, orgId: row.org_id, frozen: row.frozen, addedAt: toDate(row.added_at) }));
  }

  async orgOfApp(appDid: string): Promise<OrgAppRecord | undefined> {
    const row = await this.db.selectFrom("org_apps").selectAll().where("app_did", "=", appDid).executeTakeFirst();
    return row && { appDid: row.app_did, orgId: row.org_id, frozen: row.frozen, addedAt: toDate(row.added_at) };
  }

  async verifiedDomains(orgIds: readonly string[]): Promise<Map<string, string[]>> {
    const out = new Map<string, string[]>();
    if (orgIds.length === 0) return out;
    const rows = await this.db
      .selectFrom("org_domains")
      .select(["org_id", "domain"])
      .where("org_id", "in", [...orgIds])
      .where("status", "=", "VERIFIED")
      .orderBy("domain")
      .execute();
    for (const row of rows) out.set(row.org_id, [...(out.get(row.org_id) ?? []), row.domain]);
    return out;
  }

  async verifiedOrgNamed(name: string, exceptOrgId: string): Promise<OrgRecord | undefined> {
    const row = await this.db
      .selectFrom("organizations")
      .selectAll("organizations")
      .where(sql<string>`lower(organizations.name)`, "=", name.toLowerCase())
      .where("organizations.id", "<>", exceptOrgId)
      .where("organizations.taken_down", "=", false)
      .where((eb) =>
        eb.exists(
          eb
            .selectFrom("org_domains")
            .select("org_domains.id")
            .whereRef("org_domains.org_id", "=", "organizations.id")
            .where("org_domains.status", "=", "VERIFIED"),
        ),
      )
      .orderBy("organizations.created_at")
      .executeTakeFirst();
    return row && toOrg(row);
  }

  async appendAudit(entry: AuditRecord): Promise<void> {
    await this.db
      .insertInto("org_audit")
      .values({
        id: entry.id,
        org_id: entry.orgId,
        at: entry.at,
        actor: entry.actor,
        action: entry.action,
        details: JSON.stringify(entry.details),
      })
      .execute();
  }

  async auditPage(orgId: string, limit: number, after?: AuditCursor): Promise<AuditRecord[]> {
    let query = this.db.selectFrom("org_audit").selectAll().where("org_id", "=", orgId);
    if (after) {
      query = query.where((eb) =>
        eb.or([eb("at", "<", after.at), eb.and([eb("at", "=", after.at), eb("id", "<", after.id)])]),
      );
    }
    const rows = await query.orderBy("at", "desc").orderBy("id", "desc").limit(limit).execute();
    return rows.map((row) => ({
      id: row.id,
      orgId: row.org_id,
      at: toDate(row.at),
      actor: row.actor,
      action: row.action,
      details: jsonValue<Record<string, unknown>>(row.details, {}),
    }));
  }

  async reserveHandle(record: ReservedHandleRecord): Promise<void> {
    await this.db
      .insertInto("reserved_handles")
      .values({ handle: record.handle, until: record.until, reason: record.reason, org_id: record.orgId })
      .onConflict((oc) =>
        oc.column("handle").doUpdateSet({ until: record.until, reason: record.reason, org_id: record.orgId }),
      )
      .execute();
  }

  async reservedHandle(handle: string, now: Date): Promise<ReservedHandleRecord | undefined> {
    const row = await this.db
      .selectFrom("reserved_handles")
      .selectAll()
      .where("handle", "=", handle.toLowerCase())
      .where("until", ">", now)
      .executeTakeFirst();
    return row && { handle: row.handle, until: toDate(row.until), reason: row.reason, orgId: row.org_id };
  }
}
```

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/store/memory.ts`:

```ts
import { PGlite } from "@electric-sql/pglite";
import { Kysely } from "kysely";
import { PGliteDialect } from "kysely-pglite-dialect";
import { KyselyOrgStore } from "./kysely.js";
import { migrate } from "./migrations.js";
import type { OrgsDB, OrgsKysely } from "./types.js";

/**
 * An OrgStore in process memory, for tests and local experiments: the same
 * KyselyOrgStore on an in-process PGlite, so there is no second
 * implementation to drift from the production one.
 */
export async function openMemoryOrgStore(): Promise<{ store: KyselyOrgStore; db: OrgsKysely; close(): Promise<void> }> {
  const db = new Kysely<OrgsDB>({ dialect: new PGliteDialect(new PGlite()) });
  await migrate(db);
  return { store: new KyselyOrgStore(db), db, close: () => db.destroy() };
}
```

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/schema-core.ts`:

```ts
/** The A1 surface of renown-orgs: organizations, membership, teams, directory, audit log. */
export const CORE_SDL = `
  enum OrgRole {
    OWNER
    ADMIN
    PUBLISHER
    MEMBER
  }

  "EXPIRED is computed: an INVITED record older than 14 days."
  enum OrgMemberStatus {
    INVITED
    ACTIVE
    DECLINED
    LEFT
    REMOVED
    REVOKED
    EXPIRED
  }

  enum OrgHandleProblem {
    INVALID
    RESERVED
    TAKEN
  }

  enum OrgSecurityWarning {
    SINGLE_OWNER
  }

  """
  Proof for callers without a bearer: personal_sign by address over
  orgMessage(<field name>, <orgId argument or ->, <the other arguments>, timestamp)
  from sdk/orgs.ts. timestamp: ISO-8601 with a zone, within 10 minutes of the server clock.
  A present auth is always checked and never falls back to the bearer.
  """
  input OrgAuthInput {
    address: String!
    signature: String!
    timestamp: String!
  }

  input OrgLinkInput {
    id: String!
    label: String!
    url: String!
  }

  input OrgAppScopeInput {
    all: Boolean!
    appDids: [String!]!
  }

  type OrgLink {
    id: String!
    label: String!
    url: String!
  }

  type OrgAppScope {
    all: Boolean!
    appDids: [String!]!
  }

  type OrgSummary {
    id: String!
    did: String!
    handle: String!
    name: String!
    avatar: String
    verifiedDomains: [String!]!
  }

  type OrgApp {
    appDid: String!
    addedAt: String!
    frozen: Boolean!
  }

  type OrgTeam {
    id: String!
    orgId: String!
    slug: String!
    name: String!
    appDids: [String!]!
    memberAddresses: [String!]!
  }

  type OrgMembership {
    orgId: String!
    address: String!
    role: OrgRole!
    status: OrgMemberStatus!
    "The membership's own scope (all for owners and admins)."
    appScope: OrgAppScope!
    "appScope plus the app DIDs of the member's teams."
    effectiveScope: OrgAppScope!
    "Slugs of the member's teams."
    teams: [String!]!
  }

  type Organization {
    id: String!
    "did:web:<RENOWN_ORG_DID_HOST>:org:<id>"
    did: String!
    handle: String!
    name: String!
    bio: String
    website: String
    "attachment://v1:<sha256>; served at <renown>/media/<id>/avatar"
    avatar: String
    links: [OrgLink!]!
    listed: Boolean!
    membersPublic: Boolean!
    archived: Boolean!
    takenDown: Boolean!
    createdBy: String!
    createdAt: String!
    updatedAt: String!
    verifiedDomains: [String!]!
    "Another organization with a verified domain and the same name (case-insensitive), shown when this one has none."
    notAffiliatedWith: OrgSummary
    ownerCount: Int!
    memberCount: Int!
    securityWarnings: [OrgSecurityWarning!]!
    apps: [OrgApp!]!
    "Only for active members of the organization; [] otherwise."
    teams: [OrgTeam!]!
    "The caller's ACTIVE membership, when the caller proves an address."
    viewerMembership: OrgMembership
  }

  type OrgMember {
    "The membership record id; also the invite id while INVITED."
    id: String!
    orgId: String!
    address: String!
    role: OrgRole!
    status: OrgMemberStatus!
    appScope: OrgAppScope!
    hidden: Boolean!
    invitedBy: String
    invitedAt: String!
    joinedAt: String
    endedAt: String
    updatedAt: String!
    "invitedAt + 14 days while INVITED or EXPIRED; null otherwise."
    expiresAt: String
    teams: [String!]!
    handle: String
    displayName: String
    avatar: String
  }

  type OrgAffiliation {
    org: Organization!
    role: OrgRole!
    hidden: Boolean!
  }

  type OrgSearchPage {
    items: [Organization!]!
    next: String
  }

  type OrgInvite {
    id: String!
    orgId: String!
    org: OrgSummary!
    address: String!
    role: OrgRole!
    status: OrgMemberStatus!
    invitedBy: String
    createdAt: String!
    expiresAt: String!
  }

  type OrgInviteLink {
    id: String!
    orgId: String!
    role: OrgRole!
    maxUses: Int!
    uses: Int!
    expiresAt: String!
    revoked: Boolean!
    createdBy: String!
    createdAt: String!
  }

  type OrgInviteLinkCreated {
    link: OrgInviteLink!
    "Shown once; only its SHA-256 is stored."
    token: String!
  }

  type OrgInviteLinkPreview {
    org: OrgSummary!
    role: OrgRole!
    expiresAt: String!
    expired: Boolean!
    revoked: Boolean!
    "maxUses reached"
    full: Boolean!
    usable: Boolean!
  }

  type OrgHandleAvailability {
    "As it would be stored (trimmed, lowercased)"
    handle: String!
    available: Boolean!
    reason: OrgHandleProblem
  }

  type OrgAuditEntry {
    id: String!
    orgId: String!
    at: String!
    "Lowercase address, or system"
    actor: String!
    action: String!
    "JSON object"
    details: String!
  }

  type OrgAuditPage {
    items: [OrgAuditEntry!]!
    next: String
  }

  type Query {
    "By id or by handle (exactly one). Taken-down organizations come back with their profile emptied."
    organization(id: String, handle: String, auth: OrgAuthInput): Organization
    "Listed organizations; query (2-40 characters) prefix-matches handle or name, absent or blank lists all. limit 1-50 (default 20)."
    organizationSearch(query: String, limit: Int, after: String): OrgSearchPage!
    "Public memberships, or all ACTIVE ones (hidden included) when the caller proves address."
    organizationsByMember(address: String!, auth: OrgAuthInput): [OrgAffiliation!]!
    "Active members see everyone active (hidden included); admins with includeInactive see every record; others see the public list."
    organizationMembers(orgId: String!, includeInactive: Boolean, auth: OrgAuthInput): [OrgMember!]!
    "Public: an ACTIVE membership's role, status and scopes; null otherwise."
    orgMembership(orgId: String!, address: String!): OrgMembership
    "Admins: pending (INVITED and EXPIRED) direct invites."
    orgInvites(orgId: String!, auth: OrgAuthInput): [OrgInvite!]!
    "Admins: invite links, newest first."
    orgInviteLinks(orgId: String!, auth: OrgAuthInput): [OrgInviteLink!]!
    "The caller's pending invites."
    myOrgInvites(auth: OrgAuthInput): [OrgInvite!]!
    "Public preview of an invite link token."
    orgInviteLink(token: String!): OrgInviteLinkPreview
    orgHandleAvailability(handle: String!): OrgHandleAvailability!
    "Admins: newest first. limit 1-100 (default 50)."
    orgAuditLog(orgId: String!, limit: Int, after: String, auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgAuditPage!
  }

  type Mutation {
    createOrganization(
      handle: String!
      name: String!
      bio: String
      website: String
      links: [OrgLinkInput!]
      listed: Boolean
      membersPublic: Boolean
      auth: OrgAuthInput
    ): Organization!
    "Admins. Absent or null keeps a field, an empty string clears bio, website or avatar, links replaces the list."
    updateOrganization(
      orgId: String!
      name: String
      bio: String
      website: String
      avatar: String
      links: [OrgLinkInput!]
      membersPublic: Boolean
      auth: OrgAuthInput
    ): Organization!
    setOrgListed(orgId: String!, listed: Boolean!, auth: OrgAuthInput): Organization!
    "invitee: a 0x address or a Renown user handle. role up to ADMIN (ADMIN needs an owner)."
    inviteOrgMember(orgId: String!, invitee: String!, role: OrgRole!, auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgInvite!
    respondToOrgInvite(orgId: String!, inviteId: String!, accept: Boolean!, auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgMember!
    revokeOrgInvite(orgId: String!, inviteId: String!, auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgInvite!
    "role MEMBER or PUBLISHER; maxUses 1-100; expiresAt within 30 days."
    createOrgInviteLink(orgId: String!, role: OrgRole!, maxUses: Int!, expiresAt: String!, auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgInviteLinkCreated!
    revokeOrgInviteLink(orgId: String!, linkId: String!, auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgInviteLink!
    acceptOrgInviteLink(token: String!, auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgMember!
    leaveOrganization(orgId: String!, auth: OrgAuthInput): Boolean!
    removeOrgMember(orgId: String!, address: String!, auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgMember!
    "role up to ADMIN; owners change through proposals."
    setOrgMemberRole(orgId: String!, address: String!, role: OrgRole!, auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgMember!
    setOrgMemberAppScope(orgId: String!, address: String!, appScope: OrgAppScopeInput!, auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgMember!
    setMyOrgMembershipHidden(orgId: String!, hidden: Boolean!, auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgMember!
    createOrgTeam(orgId: String!, slug: String!, name: String!, appDids: [String!], auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgTeam!
    updateOrgTeam(orgId: String!, teamId: String!, slug: String, name: String, appDids: [String!], auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgTeam!
    deleteOrgTeam(orgId: String!, teamId: String!, auth: OrgAuthInput): Boolean!
    "addresses: ACTIVE members; replaces the team's members."
    setOrgTeamMembers(orgId: String!, teamId: String!, addresses: [String!]!, auth: OrgAuthInput): OrgTeam!
  }
`;
```

Replace `subgraphs/renown-orgs/schema.ts`:

```ts
import type { DocumentNode } from "graphql";
import { gql } from "graphql-tag";
import { CORE_SDL } from "./schema-core.js";

/**
 * The renown-orgs SDL: the A1 core, then each later phase's `extend type`
 * block. No argument has a default value: signed messages hash the
 * arguments as the client sent them.
 */
export const schema: DocumentNode = gql([CORE_SDL].join("\n"));
```

```ts
      `setOrgWorkloadDisabled(orgId: String!, did: String!, disabled: Boolean!, ${AUTH}): OrgWorkload!`,
      "attestRelease(appDid: String!, version: String!, artifactDigest: String!, orgId: String!, issuedAt: String!, signer: String!, signature: String!): ReleaseAttestation!",
    ],
  },
  A3: {
    Query: [
      `myNotifications(unreadOnly: Boolean, limit: Int, after: String, ${AUTH}): OrgNotificationPage!`,
      `myUnreadNotificationCount(${AUTH}): Int!`,
      `orgWebhooks(orgId: String!, ${AUTH}): [OrgWebhook!]!`,
      `orgWebhookDeliveries(orgId: String!, webhookId: String!, limit: Int, ${AUTH}): [OrgWebhookDelivery!]!`,
      `orgOidcClients(orgId: String!, ${AUTH}): [OrgOidcClient!]!`,
      `orgReports(status: OrgReportStatus, ${AUTH}): [OrgReport!]!`,
      `orgAuditExport(orgId: String!, ${AUTH}): String!`,
    ],
    Mutation: [
      `markNotificationsRead(ids: [String!], all: Boolean, ${AUTH}): Int!`,
      `createOrgWebhook(orgId: String!, url: String!, events: [String!]!, ${AUTH}): OrgWebhookCreated!`,
      `updateOrgWebhook(orgId: String!, webhookId: String!, url: String, events: [String!], enabled: Boolean, ${AUTH}): OrgWebhook!`,
      `deleteOrgWebhook(orgId: String!, webhookId: String!, ${AUTH}): Boolean!`,
      `testOrgWebhook(orgId: String!, webhookId: String!, ${AUTH}): OrgWebhookDelivery!`,
      `registerOrgOidcClient(orgId: String!, name: String!, redirectUris: [String!]!, ${AUTH}): OrgOidcClientCredentials!`,
      `updateOrgOidcClient(orgId: String!, clientId: String!, name: String, addRedirectUris: [String!], removeRedirectUris: [String!], status: OrgOidcClientStatus, ${AUTH}): OrgOidcClient!`,
      `rotateOrgOidcClientSecret(orgId: String!, clientId: String!, ${AUTH}): OrgOidcClientCredentials!`,
      `reportOrganization(orgId: String!, reason: OrgReportReason!, details: String, ${AUTH}): Boolean!`,
      `resolveOrgReport(reportId: String!, resolution: OrgReportResolution!, note: String, ${AUTH}): OrgReport!`,
    ],
  },
};

/** Every non-root type of each phase and its fields (or enum values), in declaration order. */
const TYPES: Record<Phase, Record<string, string>> = {
  A1: {
    OrgRole: "OWNER ADMIN PUBLISHER MEMBER",
    OrgMemberStatus: "INVITED ACTIVE DECLINED LEFT REMOVED REVOKED EXPIRED",
    OrgHandleProblem: "INVALID RESERVED TAKEN",
    OrgSecurityWarning: "SINGLE_OWNER",
    OrgAuthInput: "address signature timestamp",
    OrgLinkInput: "id label url",
    OrgAppScopeInput: "all appDids",
    OrgLink: "id label url",
    OrgAppScope: "all appDids",
    OrgSummary: "id did handle name avatar verifiedDomains",
    OrgApp: "appDid addedAt frozen",
    OrgTeam: "id orgId slug name appDids memberAddresses",
    OrgMembership: "orgId address role status appScope effectiveScope teams",
    Organization:
      "id did handle name bio website avatar links listed membersPublic archived takenDown createdBy createdAt updatedAt verifiedDomains notAffiliatedWith ownerCount memberCount securityWarnings apps teams viewerMembership",
    OrgMember:
      "id orgId address role status appScope hidden invitedBy invitedAt joinedAt endedAt updatedAt expiresAt teams handle displayName avatar",
    OrgAffiliation: "org role hidden",
    OrgSearchPage: "items next",
    OrgInvite: "id orgId org address role status invitedBy createdAt expiresAt",
    OrgInviteLink: "id orgId role maxUses uses expiresAt revoked createdBy createdAt",
    OrgInviteLinkCreated: "link token",
    OrgInviteLinkPreview: "org role expiresAt expired revoked full usable",
    OrgHandleAvailability: "handle available reason",
    OrgAuditEntry: "id orgId at actor action details",
    OrgAuditPage: "items next",
  },
  A2: {
    OrgProposalKind: "ADD_OWNER REMOVE_OWNER TRANSFER_APP_OUT CHANGE_HANDLE DELETE_ORG RECOVER FREEZE_APP UNFREEZE_APP",
    OrgProposalStatus: "PENDING EXECUTED CANCELLED EXPIRED FAILED",
    OrgDomainStatus: "PENDING VERIFIED LAPSED",
    OrgPartyKind: "PERSON ORG",
    AppTransferStatus: "PENDING ACCEPTED DECLINED CANCELLED EXPIRED",
    OrgCredentialReason: "ISSUER_NOT_MEMBER ISSUER_NOT_PUBLISHER SUSPENDED APP_FROZEN",
    OrgProposalPayloadInput: "address demoteToAdmin appDid toKind toId handle",
    OrgProposalPayload: "address demoteToAdmin appDid toKind toId handle domain",
    OrgProposal: "id orgId kind payload proposer approvals threshold status createdAt executableAt expiresAt executedAt reason",
    OrgDomain: "id orgId domain status verifiedAt lastCheckedAt failures createdAt txtName txtValue wellKnownUrl wellKnownJson",
    AppTransfer: "id appDid fromKind fromId toKind toId status createdAt expiresAt decidedAt decidedBy proposalId",
    OrgCredentialStatus: "credentialId appDid orgId counts reason",
    OrgCredential: "credentialId documentId appDid issuer issuerRole issuerStatus issuanceDate expirationDate revoked status",
    OrgWorkload: "did repository productionBranch ownerAddress disabled createdAt",
    ReleaseAttestation: "id appDid version artifactDigest orgId signer signerRole signerStillMember issuedAt signature createdAt",
    ReleaseAttestationPage: "items next",
    OrganizationStats: "orgId apps activeUsers30d activeDelegations attestations30d",
  },
  A3: {
    OrgNotificationKind:
      "INVITED INVITE_ACCEPTED ROLE_CHANGED REMOVED PROPOSAL_CREATED PROPOSAL_NEEDS_APPROVAL PROPOSAL_EXECUTED PROPOSAL_CANCELLED RECOVERY_STARTED APP_TRANSFER_REQUEST APP_TRANSFER_DONE APP_FROZEN DOMAIN_LAPSED DELEGATION_ADDED",
    OrgWebhookDeliveryStatus: "PENDING DELIVERED FAILED",
    OrgReportReason: "IMPERSONATION HANDLE_DISPUTE SPAM ABUSE OTHER",
    OrgReportStatus: "OPEN RESOLVED",
    OrgReportResolution: "DISMISS TAKE_DOWN RELEASE_HANDLE RESTORE",
    OrgOidcClientStatus: "ACTIVE DISABLED",
    OrgNotification: "id kind orgId org payload createdAt readAt",
    OrgNotificationPage: "items next",
    OrgWebhook: "id orgId url events enabled consecutiveFailures createdAt",
    OrgWebhookCreated: "webhook secret",
    OrgWebhookDelivery: "id webhookId eventId eventType status attempts responseStatus lastError createdAt nextAttemptAt deliveredAt",
    OrgOidcClient: "clientId orgId name redirectUris status createdAt",
    OrgOidcClientCredentials: "clientId clientSecret",
    OrgReport: "id orgId org reporter reason details status resolution resolvedBy resolvedAt note createdAt",
  },
};

function rootSignatures(built: GraphQLSchema, root: "Query" | "Mutation"): string[] {
  const type = built.getType(root);
  if (!isObjectType(type)) return [];
  return Object.values(type.getFields()).map((field) => {
    const args = field.args.map((arg) => `${arg.name}: ${String(arg.type)}`).join(", ");
    return `${field.name}${args ? `(${args})` : ""}: ${String(field.type)}`;
  });
}

function memberNames(built: GraphQLSchema, name: string): string | undefined {
  const type = built.getType(name);
  if (isEnumType(type)) return type.getValues().map((value) => value.name).join(" ");
  if (isObjectType(type) || isInputObjectType(type)) return Object.keys(type.getFields()).join(" ");
  return undefined;
}

describe("renown-orgs API contract", () => {
  const built = buildASTSchema(schema);

  for (const root of ["Query", "Mutation"] as const) {
    it(`serves every ${root} field of the shipped phases, and nothing outside the contract`, () => {
      const served = rootSignatures(built, root);
      for (const phase of SHIPPED) {
        for (const signature of ROOT_FIELDS[phase][root]) expect(served, phase).toContain(signature);
      }
      // A later phase grows task by task: what it already serves must match the contract too.
      const known = new Set(PHASES.flatMap((phase) => ROOT_FIELDS[phase][root]));
      for (const signature of served) expect(known.has(signature), signature).toBe(true);
    });
  }

  it("declares the types of the shipped phases (and any later-phase type already present) exactly", () => {
    for (const phase of PHASES) {
      for (const [name, members] of Object.entries(TYPES[phase])) {
        const actual = memberNames(built, name);
        if (actual === undefined && !SHIPPED.includes(phase)) continue;
        expect(actual, name).toBe(members);
      }
    }
  });

  it("declares no argument with a default value (signed messages hash the arguments as sent)", () => {
    for (const root of ["Query", "Mutation"] as const) {
      const type = built.getType(root);
      if (!isObjectType(type)) continue;
      for (const field of Object.values(type.getFields())) {
        for (const arg of field.args) expect(arg.defaultValue, `${field.name}.${arg.name}`).toBeUndefined();
      }
    }
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { OrgStore } from "../store/types.js";

/** Webhook event types (A3 delivers them). */
export const ORG_WEBHOOK_EVENTS = [
  "member.invited",
  "member.joined",
  "member.left",
  "member.removed",
  "member.role_changed",
  "team.changed",
  "app.transferred_in",
  "app.transferred_out",
  "app.frozen",
  "app.unfrozen",
  "credential.suspended",
  "proposal.created",
  "proposal.approved",
  "proposal.cancelled",
  "proposal.executed",
  "domain.verified",
  "domain.lapsed",
  "release.attested",
  "org.updated",
] as const;
export type OrgWebhookEventType = (typeof ORG_WEBHOOK_EVENTS)[number];

/** In-app notification kinds (A3 stores them). */
export const ORG_NOTIFICATION_KINDS = [
  "INVITED",
  "INVITE_ACCEPTED",
  "ROLE_CHANGED",
  "REMOVED",
  "PROPOSAL_CREATED",
  "PROPOSAL_NEEDS_APPROVAL",
  "PROPOSAL_EXECUTED",
  "PROPOSAL_CANCELLED",
  "RECOVERY_STARTED",
  "APP_TRANSFER_REQUEST",
  "APP_TRANSFER_DONE",
  "APP_FROZEN",
  "DOMAIN_LAPSED",
  "DELEGATION_ADDED",
] as const;
export type OrgNotificationKind = (typeof ORG_NOTIFICATION_KINDS)[number];

/** Who gets a notification: explicit lowercase addresses, or the org's active owners/admins (admins include owners). */
export interface OrgNotificationIntent {
  to: readonly string[] | "OWNERS" | "ADMINS";
  kind: OrgNotificationKind;
  payload?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

/** One successful org mutation or system action. */
export interface OrgActivity {
  orgId: string;
  /** Lowercase address, or "system". */
  actor: string;
  /** The GraphQL field, or system.<what> for sweeper actions. */
  action: string;
  at: Date;
  /** Audit details; never tokens, secrets or signatures. */
  details?: Record<string, unknown>;
  event?: { type: OrgWebhookEventType; data: Record<string, unknown> };
  notify?: readonly OrgNotificationIntent[];
}

export interface RecordedActivity extends OrgActivity {
  /** The audit row id, also the webhook event id. */
  id: string;
}

/** Receives every recorded activity after its audit row (A3: notifications, webhooks). */
export type ActivitySink = (activity: RecordedActivity) => Promise<void>;

/**
 * Writes the audit row, then hands the activity to each sink. Never throws:
 * the change it describes is already applied, so a failed audit or sink
 * write is logged and the mutation still succeeds.
 */
export function createActivityRecorder(deps: {
  store: () => OrgStore;
  newId: () => string;
  sinks: readonly ActivitySink[];
  log?: (message: string) => void;
}): (activity: OrgActivity) => Promise<void> {
  const log = deps.log ?? ((message: string) => console.error(message));
  return async (activity) => {
    const recorded: RecordedActivity = { ...activity, id: deps.newId() };
    try {
      await deps.store().appendAudit({
        id: recorded.id,
        orgId: activity.orgId,
        at: activity.at,
        actor: activity.actor,
        action: activity.action,
        details: activity.details ?? {},
      });
    } catch (error) {
      log(`[renown-orgs] audit write for ${activity.action} failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
    }
    for (const sink of deps.sinks) {
      try {
        await sink(recorded);
      } catch (error) {
        log(`[renown-orgs] activity sink for ${activity.action} failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
      }
    }
  };
}
```

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/core/authz.ts`:

```ts
import { roleAtLeast } from "../../../sdk/orgs.js";
import type { MemberRecord, OrgRecord, OrgRole, OrgStore, TeamRecord } from "../store/types.js";
import { forbidden, notFound } from "./errors.js";

export interface Scope {
  all: boolean;
  appDids: string[];
}

export function isAdminRole(role: OrgRole): boolean {
  return role === "OWNER" || role === "ADMIN";
}

/** The membership's own scope: owners and admins always act for every app. */
export function ownScope(member: MemberRecord): Scope {
  return { all: member.appScopeAll || isAdminRole(member.role), appDids: [...member.appDids] };
}

/** ownScope plus the app DIDs of every team the member belongs to. */
export function effectiveScope(member: MemberRecord, teams: readonly TeamRecord[]): Scope {
  const own = ownScope(member);
  const teamApps = teams.filter((team) => team.memberIds.includes(member.id)).flatMap((team) => team.appDids);
  return { all: own.all, appDids: [...new Set([...own.appDids, ...teamApps])] };
}

/** The organization; NOT_FOUND when unknown, FORBIDDEN for writes to an archived or taken-down one. */
export async function requireOrg(store: OrgStore, orgId: string, options: { writable?: boolean } = {}): Promise<OrgRecord> {
  const org = await store.organization(orgId);
  if (!org) throw notFound("Organization");
  if ((options.writable ?? true) && (org.archived || org.takenDown)) {
    throw forbidden("The organization is archived or taken down");
  }
  return org;
}

/** The caller's ACTIVE membership with at least `minRole`, else FORBIDDEN. */
export async function requireRole(store: OrgStore, orgId: string, address: string, minRole: OrgRole): Promise<MemberRecord> {
  const member = await store.openMember(orgId, address);
  if (!member || member.status !== "ACTIVE" || !roleAtLeast(member.role, minRole)) throw forbidden();
  return member;
}

/**
 * Publisher capability for `appDid`: the app belongs to the org, and
 * `address` is an ACTIVE member with at least PUBLISHER whose effective
 * scope covers it. Frozen apps are the caller's concern.
 */
export async function hasAppCapability(store: OrgStore, orgId: string, address: string, appDid: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const app = await store.orgOfApp(appDid);
  if (!app || app.orgId !== orgId) return false;
  const member = await store.openMember(orgId, address);
  if (!member || member.status !== "ACTIVE" || !roleAtLeast(member.role, "PUBLISHER")) return false;
  const scope = effectiveScope(member, await store.teams(orgId));
  return scope.all || scope.appDids.includes(appDid);
}
```

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/core/handles.ts`:

```ts
import type { GraphQLError } from "graphql";
import { handleProblem, normalizeHandle } from "../../renown-auth/core/handle.js";
import { findHandleOwner, type ReadModelDb } from "../../renown-auth/lookups.js";
import type { OrgStore } from "../store/types.js";
import { badInput, orgError, unavailable } from "./errors.js";

export { normalizeHandle };

/** Route words of renown.id that only organizations may not take (user handles keep renown-auth's list). */
export const ORG_RESERVED_HANDLES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
  "org", "orgs", "new", "invite", "invites", "notifications", "me", "verify", "people", "search",
]);

export type OrgHandleProblem = "INVALID" | "RESERVED" | "TAKEN";

/**
 * Why the normalized `handle` cannot be (or stay) the handle of `orgId`, or
 * null when it can. Users and organizations share one namespace.
 */
export async function orgHandleProblem(
  deps: { store: OrgStore; relationalDb: ReadModelDb; now: Date },
  handle: string,
  orgId?: string,
): Promise<OrgHandleProblem | null> {
  const problem = handleProblem(handle);
  if (problem) return problem;
  if (ORG_RESERVED_HANDLES.has(handle)) return "RESERVED";
  const holder = await deps.store.organizationByHandle(handle);
  if (holder) return holder.id === orgId ? null : "TAKEN";
  const reserved = await deps.store.reservedHandle(handle, deps.now);
  if (reserved && (orgId === undefined || reserved.orgId !== orgId)) return "RESERVED";
  let userDocument: string | undefined;
  try {
    userDocument = await findHandleOwner(deps.relationalDb, handle);
  } catch {
    throw unavailable("Handles cannot be checked right now");
  }
  return userDocument === undefined ? null : "TAKEN";
}

export function handleError(problem: OrgHandleProblem, handle: string): GraphQLError {
  if (problem === "INVALID") return badInput("handle", `"${handle}" is not a valid handle`);
  if (problem === "RESERVED") return orgError("HANDLE_RESERVED", `The handle "${handle}" is reserved`, "handle");
  return orgError("HANDLE_TAKEN", `The handle "${handle}" is taken`, "handle");
}
```

```ts
import { RenownUserProcessor } from "../../../processors/renown-user/index.js";
import type { DB as RenownUserDB } from "../../../processors/renown-user/schema.js";
import type { ReadModelDb } from "../../renown-auth/lookups.js";
import { contributorProfiles, type ContributorProfile } from "../../renown-stats/lookups.js";

/** The lowercase wallet behind a user handle (newest profile), or undefined. Throws when the read model is unavailable. */
export async function addressOfHandle(db: ReadModelDb, handle: string): Promise<string | undefined> {
  const row = await RenownUserProcessor.query<RenownUserDB>("renown-user", db)
    .selectFrom("renown_user")
    .select("eth_address")
    .where((eb) => eb(eb.fn("LOWER", ["renown_user.handle"]), "=", handle.toLowerCase()))
    .orderBy("renown_user.created_at", "desc")
    .orderBy("renown_user.document_id", "desc")
    .executeTakeFirst();
  return row?.eth_address ? row.eth_address.toLowerCase() : undefined;
}

/** Profile cards of lowercase addresses; empty when the read model cannot be read (members still list). */
export async function profilesOf(db: ReadModelDb, addresses: readonly string[]): Promise<Map<string, ContributorProfile>> {
  try {
    return await contributorProfiles(db, addresses);
  } catch {
    return new Map();
  }
}
```

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/runtime.ts`:

```ts
import type { IReactorClient } from "@powerhousedao/reactor";
import { generateId, type Action, type PHDocument } from "document-model";
import type { MediaBackend } from "../../media/backend.js";
import { mediaBackend } from "../../media/slot.js";
import { createRateLimiter } from "../renown-auth/core/rate-limit.js";
import type { ReadModelDb } from "../renown-auth/lookups.js";
import { createKeyedLock } from "../renown-stats/core/keyed-lock.js";
import { createActivityRecorder, type ActivitySink, type OrgActivity } from "./core/activity.js";
import { provenAddress, requireAddress, type OrgArgs, type OrgContext } from "./core/auth.js";
import type { OrgsConfig } from "./core/config.js";
import { badInput, rateLimited, unavailable } from "./core/errors.js";
import type { OrgStore } from "./store/types.js";

export type RateLimiter = { take(key: string, now?: number): boolean };
export type Resolver = (parent: unknown, args: never, ctx: OrgContext) => Promise<unknown>;
export type Area = (rt: OrgRuntime) => { Query?: Record<string, Resolver>; Mutation?: Record<string, Resolver> };

export const WRITES_PER_MINUTE = 60;
export const INVITES_PER_HOUR = 50;

export interface OrgsResolverDeps {
  reactorClient: Pick<IReactorClient, "createEmpty" | "execute" | "get">;
  relationalDb: ReadModelDb;
  /** Undefined until set up, or when the relational namespace is unavailable. */
  store(): OrgStore | undefined;
  config(): OrgsConfig;
  now?: () => Date;
  newId?: () => string;
  randomBytes?: (length: number) => Uint8Array;
  /** Where avatar refs are checked; defaults to the backend the media processor published. */
  media?: () => MediaBackend | null;
  writeLimiter?: RateLimiter;
  inviteLimiter?: RateLimiter;
  /** Extra activity sinks (A3: notifications, webhooks). */
  sinks?: readonly ActivitySink[];
  /** Initial membership-ended hooks (tests; A2 registers its own on the runtime). */
  membershipHooks?: readonly ((event: MembershipEnded) => Promise<void>)[];
}

/** A membership that just ended (LEFT or REMOVED); A2 suspends the member's org-app credentials. */
export interface MembershipEnded {
  orgId: string;
  memberId: string;
  address: string;
  reason: "LEFT" | "REMOVED";
  actor: string;
  at: Date;
}

export interface OrgRuntime {
  readonly deps: OrgsResolverDeps;
  now(): Date;
  /** The store, or SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE. */
  store(): OrgStore;
  config(): OrgsConfig;
  media(): MediaBackend | null;
  newId(): string;
  /** `bytes` random bytes, base64url. */
  token(bytes?: number): string;
  lock<T>(key: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
  /** The proven caller of a write; spends one of their 60 writes a minute. */
  writer(ctx: OrgContext, field: string, args: OrgArgs): Promise<string>;
  /** The proven caller of a read that needs one. */
  caller(ctx: OrgContext, field: string, args: OrgArgs): Promise<string>;
  /** The caller when they prove an address, else null (a wrong auth is still FORBIDDEN). */
  viewer(ctx: OrgContext, field: string, args: OrgArgs): Promise<string | null>;
  /** Spends one of the org's 50 invites an hour, or RATE_LIMITED. */
  takeInvite(orgId: string): void;
  createDocument(documentType: string): Promise<string>;
  /** Applies `actions` to a document; a rejected operation is BAD_USER_INPUT (field "document"). */
  execute(documentId: string, actions: Action[]): Promise<void>;
  record(activity: OrgActivity): Promise<void>;
  readonly hooks: { membershipEnded: ((event: MembershipEnded) => Promise<void>)[] };
}

export function createRuntime(deps: OrgsResolverDeps): OrgRuntime {
  const now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date());
  const newId = deps.newId ?? generateId;
  const randomBytes = deps.randomBytes ?? ((length: number) => crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(length)));
  const writeLimiter = deps.writeLimiter ?? createRateLimiter(WRITES_PER_MINUTE, 60_000);
  const inviteLimiter = deps.inviteLimiter ?? createRateLimiter(INVITES_PER_HOUR, 3_600_000);
  const lock = createKeyedLock();
  const store = (): OrgStore => {
    const current = deps.store();
    if (!current) throw unavailable();
    return current;
  };
  const record = createActivityRecorder({ store, newId, sinks: deps.sinks ?? [] });

  return {
    deps,
    now,
    store,
    config: () => deps.config(),
    media: () => (deps.media ?? mediaBackend)(),
    newId,
    token: (bytes = 32) => Buffer.from(randomBytes(bytes)).toString("base64url"),
    lock,
    async writer(ctx, field, args) {
      const address = await requireAddress(ctx, field, args, now());
      if (!writeLimiter.take(address, now().getTime())) throw rateLimited();
      return address;
    },
    caller: (ctx, field, args) => requireAddress(ctx, field, args, now()),
    viewer: (ctx, field, args) => provenAddress(ctx, field, args, now()),
    takeInvite(orgId) {
      if (!inviteLimiter.take(orgId, now().getTime())) throw rateLimited();
    },
    async createDocument(documentType) {
      return (await deps.reactorClient.createEmpty(documentType)).header.id;
    },
    async execute(documentId, actions) {
      if (actions.length === 0) return;
      const document: PHDocument = await deps.reactorClient.execute(documentId, "main", actions);
      const sent = new Set(actions.map((action) => action.id));
      const failed = Object.values(document.operations)
        .flat()
        .find((operation) => operation.error && sent.has(operation.action.id));
      if (failed?.error) throw badInput("document", `${failed.action.type} failed: ${failed.error}`);
    },
    record,
    hooks: { membershipEnded: [...(deps.membershipHooks ?? [])] },
  };
}
```

```ts
import { effectiveScope, ownScope, type Scope } from "./core/authz.js";
import { orgDid } from "./core/config.js";
import { profilesOf } from "./core/people.js";
import type { OrgRuntime } from "./runtime.js";
import type { InviteLinkRecord, MemberRecord, OrgLink, OrgRecord, OrgRole, StoredMemberStatus, TeamRecord } from "./store/types.js";

/** Direct invites expire 14 days after they were sent. */
export const INVITE_TTL_MS = 14 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

export type MemberStatusOutput = StoredMemberStatus | "EXPIRED";

export interface OrgSummaryOutput {
  id: string;
  did: string;
  handle: string;
  name: string;
  avatar: string | null;
  verifiedDomains: string[];
}

export interface OrgAppOutput {
  appDid: string;
  addedAt: string;
  frozen: boolean;
}

export interface OrgTeamOutput {
  id: string;
  orgId: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  appDids: string[];
  memberAddresses: string[];
}

export interface OrgMembershipOutput {
  orgId: string;
  address: string;
  role: OrgRole;
  status: MemberStatusOutput;
  appScope: Scope;
  effectiveScope: Scope;
  teams: string[];
}

export interface OrganizationOutput {
  id: string;
  did: string;
  handle: string;
  name: string;
  bio: string | null;
  website: string | null;
  avatar: string | null;
  links: OrgLink[];
  listed: boolean;
  membersPublic: boolean;
  archived: boolean;
  takenDown: boolean;
  createdBy: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  verifiedDomains: string[];
  notAffiliatedWith: OrgSummaryOutput | null;
  ownerCount: number;
  memberCount: number;
  securityWarnings: "SINGLE_OWNER"[];
  apps: OrgAppOutput[];
  teams: OrgTeamOutput[];
  viewerMembership: OrgMembershipOutput | null;
}

export interface OrgMemberOutput {
  id: string;
  orgId: string;
  address: string;
  role: OrgRole;
  status: MemberStatusOutput;
  appScope: Scope;
  hidden: boolean;
  invitedBy: string | null;
  invitedAt: string;
  joinedAt: string | null;
  endedAt: string | null;
  updatedAt: string;
  expiresAt: string | null;
  teams: string[];
  handle: string | null;
  displayName: string | null;
  avatar: string | null;
}

export interface OrgInviteOutput {
  id: string;
  orgId: string;
  org: OrgSummaryOutput;
  address: string;
  role: OrgRole;
  status: MemberStatusOutput;
  invitedBy: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  expiresAt: string;
}

export interface OrgInviteLinkOutput {
  id: string;
  orgId: string;
  role: OrgRole;
  maxUses: number;
  uses: number;
  expiresAt: string;
  revoked: boolean;
  createdBy: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export function iso(value: Date): string {
  return value.toISOString();
}

export function isoOrNull(value: Date | null): string | null {
  return value ? value.toISOString() : null;
}

export function inviteExpiresAt(member: MemberRecord): Date {
  return new Date(member.invitedAt.getTime() + INVITE_TTL_MS);
}

export function isExpiredInvite(member: MemberRecord, now: Date): boolean {
  return member.status === "INVITED" && inviteExpiresAt(member).getTime() <= now.getTime();
}

export function memberStatus(member: MemberRecord, now: Date): MemberStatusOutput {
  return isExpiredInvite(member, now) ? "EXPIRED" : member.status;
}

export function summaryOf(rt: OrgRuntime, org: OrgRecord, verifiedDomains: string[]): OrgSummaryOutput {
  return {
    id: org.id,
    did: orgDid(rt.config().didHost, org.id),
    handle: org.handle,
    name: org.name,
    avatar: org.takenDown ? null : org.avatarRef,
    verifiedDomains: org.takenDown ? [] : verifiedDomains,
  };
}

export async function orgSummaries(rt: OrgRuntime, orgs: readonly OrgRecord[]): Promise<Map<string, OrgSummaryOutput>> {
  const domains = await rt.store().verifiedDomains(orgs.map((org) => org.id));
  return new Map(orgs.map((org) => [org.id, summaryOf(rt, org, domains.get(org.id) ?? [])]));
}

export function teamOutput(team: TeamRecord, members: readonly MemberRecord[]): OrgTeamOutput {
  const addresses = new Map(members.map((member) => [member.id, member.address]));
  return {
    id: team.id,
    orgId: team.orgId,
    slug: team.slug,
    name: team.name,
    appDids: [...team.appDids],
    memberAddresses: team.memberIds.flatMap((id) => {
      const address = addresses.get(id);
      return address ? [address] : [];
    }),
  };
}

export function membershipOutput(member: MemberRecord, teams: readonly TeamRecord[], now: Date): OrgMembershipOutput {
  return {
    orgId: member.orgId,
    address: member.address,
    role: member.role,
    status: memberStatus(member, now),
    appScope: ownScope(member),
    effectiveScope: effectiveScope(member, teams),
    teams: teams.filter((team) => team.memberIds.includes(member.id)).map((team) => team.slug),
  };
}

/** An organization as GraphQL returns it to `viewer` (lowercase address or null). */
export async function organizationOutput(rt: OrgRuntime, org: OrgRecord, viewer: string | null): Promise<OrganizationOutput> {
  const base = {
    id: org.id,
    did: orgDid(rt.config().didHost, org.id),
    handle: org.handle,
    name: org.name,
    listed: org.listed,
    membersPublic: org.membersPublic,
    archived: org.archived,
    takenDown: org.takenDown,
    createdBy: org.createdBy,
    createdAt: iso(org.createdAt),
    updatedAt: iso(org.updatedAt),
  };
  if (org.takenDown) {
    return {
      ...base,
      bio: null,
      website: null,
      avatar: null,
      links: [],
      verifiedDomains: [],
      notAffiliatedWith: null,
      ownerCount: 0,
      memberCount: 0,
      securityWarnings: [],
      apps: [],
      teams: [],
      viewerMembership: null,
    };
  }
  const store = rt.store();
  const [domains, active, apps] = await Promise.all([
    store.verifiedDomains([org.id]),
    store.members(org.id, ["ACTIVE"]),
    store.orgApps(org.id),
  ]);
  const verifiedDomains = domains.get(org.id) ?? [];
  const lookalike = verifiedDomains.length === 0 ? await store.verifiedOrgNamed(org.name, org.id) : undefined;
  const notAffiliatedWith = lookalike ? (await orgSummaries(rt, [lookalike])).get(lookalike.id) ?? null : null;
  const ownerCount = active.filter((member) => member.role === "OWNER").length;
  const mine = viewer ? active.find((member) => member.address === viewer) : undefined;
  const teams = mine ? await store.teams(org.id) : [];
  return {
    ...base,
    bio: org.bio,
    website: org.website,
    avatar: org.avatarRef,
    links: org.links,
    verifiedDomains,
    notAffiliatedWith,
    ownerCount,
    memberCount: active.length,
    securityWarnings: ownerCount === 1 ? ["SINGLE_OWNER"] : [],
    apps: apps.map((app) => ({ appDid: app.appDid, addedAt: iso(app.addedAt), frozen: app.frozen })),
    teams: teams.map((team) => teamOutput(team, active)),
    viewerMembership: mine ? membershipOutput(mine, teams, rt.now()) : null,
  };
}

/** Member rows with their team slugs and profile cards. */
export async function memberOutputs(
  rt: OrgRuntime,
  members: readonly MemberRecord[],
  teams: readonly TeamRecord[],
): Promise<OrgMemberOutput[]> {
  const profiles = await profilesOf(rt.deps.relationalDb, [...new Set(members.map((member) => member.address))]);
  const now = rt.now();
  return members.map((member) => {
    const profile = profiles.get(member.address);
    const status = memberStatus(member, now);
    return {
      id: member.id,
      orgId: member.orgId,
      address: member.address,
      role: member.role,
      status,
      appScope: ownScope(member),
      hidden: member.hidden,
      invitedBy: member.invitedBy,
      invitedAt: iso(member.invitedAt),
      joinedAt: isoOrNull(member.joinedAt),
      endedAt: isoOrNull(member.endedAt),
      updatedAt: iso(member.updatedAt),
      expiresAt: status === "INVITED" || status === "EXPIRED" ? iso(inviteExpiresAt(member)) : null,
      teams: teams.filter((team) => team.memberIds.includes(member.id)).map((team) => team.slug),
      handle: profile?.handle ?? null,
      displayName: profile?.displayName ?? null,
      avatar: profile?.avatar ?? null,
    };
  });
}

export function inviteOutput(member: MemberRecord, org: OrgSummaryOutput, now: Date): OrgInviteOutput {
  return {
    id: member.id,
    orgId: member.orgId,
    org,
    address: member.address,
    role: member.role,
    status: memberStatus(member, now),
    invitedBy: member.invitedBy,
    createdAt: iso(member.invitedAt),
    expiresAt: iso(inviteExpiresAt(member)),
  };
}

export function inviteLinkOutput(link: InviteLinkRecord): OrgInviteLinkOutput {
  return {
    id: link.id,
    orgId: link.orgId,
    role: link.role,
    maxUses: link.maxUses,
    uses: link.uses,
    expiresAt: iso(link.expiresAt),
    revoked: link.revoked,
    createdBy: link.createdBy,
    createdAt: iso(link.createdAt),
  };
}
```
