- [ ] **Step 6: Write the organizations area, the resolver assembly and the subgraph**

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/areas/organizations.ts`:

```ts
import {
  actions as orgActions,
  isOrgAvatarRef,
  isOrgLinkLabel,
  isOrgLinkUrl,
  isOrgName,
  isOrgWebsite,
  MAX_ORG_BIO_LENGTH,
  MAX_ORG_LINKS,
  renownOrganizationDocumentType,
} from "../../../document-models/renown-organization/index.js";
import { avatarProblem } from "../../../media/avatar.js";
import { likePrefix } from "../../renown-read-model/search.js";
import type { OrgArgs, OrgContext } from "../core/auth.js";
import { requireOrg, requireRole } from "../core/authz.js";
import { badInput, limitReached, unavailable } from "../core/errors.js";
import { handleError, normalizeHandle, orgHandleProblem } from "../core/handles.js";
import { iso, isExpiredInvite, organizationOutput, type OrganizationOutput } from "../output.js";
import type { Area, OrgRuntime } from "../runtime.js";
import type { MemberRecord, OrgLink, OrgPatch, OrgRecord, OrgSearch, OrgStore } from "../store/types.js";

export const MAX_ORGS_PER_CREATOR = 20;
export const MAX_MEMBERSHIPS_PER_WALLET = 50;
const SEARCH_MIN = 2;
const SEARCH_MAX = 40;
const SEARCH_DEFAULT_LIMIT = 20;
const SEARCH_MAX_LIMIT = 50;
const AUDIT_DEFAULT_LIMIT = 50;
const AUDIT_MAX_LIMIT = 100;
const NAME_RULE = "name must be 1-64 characters";

interface LinkInput {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  url: string;
}

interface ProfileArgs {
  name?: string | null;
  bio?: string | null;
  website?: string | null;
  avatar?: string | null;
  links?: LinkInput[] | null;
}

/** undefined = keep, null = clear, string = set (trimmed). */
export interface ProfilePatch {
  name?: string;
  bio?: string | null;
  website?: string | null;
  avatar?: string | null;
  links?: OrgLink[];
}

function clearable(value: string | null | undefined): string | null | undefined {
  if (value === undefined || value === null) return undefined;
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  return trimmed === "" ? null : trimmed;
}

/** Validates profile arguments into a patch; BAD_USER_INPUT names the field. */
export function validateProfile(args: ProfileArgs): ProfilePatch {
  const patch: ProfilePatch = {};
  if (args.name != null) {
    const name = args.name.trim();
    if (!isOrgName(name)) throw badInput("name", NAME_RULE);
    patch.name = name;
  }
  const bio = clearable(args.bio);
  if (bio && bio.length > MAX_ORG_BIO_LENGTH) throw badInput("bio", `bio must be at most ${MAX_ORG_BIO_LENGTH} characters`);
  if (bio !== undefined) patch.bio = bio;
  const website = clearable(args.website);
  if (website && !isOrgWebsite(website)) throw badInput("website", "website must be an http(s) URL");
  if (website !== undefined) patch.website = website;
  const avatar = clearable(args.avatar);
  if (avatar && !isOrgAvatarRef(avatar)) throw badInput("avatar", "avatar must be an attachment://v1:<sha256> reference");
  if (avatar !== undefined) patch.avatar = avatar;
  if (args.links != null) {
    if (args.links.length > MAX_ORG_LINKS) throw badInput("links", `at most ${MAX_ORG_LINKS} links`);
    const ids = new Set<string>();
    patch.links = args.links.map((raw) => {
      const link = { id: raw.id.trim(), label: raw.label.trim(), url: raw.url.trim() };
      if (link.id === "" || ids.has(link.id)) throw badInput("links", "link ids must be unique and non-empty");
      ids.add(link.id);
      if (!isOrgLinkLabel(link.label) || !isOrgLinkUrl(link.url)) {
        throw badInput("links", "each link needs a 1-40 character label and an http(s) URL");
      }
      return link;
    });
  }
  return patch;
}

/** The SET_ORG_PROFILE input for a patch ("" clears in the reducer), or null when nothing changes. */
function profileAction(patch: ProfilePatch) {
  const input = {
    ...(patch.name !== undefined && { name: patch.name }),
    ...(patch.bio !== undefined && { bio: patch.bio ?? "" }),
    ...(patch.website !== undefined && { website: patch.website ?? "" }),
    ...(patch.avatar !== undefined && { avatar: patch.avatar ?? "" }),
    ...(patch.links !== undefined && { links: patch.links }),
  };
  return Object.keys(input).length > 0 ? orgActions.setOrgProfile(input) : null;
}

/** ACTIVE memberships and unexpired invites of an address. */
export async function openMemberships(store: OrgStore, address: string, now: Date): Promise<MemberRecord[]> {
  const open = await store.membershipsOf(address, ["INVITED", "ACTIVE"]);
  return open.filter((member) => !isExpiredInvite(member, now));
}

function parseSearch(args: { query?: string | null; limit?: number | null; after?: string | null }): OrgSearch {
  const query = (args.query ?? "").trim();
  let pattern: string | null = null;
  if (query !== "") {
    const length = Array.from(query).length;
    if (query.includes("\u0000") || length < SEARCH_MIN || length > SEARCH_MAX) {
      throw badInput("query", `query must be ${SEARCH_MIN}-${SEARCH_MAX} characters`);
    }
    pattern = likePrefix(query);
  }
  const limit = args.limit ?? SEARCH_DEFAULT_LIMIT;
  if (!Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > SEARCH_MAX_LIMIT) {
    throw badInput("limit", `limit must be an integer from 1 to ${SEARCH_MAX_LIMIT}`);
  }
  if (args.after == null) return { pattern, limit };
  try {
    const cursor = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(args.after, "base64url").toString("utf8")) as { h?: unknown; i?: unknown };
    if (typeof cursor.h !== "string" || typeof cursor.i !== "string" || cursor.h.length > 30 || cursor.i.length > 255) {
      throw new Error("malformed cursor");
    }
    return { pattern, limit, after: { handle: cursor.h, id: cursor.i } };
  } catch {
    throw badInput("after", "after is not a cursor from organizationSearch");
  }
}

function auditCursor(raw: string): { at: Date; id: string } {
  try {
    const cursor = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(raw, "base64url").toString("utf8")) as { a?: unknown; i?: unknown };
    const at = typeof cursor.a === "string" ? new Date(cursor.a) : new Date(Number.NaN);
    if (Number.isNaN(at.getTime()) || typeof cursor.i !== "string") throw new Error("malformed cursor");
    return { at, id: cursor.i };
  } catch {
    throw badInput("after", "after is not a cursor from orgAuditLog");
  }
}

interface CreateArgs extends OrgArgs {
  handle: string;
  name: string;
  bio?: string | null;
  website?: string | null;
  links?: LinkInput[] | null;
  listed?: boolean | null;
  membersPublic?: boolean | null;
}

interface UpdateArgs extends OrgArgs {
  orgId: string;
  name?: string | null;
  bio?: string | null;
  website?: string | null;
  avatar?: string | null;
  links?: LinkInput[] | null;
  membersPublic?: boolean | null;
}

async function assertAvatar(rt: OrgRuntime, ref: string): Promise<void> {
  const backend = rt.media();
  if (!backend) throw unavailable("Avatar uploads are not available");
  let problem: string | null;
  try {
    problem = await avatarProblem(ref, backend);
  } catch {
    throw unavailable("Avatar storage is unavailable");
  }
  if (problem) throw badInput("avatar", `Invalid avatar: ${problem}`);
}

export const organizationResolvers: Area = (rt) => ({
  Query: {
    organization: async (_: unknown, args: OrgArgs & { id?: string | null; handle?: string | null }, ctx: OrgContext) => {
      if ((args.id == null) === (args.handle == null)) throw badInput("id", "pass exactly one of id and handle");
      const viewer = await rt.viewer(ctx, "organization", args);
      const store = rt.store();
      const org = args.id != null ? await store.organization(args.id) : await store.organizationByHandle(normalizeHandle(args.handle ?? ""));
      return org ? organizationOutput(rt, org, viewer) : null;
    },
    organizationSearch: async (_: unknown, args: { query?: string | null; limit?: number | null; after?: string | null }) => {
      const search = parseSearch(args);
      const rows = await rt.store().searchOrganizations(search);
      const page = rows.slice(0, search.limit);
      const last = page.at(-1);
      const next =
        rows.length > search.limit && last
          ? Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ h: last.handle.toLowerCase(), i: last.id })).toString("base64url")
          : null;
      return { items: await Promise.all(page.map((org) => organizationOutput(rt, org, null))), next };
    },
    orgHandleAvailability: async (_: unknown, args: { handle: string }) => {
      const handle = normalizeHandle(args.handle);
      const reason = await orgHandleProblem({ store: rt.store(), relationalDb: rt.deps.relationalDb, now: rt.now() }, handle);
      return { handle, available: reason === null, reason };
    },
    orgAuditLog: async (_: unknown, args: OrgArgs & { orgId: string; limit?: number | null; after?: string | null }, ctx: OrgContext) => {
      const caller = await rt.caller(ctx, "orgAuditLog", args);
      const store = rt.store();
      await requireOrg(store, args.orgId, { writable: false });
      await requireRole(store, args.orgId, caller, "ADMIN");
      const limit = args.limit ?? AUDIT_DEFAULT_LIMIT;
      if (!Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > AUDIT_MAX_LIMIT) {
        throw badInput("limit", `limit must be an integer from 1 to ${AUDIT_MAX_LIMIT}`);
      }
      const rows = await store.auditPage(args.orgId, limit + 1, args.after != null ? auditCursor(args.after) : undefined);
      const page = rows.slice(0, limit);
      const last = page.at(-1);
      return {
        items: page.map((entry) => ({ ...entry, at: iso(entry.at), details: JSON.stringify(entry.details) })),
        next:
          rows.length > limit && last
            ? Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ a: iso(last.at), i: last.id })).toString("base64url")
            : null,
      };
    },
  },
  Mutation: {
    createOrganization: async (_: unknown, args: CreateArgs, ctx: OrgContext): Promise<OrganizationOutput> => {
      const caller = await rt.writer(ctx, "createOrganization", args);
      const store = rt.store();
      const handle = normalizeHandle(args.handle);
      const { name, ...profile } = validateProfile({ name: args.name, bio: args.bio, website: args.website, links: args.links });
      if (name === undefined) throw badInput("name", NAME_RULE);
      if ((await store.countOrganizationsCreatedBy(caller)) >= MAX_ORGS_PER_CREATOR) {
        throw limitReached(`A wallet creates at most ${MAX_ORGS_PER_CREATOR} organizations`);
      }
      if ((await openMemberships(store, caller, rt.now())).length >= MAX_MEMBERSHIPS_PER_WALLET) {
        throw limitReached(`A wallet holds at most ${MAX_MEMBERSHIPS_PER_WALLET} memberships`);
      }
      return rt.lock(`handle:${handle}`, async () => {
        const now = rt.now();
        const problem = await orgHandleProblem({ store, relationalDb: rt.deps.relationalDb, now }, handle);
        if (problem) throw handleError(problem, handle);
        const orgId = await rt.createDocument(renownOrganizationDocumentType);
        const memberId = rt.newId();
        const at = iso(now);
        const setProfile = profileAction(profile);
        await rt.execute(orgId, [
          orgActions.initOrganization({ handle, name, createdBy: caller }),
          ...(setProfile ? [setProfile] : []),
          ...(args.listed ? [orgActions.setOrgListed({ listed: true })] : []),
          ...(args.membersPublic ? [orgActions.setMembersPublic({ membersPublic: true })] : []),
          orgActions.inviteMember({ id: memberId, address: caller, role: "OWNER", invitedAt: at }),
          orgActions.setMemberStatus({ id: memberId, status: "ACTIVE", at }),
        ]);
        const org: OrgRecord = {
          id: orgId,
          handle,
          name,
          bio: profile.bio ?? null,
          website: profile.website ?? null,
          avatarRef: null,
          links: profile.links ?? [],
          listed: args.listed === true,
          membersPublic: args.membersPublic === true,
          archived: false,
          takenDown: false,
          createdBy: caller,
          createdAt: now,
          updatedAt: now,
        };
        if (!(await store.insertOrganization(org))) throw handleError("TAKEN", handle);
        await store.insertMember({
          id: memberId,
          orgId,
          address: caller,
          role: "OWNER",
          status: "ACTIVE",
          appScopeAll: true,
          appDids: [],
          hidden: false,
          invitedBy: caller,
          invitedAt: now,
          joinedAt: now,
          endedAt: null,
          updatedAt: now,
        });
        await rt.record({ orgId, actor: caller, action: "createOrganization", at: now, details: { handle, name } });
        return organizationOutput(rt, org, caller);
      });
    },
    updateOrganization: async (_: unknown, args: UpdateArgs, ctx: OrgContext): Promise<OrganizationOutput> => {
      const caller = await rt.writer(ctx, "updateOrganization", args);
      const patch = validateProfile(args);
      if (patch.avatar) await assertAvatar(rt, patch.avatar);
      return rt.lock(`org:${args.orgId}`, async () => {
        const store = rt.store();
        await requireOrg(store, args.orgId);
        await requireRole(store, args.orgId, caller, "ADMIN");
        const now = rt.now();
        const setProfile = profileAction(patch);
        const membersPublic = args.membersPublic ?? undefined;
        await rt.execute(args.orgId, [
          ...(setProfile ? [setProfile] : []),
          ...(membersPublic !== undefined ? [orgActions.setMembersPublic({ membersPublic })] : []),
        ]);
        const changes: OrgPatch = {
          ...(patch.name !== undefined && { name: patch.name }),
          ...(patch.bio !== undefined && { bio: patch.bio }),
          ...(patch.website !== undefined && { website: patch.website }),
          ...(patch.avatar !== undefined && { avatarRef: patch.avatar }),
          ...(patch.links !== undefined && { links: patch.links }),
          ...(membersPublic !== undefined && { membersPublic }),
        };
        const org = await store.updateOrganization(args.orgId, changes, now);
        if (!org) throw unavailable();
        await rt.record({
          orgId: org.id,
          actor: caller,
          action: "updateOrganization",
          at: now,
          details: { fields: Object.keys(changes) },
          event: { type: "org.updated", data: { fields: Object.keys(changes) } },
        });
        return organizationOutput(rt, org, caller);
      });
    },
    setOrgListed: async (_: unknown, args: OrgArgs & { orgId: string; listed: boolean }, ctx: OrgContext) => {
      const caller = await rt.writer(ctx, "setOrgListed", args);
      return rt.lock(`org:${args.orgId}`, async () => {
        const store = rt.store();
        await requireOrg(store, args.orgId);
        await requireRole(store, args.orgId, caller, "ADMIN");
        const now = rt.now();
        await rt.execute(args.orgId, [orgActions.setOrgListed({ listed: args.listed })]);
        const org = await store.updateOrganization(args.orgId, { listed: args.listed }, now);
        if (!org) throw unavailable();
        await rt.record({
          orgId: org.id,
          actor: caller,
          action: "setOrgListed",
          at: now,
          details: { listed: args.listed },
          event: { type: "org.updated", data: { fields: ["listed"] } },
        });
        return organizationOutput(rt, org, caller);
      });
    },
  },
});
```

Replace `subgraphs/renown-orgs/resolvers.ts`:

```ts
import { organizationResolvers } from "./areas/organizations.js";
import { createRuntime, type Area, type OrgsResolverDeps, type Resolver } from "./runtime.js";

/** The resolver areas of renown-orgs; each later task appends its own. */
const AREAS: readonly Area[] = [organizationResolvers];

/** All renown-orgs resolvers over one runtime (one lock map, one set of rate limiters). */
export function createResolvers(deps: OrgsResolverDeps): Record<string, unknown> {
  const rt = createRuntime(deps);
  const Query: Record<string, Resolver> = {};
  const Mutation: Record<string, Resolver> = {};
  for (const area of AREAS) {
    const built = area(rt);
    for (const [target, fields] of [[Query, built.Query], [Mutation, built.Mutation]] as const) {
      for (const [name, resolver] of Object.entries(fields ?? {})) {
        if (name in target) throw new Error(`renown-orgs: ${name} is defined twice`);
        target[name] = resolver;
      }
    }
  }
  return { Query, Mutation };
}
```

Replace `subgraphs/renown-orgs/index.ts`:

```ts
import { BaseSubgraph } from "@powerhousedao/reactor-api";
import type { DocumentNode } from "graphql";
import type { ReadModelDb } from "../renown-auth/lookups.js";
import { loadOrgsConfig, type OrgsConfig } from "./core/config.js";
import { createResolvers } from "./resolvers.js";
import { schema } from "./schema.js";
import { KyselyOrgStore } from "./store/kysely.js";
import { migrate } from "./store/migrations.js";
import { ORGS_NAMESPACE, type OrgsKysely } from "./store/types.js";

/**
 * Renown organisations. Every write goes to the organization document (the
 * signed record) through the in-process reactor client and to the
 * `renown-orgs` relational namespace (the source of truth for reads), under
 * one lock per organization; every field authorises its caller itself.
 *
 * Never fails the host: without its namespace every field answers
 * SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE.
 */
export class RenownOrgsSubgraph extends BaseSubgraph {
  #store: KyselyOrgStore | undefined;
  #config: OrgsConfig | undefined;
  #setUp = false;

  name = "renown-orgs";
  typeDefs: DocumentNode = schema;
  resolvers: Record<string, unknown> = createResolvers({
    reactorClient: this.reactorClient,
    relationalDb: this.relationalDb as unknown as ReadModelDb,
    store: () => this.#store,
    config: () => this.#getConfig(),
  });
  additionalContextFields = {};

  #getConfig(): OrgsConfig {
    return (this.#config ??= loadOrgsConfig(process.env));
  }

  async onSetup() {
    // Idempotent: a second setup must not migrate twice.
    if (this.#setUp) return;
    this.#setUp = true;
    // Never throw from here, or the host's other subgraphs go down with this one.
    try {
      const db = (await this.relationalDb.createNamespace(ORGS_NAMESPACE)) as unknown as OrgsKysely;
      await migrate(db);
      this.#store = new KyselyOrgStore(db);
    } catch (error) {
      const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "unknown error";
      console.error(`[renown-orgs] relational namespace/migration failed (${reason}) — organizations disabled`);
    }
  }

  onDisconnect(): Promise<void> {
    this.#store = undefined;
    this.#setUp = false;
    return Promise.resolve();
  }
}
```

```ts
import { PGlite } from "@electric-sql/pglite";
import type { IRelationalDb } from "@powerhousedao/reactor-browser";
import type { Action, PHDocument } from "document-model";
import { GraphQLError } from "graphql";
import { Kysely, sql } from "kysely";
import { PGliteDialect } from "kysely-pglite-dialect";
import { privateKeyToAccount, type PrivateKeyAccount } from "viem/accounts";
import { expect, vi } from "vitest";
import {
  reducer as profileReducer,
  renownAppProfileDocumentType,
  utils as profileUtils,
} from "../../../document-models/renown-app-profile/index.js";
import {
  reducer as orgReducer,
  renownOrganizationDocumentType,
  utils as orgUtils,
} from "../../../document-models/renown-organization/index.js";
import type { MediaBackend } from "../../../media/backend.js";
import { RenownCredentialProcessor } from "../../../processors/renown-credential/index.js";
import { up as upCredential } from "../../../processors/renown-credential/migrations.js";
import { RenownUserProcessor } from "../../../processors/renown-user/index.js";
import { up as upUser } from "../../../processors/renown-user/migrations.js";
import { orgMessage } from "../../../sdk/orgs.js";
import type { OrgAuthInput } from "../core/auth.js";
import type { OrgsConfig } from "../core/config.js";
import type { OrganizationOutput } from "../output.js";
import { createResolvers } from "../resolvers.js";
import type { OrgsResolverDeps } from "../runtime.js";
import { KyselyOrgStore } from "../store/kysely.js";
import { migrate } from "../store/migrations.js";
import { ORGS_NAMESPACE } from "../store/types.js";

// Hardhat's well-known test keys.
export const ALICE = privateKeyToAccount("0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80");
export const BOB = privateKeyToAccount("0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d");
export const CAROL = privateKeyToAccount("0x5de4111afa1a4b94908f83103eb1f1706367c2e68ca870fc3fb9a804cdab365a");
export const DAVE = privateKeyToAccount("0x7c852118294e51e653712a81e05800f419141751be58f605c371e15141b007a6");
export const EVE = privateKeyToAccount("0x47e179ec197488593b187f80a00eb0da91f1b9d0b13f8733639f19c30a34926a");
export const lower = (account: { address: string }) => account.address.toLowerCase();

export const T0 = new Date("2026-10-12T12:00:00.000Z");
export const USER_NS = RenownUserProcessor.getNamespace("renown-user");
export const CRED_NS = RenownCredentialProcessor.getNamespace("renown-credential");
export const TEST_CONFIG: OrgsConfig = {
  didHost: "renown.test",
  timelockSeconds: 3600,
  recoverySeconds: 7200,
  platformAdmins: new Set([lower(EVE)]),
};

export interface Harness {
  root: Kysely<any>;
  relationalDb: IRelationalDb<unknown>;
}

/** One PGlite with every namespace renown-orgs reads: its own, the user and credential read models. */
export async function openHarness(): Promise<Harness> {
  const root = new Kysely<any>({ dialect: new PGliteDialect(new PGlite()) });
  await sql`create schema ${sql.id(ORGS_NAMESPACE)}`.execute(root);
  await migrate(root.withSchema(ORGS_NAMESPACE));
  await sql`create schema ${sql.id(USER_NS)}`.execute(root);
  await upUser(root.withSchema(USER_NS) as never);
  await sql`create schema ${sql.id(CRED_NS)}`.execute(root);
  await upCredential(root.withSchema(CRED_NS) as never);
  const relationalDb = { queryNamespace: (namespace: string) => root.withSchema(namespace) } as unknown as IRelationalDb<unknown>;
  return { root, relationalDb };
}

/** Empties every table of every namespace the harness created (later phases add tables; this follows). */
export async function resetHarness(h: Harness): Promise<void> {
  for (const schema of [ORGS_NAMESPACE, USER_NS, CRED_NS]) {
    const { rows } = await sql<{ table_name: string }>`
      select table_name from information_schema.tables where table_schema = ${schema}`.execute(h.root);
    for (const { table_name } of rows) await h.root.withSchema(schema).deleteFrom(table_name).execute();
  }
}

/** A Renown profile row, as the renown-user processor writes it. */
export async function insertUser(h: Harness, row: { address: string; handle?: string | null; documentId: string; displayName?: string | null }): Promise<void> {
  await h.root
    .withSchema(USER_NS)
    .insertInto("renown_user")
    .values({
      document_id: row.documentId,
      eth_address: row.address,
      username: null,
      user_image: null,
      display_name: row.displayName ?? null,
      handle: row.handle ?? null,
      bio: null,
      links: "[]",
      avatar_ref: null,
      created_at: T0,
      updated_at: T0,
    })
    .execute();
}

/** A media backend holding a valid PNG for every hash (or nothing, with `stored: false`). */
export function pngMedia(stored = true): MediaBackend {
  const head = new Uint8Array([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a]);
  return {
    kind: "s3",
    reserve: vi.fn(),
    serve: vi.fn(),
    inspect: vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve(stored ? { mimeType: "image/png", sizeBytes: 50_000, head } : undefined)),
  } as unknown as MediaBackend;
}

/** A reactor client over the real reducers, in memory. */
export function fakeReactor() {
  const docs = new Map<string, PHDocument>();
  const reducers: Record<string, (doc: PHDocument, action: Action) => PHDocument> = {
    [renownOrganizationDocumentType]: orgReducer as never,
    [renownAppProfileDocumentType]: profileReducer as never,
  };
  const creators: Record<string, () => PHDocument> = {
    [renownOrganizationDocumentType]: () => orgUtils.createDocument() as never,
    [renownAppProfileDocumentType]: () => profileUtils.createDocument() as never,
  };
  return {
    docs,
    createEmpty: vi.fn((documentType: string) => {
      const doc = creators[documentType]();
      docs.set(doc.header.id, doc);
      return Promise.resolve(doc);
    }),
    execute: vi.fn((id: string, _branch: string, actions: Action[]) => {
      let doc = docs.get(id);
      if (!doc) return Promise.reject(new Error(`no document ${id}`));
      for (const action of actions) doc = reducers[doc.header.documentType](doc, action);
      docs.set(id, doc);
      return Promise.resolve(doc);
    }),
    get: vi.fn((id: string) => {
      const doc = docs.get(id);
      return doc ? Promise.resolve(doc) : Promise.reject(new Error(`no document ${id}`));
    }),
    /** The global state of a document. */
    state<T>(id: string): T {
      const doc = docs.get(id);
      if (!doc) throw new Error(`no document ${id}`);
      return (doc.state as unknown as { global: T }).global;
    },
  };
}

type Account = PrivateKeyAccount;
type Resolver = (parent: unknown, args: Record<string, unknown>, ctx: unknown) => Promise<unknown>;

/** Resolvers over the harness, with a settable clock and deterministic ids. */
export function harnessResolvers(h: Harness, overrides: Partial<OrgsResolverDeps> = {}) {
  const reactor = fakeReactor();
  const store = new KyselyOrgStore(h.root.withSchema(ORGS_NAMESPACE));
  const clock = { now: T0 };
  let ids = 0;
  const resolvers = createResolvers({
    reactorClient: reactor as unknown as OrgsResolverDeps["reactorClient"],
    relationalDb: h.relationalDb as OrgsResolverDeps["relationalDb"],
    store: () => store,
    config: () => TEST_CONFIG,
    now: () => clock.now,
    newId: () => `id-${String(++ids).padStart(4, "0")}`,
    media: () => null,
    ...overrides,
  }) as { Query: Record<string, Resolver>; Mutation: Record<string, Resolver> };
  const ctx = (who?: Account) => (who ? { user: { address: who.address } } : {});
  async function auth(field: string, args: Record<string, unknown>, who: Account, timestamp = clock.now.toISOString()): Promise<OrgAuthInput> {
    const message = await orgMessage(field, typeof args.orgId === "string" ? args.orgId : null, args, timestamp);
    return { address: who.address, signature: await who.signMessage({ message }), timestamp };
  }
  return {
    reactor,
    store,
    clock,
    resolvers,
    /** Advances the clock by `ms`. */
    tick(ms: number) {
      clock.now = new Date(clock.now.getTime() + ms);
    },
    query: <T = unknown>(field: string, args: Record<string, unknown> = {}, who?: Account) =>
      resolvers.Query[field](null, args, ctx(who)) as Promise<T>,
    mutate: <T = unknown>(field: string, args: Record<string, unknown>, who?: Account) =>
      resolvers.Mutation[field](null, args, ctx(who)) as Promise<T>,
    auth,
    /** A mutation authorised by `who`'s signature over exactly `args` (no bearer). */
    signed: async <T = unknown>(field: string, args: Record<string, unknown>, who: Account) =>
      resolvers.Mutation[field](null, { ...args, auth: await auth(field, args, who) }, {}) as Promise<T>,
  };
}

export type TestResolvers = ReturnType<typeof harnessResolvers>;

/** createOrganization as `owner`. */
export function createOrg(t: TestResolvers, owner: Account, handle: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Promise<OrganizationOutput> {
  return t.mutate<OrganizationOutput>("createOrganization", { handle, name: handle.toUpperCase(), ...extra }, owner);
}

/** The GraphQL error `promise` rejects with (fails the test if it resolves). */
export async function failure(promise: Promise<unknown>): Promise<{ code: unknown; field: unknown; message: string }> {
  const error = await promise.then(
    () => undefined,
    (e: unknown) => e,
  );
  expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(GraphQLError);
  const e = error as GraphQLError;
  return { code: e.extensions.code, field: e.extensions.field, message: e.message };
}
```

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/tests/organizations.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { RenownOrganizationState } from "../../../document-models/renown-organization/index.js";
import type { OrganizationOutput } from "../output.js";
import {
  ALICE,
  BOB,
  CAROL,
  createOrg,
  failure,
  harnessResolvers,
  insertUser,
  lower,
  openHarness,
  pngMedia,
  resetHarness,
  T0,
  type Harness,
} from "./harness.js";
import { memberRecord, org as orgRecord } from "./store-contract.js";

vi.mock("../../renown-auth/core/smart-wallet.js", () => ({
  verifyMessageOnChain: () => Promise.resolve(false),
  verifyTypedDataOnChain: () => Promise.resolve(false),
}));

const AVATAR = `attachment://v1:${"b".repeat(64)}`;
let h: Harness;

beforeAll(async () => {
  h = await openHarness();
});
afterAll(async () => {
  await h.root.destroy();
});
beforeEach(async () => {
  await resetHarness(h);
});

describe("createOrganization", () => {
  it("creates the org, its document and the creator as the only active owner", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const org = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme", {
      name: "Acme Inc.",
      bio: " Tools ",
      links: [{ id: "l1", label: "Docs", url: "https://docs.acme.example" }],
      listed: true,
    });
    expect(org).toMatchObject({
      handle: "acme",
      name: "Acme Inc.",
      bio: "Tools",
      did: `did:web:renown.test:org:${org.id}`,
      listed: true,
      membersPublic: false,
      createdBy: lower(ALICE),
      ownerCount: 1,
      memberCount: 1,
      securityWarnings: ["SINGLE_OWNER"],
      viewerMembership: { role: "OWNER", status: "ACTIVE", appScope: { all: true, appDids: [] }, teams: [] },
    });
    const state = t.reactor.state<RenownOrganizationState>(org.id);
    expect(state).toMatchObject({ handle: "acme", name: "Acme Inc.", bio: "Tools", listed: true, createdBy: lower(ALICE) });
    expect(state.members).toMatchObject([{ address: lower(ALICE), role: "OWNER", status: "ACTIVE" }]);
    expect((await t.store.auditPage(org.id, 10)).map((e) => [e.action, e.actor])).toEqual([["createOrganization", lower(ALICE)]]);
  });

  it("accepts a signed createOrganization and rejects one signed over other arguments", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const created = await t.signed<OrganizationOutput>("createOrganization", { handle: "acme", name: "Acme" }, BOB);
    expect(created.createdBy).toBe(lower(BOB));
    const auth = await t.auth("createOrganization", { handle: "beta", name: "Beta" }, BOB);
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("createOrganization", { handle: "beta", name: "Beta", bio: "x", auth }))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    // A null the client signed is an argument; omitting it changes the hash.
    const withNull = await t.auth("createOrganization", { handle: "gamma", name: "Gamma", bio: null }, BOB);
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("createOrganization", { handle: "gamma", name: "Gamma", auth: withNull }))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect((await t.mutate<OrganizationOutput>("createOrganization", { handle: "gamma", name: "Gamma", bio: null, auth: withNull })).handle).toBe("gamma");
  });

  it("keeps one namespace with users and refuses invalid, reserved and taken handles", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    await insertUser(h, { address: lower(CAROL), handle: "frank", documentId: "u1" });
    await t.store.reserveHandle({ handle: "oldco", until: new Date(T0.getTime() + 60_000), reason: "ARCHIVED", orgId: "o-old" });
    const attempt = (handle: string) => failure(createOrg(t, BOB, handle));
    expect(await attempt("Acme Inc")).toMatchObject({ code: "BAD_USER_INPUT", field: "handle" });
    expect(await attempt("admin")).toMatchObject({ code: "HANDLE_RESERVED", field: "handle" });
    expect(await attempt("orgs")).toMatchObject({ code: "HANDLE_RESERVED" });
    expect(await attempt(" ACME ")).toMatchObject({ code: "HANDLE_TAKEN", field: "handle" });
    expect(await attempt("Frank")).toMatchObject({ code: "HANDLE_TAKEN" });
    expect(await attempt("oldco")).toMatchObject({ code: "HANDLE_RESERVED" });
    t.tick(60_000);
    expect((await createOrg(t, BOB, "oldco")).handle).toBe("oldco");
  });

  it("validates the profile, naming the field", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const link = (id: string) => ({ id, label: "x", url: "https://x.example" });
    const cases: [Record<string, unknown>, string][] = [
      [{ name: "  " }, "name"],
      [{ bio: "b".repeat(281) }, "bio"],
      [{ website: "ftp://acme.example" }, "website"],
      [{ links: Array.from({ length: 9 }, (_, i) => link(`l${i}`)) }, "links"],
      [{ links: [link("l1"), link("l1")] }, "links"],
      [{ links: [{ id: "l1", label: "x", url: "javascript:alert(1)" }] }, "links"],
    ];
    for (const [extra, field] of cases) {
      expect(await failure(createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme", extra))).toMatchObject({ code: "BAD_USER_INPUT", field });
    }
    expect(await t.store.organizationByHandle("acme")).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("limits organizations per creator and memberships per wallet", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) await t.store.insertOrganization(orgRecord(`o${i}`, `made-${i}`, { createdBy: lower(ALICE) }));
    expect((await failure(createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme"))).code).toBe("LIMIT_REACHED");
    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) await t.store.insertMember(memberRecord(`m${i}`, `x${i}`, lower(BOB)));
    expect((await failure(createOrg(t, BOB, "beta"))).code).toBe("LIMIT_REACHED");
  });

  it("rate limits writes per address", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h, { writeLimiter: { take: () => false } });
    expect((await failure(createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme"))).code).toBe("RATE_LIMITED");
  });
});

describe("updateOrganization and setOrgListed", () => {
  it("patches the profile for admins: absent keeps, empty clears, links replace", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme", { bio: "Tools", website: "https://acme.example" });
    const updated = await t.mutate<OrganizationOutput>(
      "updateOrganization",
      { orgId: id, name: "Acme Labs", bio: "", links: [{ id: "l1", label: "Blog", url: "https://blog.acme.example" }], membersPublic: true },
      ALICE,
    );
    expect(updated).toMatchObject({ name: "Acme Labs", bio: null, website: "https://acme.example", membersPublic: true, links: [{ id: "l1" }] });
    expect(t.reactor.state<RenownOrganizationState>(id)).toMatchObject({ name: "Acme Labs", bio: null, website: "https://acme.example", membersPublic: true });
    expect((await t.store.auditPage(id, 1))[0]).toMatchObject({ action: "updateOrganization", details: { fields: ["name", "bio", "links", "membersPublic"] } });
  });

  it("checks the avatar like a profile avatar", async () => {
    const offline = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(offline, ALICE, "acme");
    expect((await failure(offline.mutate("updateOrganization", { orgId: id, avatar: AVATAR }, ALICE))).code).toBe("SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE");
    const missing = harnessResolvers(h, { media: () => pngMedia(false) });
    expect(await failure(missing.mutate("updateOrganization", { orgId: id, avatar: AVATAR }, ALICE))).toMatchObject({ code: "BAD_USER_INPUT", field: "avatar" });
    const stored = harnessResolvers(h, { media: () => pngMedia() });
    stored.reactor.docs.set(id, offline.reactor.docs.get(id)!);
    expect((await stored.mutate<OrganizationOutput>("updateOrganization", { orgId: id, avatar: AVATAR }, ALICE)).avatar).toBe(AVATAR);
  });

  it("refuses non-admins, unknown organizations and archived ones", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("updateOrganization", { orgId: id, name: "Mine" }, BOB))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("updateOrganization", { orgId: "nope", name: "X" }, ALICE))).code).toBe("NOT_FOUND");
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("setOrgListed", { orgId: id, listed: true }))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    await t.store.updateOrganization(id, { archived: true }, T0);
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("setOrgListed", { orgId: id, listed: true }, ALICE))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
  });
});

describe("organization, organizationSearch, orgHandleAvailability", () => {
  it("finds an organization by id or handle; teams and the viewer's membership only for members", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    expect((await t.query<OrganizationOutput>("organization", { handle: "ACME" }))?.id).toBe(id);
    const anonymous = await t.query<OrganizationOutput>("organization", { id });
    expect(anonymous).toMatchObject({ viewerMembership: null, teams: [] });
    expect((await t.query<OrganizationOutput>("organization", { id }, ALICE))?.viewerMembership?.role).toBe("OWNER");
    expect(await t.query("organization", { id: "nope" })).toBeNull();
    expect((await failure(t.query("organization", {}))).field).toBe("id");
    expect((await failure(t.query("organization", { id, handle: "acme" }))).field).toBe("id");
  });

  it("empties a taken-down organization's profile", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme", { bio: "Tools" });
    await t.store.updateOrganization(id, { takenDown: true }, T0);
    expect(await t.query("organization", { id }, ALICE)).toMatchObject({
      handle: "acme",
      takenDown: true,
      bio: null,
      links: [],
      apps: [],
      memberCount: 0,
      viewerMembership: null,
    });
  });

  it("warns when another organization with a verified domain has the same name", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const real = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme", { name: "Acme" });
    const copy = await createOrg(t, BOB, "acme-official", { name: "ACME" });
    await t.store.db
      .insertInto("org_domains")
      .values({ id: "d1", org_id: real.id, domain: "acme.example", token: "t", status: "VERIFIED", verified_at: T0, last_checked_at: T0, failures: 0, created_at: T0 })
      .execute();
    expect(await t.query("organization", { id: copy.id })).toMatchObject({
      verifiedDomains: [],
      notAffiliatedWith: { id: real.id, handle: "acme", verifiedDomains: ["acme.example"] },
    });
    expect(await t.query("organization", { id: real.id })).toMatchObject({ verifiedDomains: ["acme.example"], notAffiliatedWith: null });
  });

  it("lists listed, live organizations by prefix and pages with a cursor", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const acme = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme", { name: "Acme", listed: true });
    await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acorn", { name: "Oak", listed: true });
    await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acid", { name: "Acid" });
    await t.mutate("setOrgListed", { orgId: acme.id, listed: true }, ALICE);
    const first = await t.query<{ items: OrganizationOutput[]; next: string | null }>("organizationSearch", { query: "AC", limit: 1 });
    expect(first.items.map((o) => o.handle)).toEqual(["acme"]);
    const second = await t.query<{ items: OrganizationOutput[]; next: string | null }>("organizationSearch", { query: "ac", limit: 1, after: first.next });
    expect([second.items.map((o) => o.handle), second.next]).toEqual([["acorn"], null]);
    const all = await t.query<{ items: OrganizationOutput[] }>("organizationSearch", { query: "  " });
    expect(all.items.map((o) => o.handle)).toEqual(["acme", "acorn"]);
  });

  it("validates search arguments", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    expect((await failure(t.query("organizationSearch", { query: "a" }))).field).toBe("query");
    expect((await failure(t.query("organizationSearch", { query: "x".repeat(41) }))).field).toBe("query");
    expect((await failure(t.query("organizationSearch", { limit: 51 }))).field).toBe("limit");
    expect((await failure(t.query("organizationSearch", { after: "garbage" }))).field).toBe("after");
  });

  it("answers handle availability across users, organizations and reserved handles", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    await insertUser(h, { address: lower(BOB), handle: "bob", documentId: "u1" });
    expect(await t.query("orgHandleAvailability", { handle: " Acme " })).toEqual({ handle: "acme", available: false, reason: "TAKEN" });
    expect(await t.query("orgHandleAvailability", { handle: "bob" })).toEqual({ handle: "bob", available: false, reason: "TAKEN" });
    expect(await t.query("orgHandleAvailability", { handle: "invite" })).toEqual({ handle: "invite", available: false, reason: "RESERVED" });
    expect(await t.query("orgHandleAvailability", { handle: "x" })).toEqual({ handle: "x", available: false, reason: "INVALID" });
    expect(await t.query("orgHandleAvailability", { handle: "fresh" })).toEqual({ handle: "fresh", available: true, reason: null });
  });
});

describe("orgAuditLog", () => {
  it("pages the audit log for admins only", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    for (const listed of [true, false, true]) {
      t.tick(1000);
      await t.mutate("setOrgListed", { orgId: id, listed }, ALICE);
    }
    type Page = { items: { action: string; details: string }[]; next: string | null };
    const first = await t.query<Page>("orgAuditLog", { orgId: id, limit: 2 }, ALICE);
    expect(first.items.map((e) => e.action)).toEqual(["setOrgListed", "setOrgListed"]);
    expect(JSON.parse(first.items[0].details)).toEqual({ listed: true });
    const rest = await t.query<Page>("orgAuditLog", { orgId: id, limit: 2, after: first.next }, ALICE);
    expect([rest.items.map((e) => e.action), rest.next]).toEqual([["setOrgListed", "createOrganization"], null]);
    expect((await failure(t.query("orgAuditLog", { orgId: id }, BOB))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect((await failure(t.query("orgAuditLog", { orgId: id }))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect((await failure(t.query("orgAuditLog", { orgId: id, limit: 0 }, ALICE))).field).toBe("limit");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { PGlite } from "@electric-sql/pglite";
import { GraphQLError } from "graphql";
import { Kysely, sql } from "kysely";
import { PGliteDialect } from "kysely-pglite-dialect";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { RenownOrgsSubgraph } from "../index.js";

type Resolver = (parent: unknown, args: unknown, ctx: unknown) => Promise<unknown>;

function makeSubgraph(open: () => Promise<unknown>) {
  const createNamespace = vi.fn(open);
  const subgraph = new RenownOrgsSubgraph({
    http: { owner: "@powerhousedao/renown-package", baseUrl: "https://sb.example", get: vi.fn(), post: vi.fn() },
    reactorClient: {},
    relationalDb: { createNamespace, queryNamespace: () => { throw new Error("no read models here"); } },
  } as never);
  const resolver = (type: string, field: string) =>
    (subgraph.resolvers as Record<string, Record<string, Resolver>>)[type][field];
  return { subgraph, resolver, createNamespace };
}

async function pgliteNamespace(): Promise<unknown> {
  const root = new Kysely<any>({ dialect: new PGliteDialect(new PGlite()) });
  await sql`create schema "renown-orgs"`.execute(root);
  return root.withSchema("renown-orgs");
}

describe("RenownOrgsSubgraph", () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  it("is named renown-orgs and migrates its namespace once", async () => {
    const { subgraph, resolver, createNamespace } = makeSubgraph(pgliteNamespace);
    expect(subgraph.name).toBe("renown-orgs");
    await subgraph.onSetup();
    await subgraph.onSetup();
    expect(createNamespace).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(createNamespace).toHaveBeenCalledWith("renown-orgs");
    expect(await resolver("Query", "organization")(null, { id: "nope" }, {})).toBeNull();
  });

  it("never throws from setup; without its namespace every field is SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE", async () => {
    vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const { subgraph, resolver } = makeSubgraph(() => Promise.reject(new Error("db down")));
    await expect(subgraph.onSetup()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    const error = await resolver("Query", "organization")(null, { id: "x" }, {}).catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(GraphQLError);
    expect((error as GraphQLError).extensions.code).toBe("SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE");
  });
});
```

```ts
  await store.insertOrganization(org("o3", "gone", { membersPublic: true, archived: true }));

  await store.insertOrganization(org("o5", "zeta", { name: "Zeta", membersPublic: true }));
  await store.insertMember(memberRecord("m1", "o1", ALICE, { role: "ADMIN" }));
  await store.insertMember(memberRecord("m2", "o2", ALICE));
  await store.insertMember(memberRecord("m3", "o3", ALICE));
  await store.insertMember(memberRecord("m4", "o4", ALICE));
  await store.insertMember(memberRecord("m5", "o5", ALICE, { hidden: true }));

});
```

```ts
import { PGlite } from "@electric-sql/pglite";
import type { ISubgraph } from "@powerhousedao/reactor-api";
import { print } from "graphql";
import { Kysely, sql } from "kysely";

import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { RenownUserProcessor } from "../../../processors/renown-user/index.js";
import { up } from "../../../processors/renown-user/migrations.js";
import { KyselyOrgStore } from "../../renown-orgs/store/kysely.js";

const USER_NS = RenownUserProcessor.getNamespace("renown-user");
const ALICE = "0xabc0000000000000000000000000000000000001";
let root: Kysely<any>;
type Field = (parent: unknown, args: unknown) => Promise<unknown>;
let resolvers: { Query: Record<string, Field>; ReadRenownUser: Record<string, Field> };

beforeAll(async () => {
  root = new Kysely<any>({ dialect: new PGliteDialect(new PGlite()) });
  await sql`create schema ${sql.id(USER_NS)}`.execute(root);
  await up(root.withSchema(USER_NS) as never);
  await sql`create schema "renown-orgs"`.execute(root);
  await migrate(root.withSchema("renown-orgs"));
  const store = new KyselyOrgStore(root.withSchema("renown-orgs"));
  await store.insertOrganization(org("o1", "acme", { name: "Acme", membersPublic: true }));
  await store.insertMember(memberRecord("m1", "o1", ALICE, { role: "PUBLISHER" }));
  await store.reserveHandle({ handle: "oldco", until: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000), reason: "ARCHIVED", orgId: null });
  const subgraph = { relationalDb: { queryNamespace: (ns: string) => root.withSchema(ns) } } as unknown as ISubgraph;
  resolvers = getResolvers(subgraph) as typeof resolvers;
});

afterAll(async () => {
  await root.destroy();
});

describe("renown read model and organizations", () => {
  it("reports organization handles as TAKEN and reserved handles as RESERVED", async () => {
    expect(await resolvers.Query.renownHandleAvailability(null, { handle: "ACME", address: ALICE })).toEqual({
      handle: "acme",
      available: false,
      reason: "TAKEN",
    });
    expect(await resolvers.Query.renownHandleAvailability(null, { handle: "oldco" })).toEqual({
      handle: "oldco",
      available: false,
      reason: "RESERVED",
    });
    expect(await resolvers.Query.renownHandleAvailability(null, { handle: "fresh" })).toMatchObject({ available: true });
  });

  it("resolves memberOf to the user's public memberships", async () => {
    expect(await resolvers.ReadRenownUser.memberOf({ ethAddress: ALICE }, {})).toEqual([
      { orgId: "o1", handle: "acme", name: "Acme", role: "PUBLISHER" },
    ]);
    expect(await resolvers.ReadRenownUser.memberOf({ ethAddress: null }, {})).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("declares memberOf on ReadRenownUser", () => {
    const sdl = print(schema);
    expect(sdl).toMatch(/type ReadRenownUser \{[^}]*memberOf: \[ReadRenownOrgMembership!\]!/);
    expect(sdl).toMatch(/type ReadRenownOrgMembership \{\s+orgId: String!\s+handle: String!\s+name: String!\s+role: String!\s+\}/);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the lookups**

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/store/lookups.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from "kysely";
import type { ReadModelDb } from "../../renown-auth/lookups.js";
import { ORGS_NAMESPACE, type OrgRole, type OrgsDB } from "./types.js";

/**
 * Readers of the renown-orgs namespace for other subgraphs (renown-auth,
 * renown-read-model, renown-oidc, media). They import nothing of the
 * renown-orgs runtime, so there is no import cycle.
 */

/** Postgres "undefined table" or "invalid schema name": the renown-orgs namespace was never set up. */
export function isMissingNamespace(error: unknown): boolean {
  const code = (error as { code?: unknown } | null)?.code;
  return code === "42P01" || code === "3F000";
}

function orgs(db: ReadModelDb) {
  return db.queryNamespace<OrgsDB>(ORGS_NAMESPACE);
}

/**
 * TAKEN when an organization holds `handle` (case-insensitively), RESERVED
 * while it is in reserved_handles, else null. A missing namespace reads as
 * null; any other error is thrown.
 */
export async function orgHandleStatus(db: ReadModelDb, handle: string, now: Date): Promise<"TAKEN" | "RESERVED" | null> {
  const wanted = handle.toLowerCase();
  try {
    const holder = await orgs(db)
      .selectFrom("organizations")
      .select("id")
      .where(sql<string>`lower(handle)`, "=", wanted)
      .executeTakeFirst();
    if (holder) return "TAKEN";
    const reserved = await orgs(db)
      .selectFrom("reserved_handles")
      .select("handle")
      .where("handle", "=", wanted)
      .where("until", ">", now)
      .executeTakeFirst();
    return reserved ? "RESERVED" : null;
  } catch (error) {
    if (isMissingNamespace(error)) return null;
    throw error;
  }
}

export interface PublicMembership {
  orgId: string;
  handle: string;
  name: string;
  role: OrgRole;
}

/** ACTIVE, non-hidden memberships of `address` in membersPublic organizations that are neither archived nor taken down. */
export async function publicMembershipsOf(db: ReadModelDb, address: string): Promise<PublicMembership[]> {
  try {
    const rows = await orgs(db)
      .selectFrom("org_members")
      .innerJoin("organizations", "organizations.id", "org_members.org_id")
      .select(["organizations.id as org_id", "organizations.handle", "organizations.name", "org_members.role"])
      .where("org_members.address", "=", address.toLowerCase())
      .where("org_members.status", "=", "ACTIVE")
      .where("org_members.hidden", "=", false)
      .where("organizations.members_public", "=", true)
      .where("organizations.archived", "=", false)
      .where("organizations.taken_down", "=", false)
      .orderBy("organizations.handle")
      .execute();
    return rows.map((row) => ({ orgId: row.org_id, handle: row.handle, name: row.name, role: row.role }));
  } catch (error) {
    if (isMissingNamespace(error)) return [];
    throw error;
  }
}

/** The avatar ref of a live (not taken down) organization, or null. Never throws. */
export async function orgAvatarRef(db: ReadModelDb, orgId: string): Promise<string | null> {
  try {
    const row = await orgs(db)
      .selectFrom("organizations")
      .select("avatar_ref")
      .where("id", "=", orgId)
      .where("taken_down", "=", false)
      .executeTakeFirst();
    return row?.avatar_ref ?? null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
```

In `subgraphs/renown-auth/resolvers.ts` add the import

```ts
import { orgHandleStatus } from "../renown-orgs/store/lookups.js";
```

and replace `assertHandleFree` inside `createResolvers` with:

```ts
  /** HANDLE_TAKEN unless `handle` is free or already this profile's; users and organizations share one namespace. */
  async function assertHandleFree(handle: string, documentId: string | undefined): Promise<void> {
    const owner = await findHandleOwner(relationalDb, handle);
    if (owner !== undefined && owner !== documentId) {
      throw fieldError("HANDLE_TAKEN", "handle", `The handle "${handle}" is taken`);
    }
    let orgStatus: "TAKEN" | "RESERVED" | null;
    try {
      orgStatus = await orgHandleStatus(relationalDb, handle, now());
    } catch {
      throw new GraphQLError("Handles cannot be checked right now", { extensions: { code: "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE" } });
    }
    if (orgStatus === "TAKEN") throw fieldError("HANDLE_TAKEN", "handle", `The handle "${handle}" belongs to an organization`);
    if (orgStatus === "RESERVED") throw fieldError("HANDLE_TAKEN", "handle", `The handle "${handle}" is reserved`);
  }
```

### Task 7: Membership lifecycle — invites, invite links, leave/remove, roles, app scopes, hidden, visibility

**Files:**
- Create: `subgraphs/renown-orgs/areas/members.ts`
- Modify: `subgraphs/renown-orgs/resolvers.ts` (register the area), `subgraphs/renown-orgs/tests/harness.ts` (append `join`)
- Test: `subgraphs/renown-orgs/tests/members.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5 runtime, outputs, authz, `openMemberships`, `MAX_MEMBERSHIPS_PER_WALLET`; `sha256Hex` from `sdk/orgs.ts`; `addressOfHandle`.
- Produces: `memberResolvers: Area` (all A1 membership queries and mutations), `MAX_PENDING_INVITES = 200`, `MAX_LINK_USES = 100`, `MAX_LINK_DAYS = 30`, `endMembership(rt, member, status, actor, now): Promise<MemberRecord>` (document + store + teams + `membershipEnded` hooks for LEFT/REMOVED; A2 reuses it), `assertManages(actor, roles)`, `addressArg(field, value)`; harness `join(t, orgId, who, role, by?): Promise<string>` (returns the member id).
- Activity emitted: `inviteOrgMember` → `member.invited` + INVITED to the invitee; accepting (invite or link) → `member.joined` + INVITE_ACCEPTED to ADMINS; `leaveOrganization` → `member.left`; `removeOrgMember` → `member.removed` + REMOVED to the member; `setOrgMemberRole` → `member.role_changed` + ROLE_CHANGED to the member; `setOrgMemberAppScope` → `member.role_changed` (data carries `appScope`). Every other mutation writes its audit row only.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add `join` to the harness**

Append to `subgraphs/renown-orgs/tests/harness.ts`:

```ts
/** `who` joins `orgId` with `role`: invited by `by`, then accepted. Returns the member id. */
export async function join(t: TestResolvers, orgId: string, who: Account, role: string, by: Account = ALICE): Promise<string> {
  const invite = await t.mutate<{ id: string }>("inviteOrgMember", { orgId, invitee: who.address, role }, by);
  await t.mutate("respondToOrgInvite", { orgId, inviteId: invite.id, accept: true }, who);
  return invite.id;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests**

Create `subgraphs/renown-orgs/tests/members.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { actions as orgActions, type RenownOrganizationState } from "../../../document-models/renown-organization/index.js";
import type { RecordedActivity } from "../core/activity.js";
import type { OrgInviteLinkOutput, OrgInviteOutput, OrgMemberOutput, OrgMembershipOutput, OrganizationOutput } from "../output.js";
import type { MembershipEnded } from "../runtime.js";
import {
  ALICE,
  BOB,
  CAROL,
  DAVE,
  EVE,
  createOrg,
  failure,
  harnessResolvers,
  insertUser,
  join,
  lower,
  openHarness,
  resetHarness,
  T0,
  type Harness,
  type TestResolvers,
} from "./harness.js";
import { memberRecord } from "./store-contract.js";

vi.mock("../../renown-auth/core/smart-wallet.js", () => ({
  verifyMessageOnChain: () => Promise.resolve(false),
  verifyTypedDataOnChain: () => Promise.resolve(false),
}));

const DAY = 86_400_000;
const APP = "did:key:z6MkhaXgBZDvotDkL5257faiztiGiC2QtKLGpbnnEGta2doK";
let h: Harness;

beforeAll(async () => {
  h = await openHarness();
});
afterAll(async () => {
  await h.root.destroy();
});
beforeEach(async () => {
  await resetHarness(h);
});

function recording(extra: Parameters<typeof harnessResolvers>[1] = {}) {
  const events: RecordedActivity[] = [];
  const t = harnessResolvers(h, { sinks: [(activity) => Promise.resolve(void events.push(activity))], ...extra });
  return { t, events };
}

async function members(t: TestResolvers, orgId: string, who = ALICE, includeInactive = false) {
  return t.query<OrgMemberOutput[]>("organizationMembers", { orgId, includeInactive }, who);
}

/** Makes an existing ACTIVE member an OWNER (A1 has no mutation for it; A2's ADD_OWNER does this). */
async function promoteToOwner(t: TestResolvers, orgId: string, memberId: string) {
  await t.reactor.execute(orgId, "main", [orgActions.setMemberRole({ id: memberId, role: "OWNER", at: T0.toISOString() })]);
  await t.store.updateMember(memberId, { role: "OWNER", appScopeAll: true, updatedAt: T0 });
}

describe("direct invites", () => {
  it("invites by handle; the invitee sees it, accepts and becomes an active member", async () => {
    const { t, events } = recording();
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    await insertUser(h, { address: lower(BOB), handle: "bob", documentId: "u-bob", displayName: "Bob" });
    const invite = await t.mutate<OrgInviteOutput>("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: " Bob ", role: "PUBLISHER" }, ALICE);
    expect(invite).toMatchObject({ orgId: id, address: lower(BOB), role: "PUBLISHER", status: "INVITED", invitedBy: lower(ALICE), org: { handle: "acme" } });
    expect(invite.expiresAt).toBe(new Date(T0.getTime() + 14 * DAY).toISOString());
    expect((await t.query<OrgInviteOutput[]>("myOrgInvites", {}, BOB)).map((i) => i.id)).toEqual([invite.id]);
    expect((await t.query<OrgInviteOutput[]>("orgInvites", { orgId: id }, ALICE)).map((i) => i.id)).toEqual([invite.id]);
    const joined = await t.mutate<OrgMemberOutput>("respondToOrgInvite", { orgId: id, inviteId: invite.id, accept: true }, BOB);
    expect(joined).toMatchObject({ address: lower(BOB), status: "ACTIVE", role: "PUBLISHER", handle: "bob", displayName: "Bob", expiresAt: null });
    expect((await members(t, id)).map((m) => [m.address, m.role])).toEqual([[lower(ALICE), "OWNER"], [lower(BOB), "PUBLISHER"]]);
    expect(t.reactor.state<RenownOrganizationState>(id).members.map((m) => [m.address, m.status])).toEqual([
      [lower(ALICE), "ACTIVE"],
      [lower(BOB), "ACTIVE"],
    ]);
    expect(events.map((e) => [e.action, e.event?.type, e.notify?.map((n) => [n.to, n.kind])])).toEqual([
      ["createOrganization", undefined, undefined],
      ["inviteOrgMember", "member.invited", [[[lower(BOB)], "INVITED"]]],
      ["respondToOrgInvite", "member.joined", [["ADMINS", "INVITE_ACCEPTED"]]],
    ]);
  });

  it("lets the invitee decline and an admin revoke; only the invitee answers", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    const first = await t.mutate<OrgInviteOutput>("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: BOB.address, role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE);
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("respondToOrgInvite", { orgId: id, inviteId: first.id, accept: true }, CAROL))).code).toBe("NOT_FOUND");
    expect((await t.mutate<OrgMemberOutput>("respondToOrgInvite", { orgId: id, inviteId: first.id, accept: false }, BOB)).status).toBe("DECLINED");
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("respondToOrgInvite", { orgId: id, inviteId: first.id, accept: true }, BOB))).field).toBe("inviteId");
    const second = await t.mutate<OrgInviteOutput>("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: BOB.address, role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE);
    expect((await t.mutate<OrgInviteOutput>("revokeOrgInvite", { orgId: id, inviteId: second.id }, ALICE)).status).toBe("REVOKED");
    expect(await t.query("myOrgInvites", {}, BOB)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("enforces who invites whom: admins up to publisher, owners admins, nobody owners", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    await join(t, id, BOB, "ADMIN");
    await join(t, id, CAROL, "MEMBER");
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: DAVE.address, role: "MEMBER" }, CAROL))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: DAVE.address, role: "ADMIN" }, BOB))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect(await failure(t.mutate("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: DAVE.address, role: "OWNER" }, ALICE))).toMatchObject({ code: "BAD_USER_INPUT", field: "role" });
    const adminInvite = await t.mutate<OrgInviteOutput>("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: DAVE.address, role: "ADMIN" }, ALICE);
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("revokeOrgInvite", { orgId: id, inviteId: adminInvite.id }, BOB))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect((await t.mutate<OrgInviteOutput>("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: EVE.address, role: "PUBLISHER" }, BOB)).role).toBe("PUBLISHER");
    expect(await failure(t.mutate("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: CAROL.address, role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE))).toMatchObject({ field: "invitee" });
    expect(await failure(t.mutate("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: EVE.address, role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE))).toMatchObject({ field: "invitee" });
    expect(await failure(t.mutate("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: "nobody", role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE))).toMatchObject({ field: "invitee" });
    expect(await failure(t.mutate("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: "0x123", role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE))).toMatchObject({ field: "invitee" });
  });

  it("re-invites over an expired invite, and expired invites count toward no limit", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    const old = await t.mutate<OrgInviteOutput>("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: BOB.address, role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE);
    t.tick(15 * DAY);
    expect((await t.query<OrgInviteOutput[]>("orgInvites", { orgId: id }, ALICE)).map((i) => i.status)).toEqual(["EXPIRED"]);
    expect(await failure(t.mutate("respondToOrgInvite", { orgId: id, inviteId: old.id, accept: true }, BOB))).toMatchObject({ field: "inviteId" });
    const fresh = await t.mutate<OrgInviteOutput>("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: BOB.address, role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE);
    expect((await t.store.member(old.id))?.status).toBe("REVOKED");
    expect(t.reactor.state<RenownOrganizationState>(id).members.map((m) => [m.id, m.status])).toEqual([
      [expect.any(String), "ACTIVE"],
      [old.id, "REVOKED"],
      [fresh.id, "INVITED"],
    ]);
    const stale = new Date(t.clock.now.getTime() - 15 * DAY);
    for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
      await t.store.insertMember(memberRecord(`x${i}`, id, `0x${(i + 1).toString(16).padStart(40, "0")}`, { status: "INVITED", invitedAt: stale, joinedAt: null }));
    }
    await t.mutate("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: CAROL.address, role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE);
    for (let i = 0; i < 198; i++) {
      await t.store.insertMember(memberRecord(`y${i}`, id, `0x${(i + 1000).toString(16).padStart(40, "0")}`, { status: "INVITED", invitedAt: t.clock.now, joinedAt: null }));
    }
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: DAVE.address, role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE))).code).toBe("LIMIT_REACHED");
  });

  it("rate limits invites per organization", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h, { inviteLimiter: { take: () => false } });
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: BOB.address, role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE))).code).toBe("RATE_LIMITED");
  });
});

describe("invite links", () => {
  async function link(t: TestResolvers, orgId: string, over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
    return t.mutate<{ link: OrgInviteLinkOutput; token: string }>(
      "createOrgInviteLink",
      { orgId, role: "PUBLISHER", maxUses: 2, expiresAt: new Date(t.clock.now.getTime() + DAY).toISOString(), ...over },
      ALICE,
    );
  }

  it("shows the token once, stores only its hash, previews and accepts it up to maxUses", async () => {
    const { t, events } = recording();
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    const { link: created, token } = await link(t, id);
    expect(token).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/);
    expect(JSON.stringify(await t.store.inviteLinks(id))).not.toContain(token);
    expect(JSON.stringify(await t.store.auditPage(id, 10))).not.toContain(token);
    expect(await t.query("orgInviteLink", { token })).toMatchObject({ org: { handle: "acme" }, role: "PUBLISHER", expired: false, revoked: false, full: false, usable: true });
    expect(await t.query("orgInviteLink", { token: "nope" })).toBeNull();
    expect((await t.mutate<OrgMemberOutput>("acceptOrgInviteLink", { token }, CAROL))).toMatchObject({ address: lower(CAROL), role: "PUBLISHER", status: "ACTIVE" });
    await t.mutate("acceptOrgInviteLink", { token }, DAVE);
    expect(await failure(t.mutate("acceptOrgInviteLink", { token }, EVE))).toMatchObject({ code: "BAD_USER_INPUT", field: "token" });
    expect(await t.query("orgInviteLink", { token })).toMatchObject({ full: true, usable: false });
    expect((await t.query<OrgInviteLinkOutput[]>("orgInviteLinks", { orgId: id }, ALICE))[0]).toMatchObject({ id: created.id, uses: 2 });
    expect(events.filter((e) => e.event?.type === "member.joined")).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("lets exactly one of two racing acceptors take the last use", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    const { token } = await link(t, id, { maxUses: 1 });
    const results = await Promise.allSettled([
      t.mutate("acceptOrgInviteLink", { token }, CAROL),
      t.mutate("acceptOrgInviteLink", { token }, DAVE),
    ]);
    expect(results.filter((r) => r.status === "fulfilled")).toHaveLength(1);
    expect((await members(t, id)).length).toBe(2);
  });

  it("refuses revoked, expired and foreign tokens, members and pending invitees", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    const revoked = await link(t, id);
    expect((await t.mutate<OrgInviteLinkOutput>("revokeOrgInviteLink", { orgId: id, linkId: revoked.link.id }, ALICE)).revoked).toBe(true);
    expect(await failure(t.mutate("acceptOrgInviteLink", { token: revoked.token }, CAROL))).toMatchObject({ field: "token" });
    const live = await link(t, id);
    expect(await failure(t.mutate("acceptOrgInviteLink", { token: live.token }, ALICE))).toMatchObject({ field: "token" });
    await t.mutate("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: BOB.address, role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE);
    expect(await failure(t.mutate("acceptOrgInviteLink", { token: live.token }, BOB))).toMatchObject({ field: "token" });
    expect(await failure(t.mutate("acceptOrgInviteLink", { token: "made-up" }, CAROL))).toMatchObject({ field: "token" });
    t.tick(DAY);
    expect(await failure(t.mutate("acceptOrgInviteLink", { token: live.token }, CAROL))).toMatchObject({ field: "token" });
    expect(await t.query("orgInviteLink", { token: live.token })).toMatchObject({ expired: true, usable: false });
  });

  it("validates role, maxUses and expiresAt, for admins only", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    const later = (ms: number) => new Date(t.clock.now.getTime() + ms).toISOString();
    expect(await failure(link(t, id, { role: "ADMIN" }))).toMatchObject({ field: "role" });
    expect(await failure(link(t, id, { maxUses: 0 }))).toMatchObject({ field: "maxUses" });
    expect(await failure(link(t, id, { maxUses: 101 }))).toMatchObject({ field: "maxUses" });
    expect(await failure(link(t, id, { expiresAt: later(31 * DAY) }))).toMatchObject({ field: "expiresAt" });
    expect(await failure(link(t, id, { expiresAt: later(-1000) }))).toMatchObject({ field: "expiresAt" });
    expect(await failure(link(t, id, { expiresAt: "tomorrow" }))).toMatchObject({ field: "expiresAt" });
    expect((await link(t, id, { maxUses: 100, expiresAt: later(30 * DAY) })).link.maxUses).toBe(100);
    const notAdmin = t.mutate("createOrgInviteLink", { orgId: id, role: "MEMBER", maxUses: 1, expiresAt: later(DAY) }, BOB);
    expect((await failure(notAdmin)).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
  });
});

describe("leaving and removal", () => {
  it("lets members leave, ends their teams and runs the membership-ended hooks; the last owner cannot leave", async () => {
    const ended: MembershipEnded[] = [];
    const t = harnessResolvers(h, { membershipHooks: [(event) => Promise.resolve(void ended.push(event))] });
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    const bob = await join(t, id, BOB, "PUBLISHER");
    await t.store.insertTeam({ id: "t1", orgId: id, slug: "core", name: "Core", appDids: [], memberIds: [bob], createdAt: T0 });
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("leaveOrganization", { orgId: id }, ALICE))).code).toBe("LAST_OWNER");
    expect(await t.mutate("leaveOrganization", { orgId: id }, BOB)).toBe(true);
    expect(await t.store.member(bob)).toMatchObject({ status: "LEFT", endedAt: T0 });
    expect((await t.store.team("t1"))?.memberIds).toEqual([]);
    expect(ended).toEqual([{ orgId: id, memberId: bob, address: lower(BOB), reason: "LEFT", actor: lower(BOB), at: T0 }]);
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("leaveOrganization", { orgId: id }, BOB))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
  });

  it("serialises concurrent leaves under the org lock, so one owner always remains", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    await promoteToOwner(t, id, await join(t, id, BOB, "ADMIN"));
    const results = await Promise.allSettled([
      t.mutate("leaveOrganization", { orgId: id }, ALICE),
      t.mutate("leaveOrganization", { orgId: id }, BOB),
    ]);
    expect(results.map((r) => r.status).sort()).toEqual(["fulfilled", "rejected"]);
    expect((await t.store.members(id, ["ACTIVE"])).filter((m) => m.role === "OWNER")).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("lets admins remove up to publisher and owners remove admins, never owners", async () => {
    const { t, events } = recording();
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    await join(t, id, BOB, "ADMIN");
    await join(t, id, CAROL, "PUBLISHER");
    await join(t, id, DAVE, "ADMIN");
    expect((await t.mutate<OrgMemberOutput>("removeOrgMember", { orgId: id, address: CAROL.address }, BOB)).status).toBe("REMOVED");
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("removeOrgMember", { orgId: id, address: DAVE.address }, BOB))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("removeOrgMember", { orgId: id, address: ALICE.address }, BOB))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("removeOrgMember", { orgId: id, address: BOB.address }, BOB))).field).toBe("address");
    expect((await failure(t.mutate("removeOrgMember", { orgId: id, address: EVE.address }, ALICE))).code).toBe("NOT_FOUND");
    expect((await t.mutate<OrgMemberOutput>("removeOrgMember", { orgId: id, address: DAVE.address }, ALICE)).status).toBe("REMOVED");
    expect(events.filter((e) => e.action === "removeOrgMember").map((e) => [e.event?.type, e.notify?.[0]?.to])).toEqual([
      ["member.removed", [lower(CAROL)]],
      ["member.removed", [lower(DAVE)]],
    ]);
  });
});

describe("roles, app scopes and hiding", () => {
  it("lets admins change roles up to publisher and owners manage admins", async () => {
    const { t, events } = recording();
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    await join(t, id, BOB, "MEMBER");
    await join(t, id, CAROL, "ADMIN");
    const role = (who: typeof BOB, value: string, by: typeof BOB) =>
      t.mutate<OrgMemberOutput>("setOrgMemberRole", { orgId: id, address: who.address, role: value }, by);
    expect((await role(BOB, "PUBLISHER", CAROL)).role).toBe("PUBLISHER");
    expect((await failure(role(BOB, "ADMIN", CAROL))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect((await failure(role(CAROL, "MEMBER", CAROL))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect((await failure(role(ALICE, "ADMIN", ALICE))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
    expect(await failure(role(BOB, "OWNER", ALICE))).toMatchObject({ code: "BAD_USER_INPUT", field: "role" });
    expect((await role(BOB, "ADMIN", ALICE)).appScope).toEqual({ all: true, appDids: [] });
    expect((await role(BOB, "ADMIN", ALICE)).role).toBe("ADMIN");
    expect(events.filter((e) => e.action === "setOrgMemberRole").map((e) => e.event?.data)).toEqual([
      { address: lower(BOB), from: "MEMBER", to: "PUBLISHER" },
      { address: lower(BOB), from: "PUBLISHER", to: "ADMIN" },
    ]);
  });

  it("sets the app scope of publishers and members; owners and admins always have all", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    await join(t, id, BOB, "PUBLISHER");
    await join(t, id, CAROL, "ADMIN");
    const scope = (who: typeof BOB, appScope: unknown) => t.mutate<OrgMemberOutput>("setOrgMemberAppScope", { orgId: id, address: who.address, appScope }, ALICE);
    expect((await scope(BOB, { all: false, appDids: [APP, APP] })).appScope).toEqual({ all: false, appDids: [APP] });
    expect(await t.query<OrgMembershipOutput>("orgMembership", { orgId: id, address: BOB.address })).toMatchObject({
      role: "PUBLISHER",
      appScope: { all: false, appDids: [APP] },
      effectiveScope: { all: false, appDids: [APP] },
    });
    expect(await failure(scope(BOB, { all: false, appDids: ["did:web:nope"] }))).toMatchObject({ field: "appScope" });
    expect(await failure(scope(CAROL, { all: false, appDids: [] }))).toMatchObject({ field: "address" });
  });

  it("lets a member hide their membership from public views", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme", { membersPublic: true });
    await join(t, id, BOB, "MEMBER");
    expect((await t.query<OrgMemberOutput[]>("organizationMembers", { orgId: id })).map((m) => m.address)).toEqual([lower(ALICE), lower(BOB)]);
    expect((await t.mutate<OrgMemberOutput>("setMyOrgMembershipHidden", { orgId: id, hidden: true }, BOB)).hidden).toBe(true);
    expect((await t.query<OrgMemberOutput[]>("organizationMembers", { orgId: id })).map((m) => m.address)).toEqual([lower(ALICE)]);
    expect((await members(t, id, ALICE)).map((m) => m.address)).toEqual([lower(ALICE), lower(BOB)]);
    expect(await t.query("organizationsByMember", { address: BOB.address })).toEqual([]);
    expect((await t.query<{ org: OrganizationOutput; hidden: boolean }[]>("organizationsByMember", { address: BOB.address }, BOB)).map((a) => [a.org.handle, a.hidden])).toEqual([["acme", true]]);
    expect(await t.query("orgMembership", { orgId: id, address: BOB.address })).toMatchObject({ role: "MEMBER", status: "ACTIVE" });
  });
});

describe("visibility", () => {
  it("shows private member lists to members only and inactive records to admins only", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme");
    await join(t, id, BOB, "MEMBER");
    await t.mutate("inviteOrgMember", { orgId: id, invitee: CAROL.address, role: "MEMBER" }, ALICE);
    expect(await t.query("organizationMembers", { orgId: id })).toEqual([]);
    expect(await t.query("organizationMembers", { orgId: id }, DAVE)).toEqual([]);
    expect((await members(t, id, BOB, true)).map((m) => m.status)).toEqual(["ACTIVE", "ACTIVE"]);
    expect((await members(t, id, ALICE, true)).map((m) => m.status)).toEqual(["ACTIVE", "ACTIVE", "INVITED"]);
    expect(await t.query("organizationsByMember", { address: BOB.address })).toEqual([]);
    expect(await t.query("orgMembership", { orgId: id, address: CAROL.address })).toBeNull();
    expect(await t.query("orgMembership", { orgId: "nope", address: BOB.address })).toBeNull();
    expect((await failure(t.query("orgInvites", { orgId: id }, BOB))).code).toBe("FORBIDDEN");
  });

  it("drops archived organizations from memberships", async () => {
    const t = harnessResolvers(h);
    const { id } = await createOrg(t, ALICE, "acme", { membersPublic: true });
    expect((await t.query<unknown[]>("organizationsByMember", { address: ALICE.address })).length).toBe(1);
    await t.store.updateOrganization(id, { archived: true }, T0);
    expect(await t.query("organizationsByMember", { address: ALICE.address }, ALICE)).toEqual([]);
    expect(await t.query("orgMembership", { orgId: id, address: ALICE.address })).toBeNull();
  });
});
```
